## 连续语言模型与语言流 [[Back]](#toc)

连续 embedding/latent 空间中的语言建模，包括 ELF、CoLa、扩散语言模型与 flow matching language model。

### [1] [Journey Operators for Structured Multi-Axis Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26775)
*Mahesh Godavarti*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.AI

Direction: 连续语言模型与连续多模态模型

Subtopic: 连续语言模型与语言流

Importance: 值得关注 (74.0) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 Journey Operators 框架，将位置编码（如 RoPE）统一为多轴结构数据上的路径变换算子，证明了在特定对称性假设下块状旋转是必然形式，并基于此设计了 JoFormer 模型，在视觉、语言和长度泛化任务上验证了其效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有位置编码方法（如 RoPE）虽然有效，但缺乏统一的数学框架来解释其为何有效、何时有效，以及如何扩展到更复杂的多轴结构数据（如图像、3D 体积等）。作者希望建立一个通用的理论框架，统一理解位置编码的本质，并指导设计新的模型架构。

Method: 提出 Journey Operators 框架：每个数据项携带内容及每个轴上的小变换，路径连接两个位置定义"旅程"，旅程算子为沿路径的逐轴变换乘积，控制数据沿路径的组成方式和相对位置的描述方式。当变换固定时，恢复 RoPE 及其多维变体；当变换依赖数据时，获得内容自适应的位置归纳偏置。证明了当轴变换可交换时，路径无关性成立；在环面框架对称性、上循环、双线性、范数保持等假设下，成对评分规则必须为块状旋转形式。基于该理论设计了 JoFormer 模型用于值聚合，并与注意力机制和状态空间模型建立联系。

Result: 在视觉、语言和长度泛化任务上的初步实验表明，该框架提供的归纳偏置在实践中具有可观察的效果，验证了理论分析的正确性。

Conclusion: Journey Operators 为理解位置编码提供了统一的数学框架，揭示了 RoPE 类方法的本质，并为设计具有内容自适应位置偏置的新型模型（如 JoFormer）提供了理论基础。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Journey Operators 框架，将 RoPE 及其多维变体统一为一种基于轴变换的连续位置编码理论，并设计了 JoFormer 模型。其核心涉及连续潜空间中的位置变换、轴间交换性、以及内容自适应的位置归纳偏置，与连续语言模型/语言流（continuous_language_flow）中关于连续 embedding/latent 空间建模的主题高度相关。

Importance reason: Directly advances continuous language/multimodal models by unifying RoPE and multi-axis positional encoding with theoretical grounding. Signals: continuous language model, theoretical unification of RoPE, multi-axis composition, content-adaptive positional bias, JoFormer design

Abstract: Many kinds of data have structure along one or more axes: words in a sentence, pixels in an image, nodes in a tree, frames in audio, or cells in a 3D volume. Along one axis, order matters: "the dog bit the man" is different from "the man bit the dog." Across independent axes, however, neither composition nor movement should depend on the order of axes: in an image, composing right then down should give the same result as composing down then right, and moving right then down should describe the same relative position as moving down then right. We develop a framework for modeling this kind of multi-axis structure. Each data item carries its content together with a small transformation for each axis. A path connecting two positions defines a journey; the journey operator is the product of per-axis transformations along that path, governing both how data composes along the path and how relative position is described. When the transformations are fixed, our framework recovers Rotary Position Embedding (RoPE) and its multi-dimensional variants. When they depend on the data, the model gains a content-adaptive positional inductive bias. We show exactly when these paths are well-defined: both composition and movement across axes are path-independent precisely when the axis transformations commute. We also prove that, under the stated toral-frame symmetry, cocycle, bilinearity, and norm-preservation assumptions, the resulting pairwise scoring rule must take the form of block-wise rotations, explaining why RoPE-like methods arise naturally. Finally, we use this theory to design JoFormer, a model for value aggregation, and relate it to attention and state-space models (SSMs). Initial experiments across vision, language, and length generalization suggest that these inductive biases can have observable consequences in practice.

</details>

### [2] [Embodied Agents Take Control: Minimal-Interface Zero-Shot Agents Rival Industrial-Scale Policies in Vision-and-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26148)
*Jian Zhou, Xunyi Zhao, Gengze Zhou, Zerui Li, Sihao Lin, Jiajun Liu, Qi Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 高优先级 (81.01) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文研究具身智能体的"智能体控制"范式——即通用大语言模型自主维持感知-行动-验证-纠错的完整决策循环。在零样本视觉语言导航任务中，仅使用单目RGB相机和离散动作，默认配置下opus-5模型达到70.7%成功率，fable-5在最大努力配置下达到78%。混合使用预训练航点工具后，fable-5以更少的步数和时间达到76.7%成功率。实验表明模型选择是决定性因素，智能体控制范式在零样本导航中已具备竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前具身智能体系统通常依赖任务特定的工作流或具身策略来维持长程决策循环。本文探索第三种范式——"智能体控制"（agentic control），即让通用智能体自主维持整个决策循环，研究其在零样本视觉语言导航中的可行性。

Method: 使用三种软件工程智能体框架（agent harnesses），仅给智能体单目RGB相机和离散动作作为最小接口。在零样本VLN任务中，评估不同模型（opus-5, fable-5等）在不同配置下的表现。进一步引入预训练航点工具作为可选能力，形成混合智能体方案。

Result: 默认配置下opus-5达到70.7±3.5%成功率，fable-5最大努力配置下达到78%。混合航点工具的fable-5在默认努力下达到76.7±0.6%，使用一半的环境步数和不到四分之一的运行时间。模型选择对成功率影响最大，强制航点接口对弱模型有帮助但可能阻碍强模型。在长程任务上性能显著下降，延迟和上下文增长限制持续运行。

Conclusion: 智能体控制范式在零样本导航中已具备与工业级策略相竞争的能力。模型、智能体框架和接口是互补的优化路径，共同推动自主具身智能体的发展。

Classification reason: 论文研究具身智能体在视觉语言导航（VLN）中的零样本控制策略，属于具身智能体在环境中的感知、规划与决策，与[具身世界模型与规划控制]方向高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strong empirical results for zero-shot embodied navigation but outside user's core priority subtopics. Signals: zero-shot navigation, agentic control, LLM-driven decision loop, model-centric capability, minimal interface

Abstract: Autonomous embodied agents must sustain a long decision-making loop that involves perceiving, acting, verifying, and self-correcting over many steps. Current systems sustain this loop through task-specific workflows or embodied policies. We study a third form, agentic embodied control, in which a general-purpose agent holds the loop itself. Using zero-shot navigation as a controlled testbed, we evaluate three software-engineering agent harnesses given only a monocular RGB camera and discrete actions. Under this strictly minimal condition, replicated default-effort configurations reach 70.7$\pm$3.5% success (opus-5, mean over three runs), and fable-5 reaches 78% at maximum effort. When a trained waypoint tool is exposed alongside primitives as an optional capability, the hybrid fable-5 agent reaches 76.7$\pm$0.6% at default effort, using half the environment steps and less than one quarter of the wall time of the maximum-effort primitive run. Controlled interventions show that capability is primarily model-centered: model choice strongly changes success, harness effects are descriptive, and a forced waypoint interface helps weaker models but can hinder stronger ones. Performance nevertheless falls sharply on longer-horizon tasks, while latency and context growth limit sustained operation. These results show that agentic control is already competitive in zero-shot navigation and that models, harnesses, and interfaces offer complementary paths toward autonomous embodied agents.

</details>


### [3] [Explicit Kinematic Guidance from Analytic Concepts for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26513)
*Mingyang Sun, Jiude Wei, Xiujian Liang, Qichen He, Donglin Wang, Cewu Lu, Jianhua Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 普通相关 (59.89) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出Concept Expert模块，为VLA模型构建可执行的分析概念（Analytic Concepts），将物体表示为显式的程序化蓝图。该机制在VLA推理前利用3D信息估计初始运动学参数，在操作过程中动态跟踪概念参数，为VLA微调提供稠密奖励和精确空间引导，在监督学习和强化学习设置下均提升了成功率和学习效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前VLA模型主要依赖2D输入，忽略了3D物理世界中丰富的物体结构信息和常识知识，限制了其在复杂高精度操作任务中的空间感知能力和适应性。

Method: 构建Concept Expert模块，分两阶段运作：(1) VLA推理前，利用视觉基础模型(VFMs)的3D信息估计初始运动学和结构参数；(2) 操作过程中，VLA模型动态跟踪概念参数并与观测变化对齐。分析概念为VLA微调提供稠密程序化操作奖励和精确空间引导，同时保持端到端学习灵活性。

Result: 在监督学习和强化学习设置下，该方法在成功率和学习效率上均取得一致性提升，证明了结构化概念引导对VLA后训练的有效性。

Conclusion: 显式的、基于概念的结构化引导能够有效提升VLA模型在复杂操作任务中的表现，为具身智能中融合3D结构化知识与端到端学习提供了可行路径。

Classification reason: 该论文提出面向VLA（视觉-语言-动作）模型的概念专家模块，通过3D信息构建可执行的分析概念（Analytic Concepts），为机器人操作提供显式运动学引导和稠密奖励，属于具身智能中世界模型与规划控制方向，因此映射到[CANONICAL]具身世界模型与规划控制。

Importance reason: Focuses on robotics manipulation, not core continuous multimodal or generation topics. Signals: VLA model, analytic concepts, 3D spatial guidance, reinforcement learning, robotics manipulation

Abstract: Current Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models rely mainly on 2D inputs, neglecting the rich object structural information and commonsense knowledge inherent in the 3D physical world. This deficiency restricts their spatial awareness and adaptability for complex, high-precision manipulation. To bridge this crucial gap, we construct a Concept Expert module for VLA to build executable Analytic Concepts that represent objects as explicit, programmatic blueprints. Our mechanism operates in two synergistic phases: First, prior to VLA inference, the Concept Expert leverages 3D information from Vision Foundation Models (VFMs) to estimate the initial kinematic and structural parameters. Second, throughout the manipulation process, the VLA model utilizes its inherent capability to dynamically track the dynamic concept parameters, continuously aligning them with observational changes to ensure persistent accuracy. Once established, the Analytic Concepts provide explicit, high-quality guidance for VLA fine-tuning through (1) dense, programmatic manipulation rewards and (2) precise spatial guidance. This formulation allows VLA models to learn physically grounded interaction behaviors while maintaining end-to-end learning flexibility. Our experimental results show consistent improvements in success rate and learning efficiency across supervised and reinforcement learning settings, demonstrating the effectiveness of structured, concept-based guidance for VLA post-training.

</details>


### [4] [Enfold: Folding World-Generator Computation into Predictive Representations for Efficient Embodied Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26657)
*Weili Zeng, Yitong Xing, Fulong Liu, Chengqun Yang, Antao Xiang, Feng Tian, Jingnan Gao, Jisong Cai, Xin Wang, Xiaomin Wu, Yao Mu, Yichao Yan*

Main category: cs.RO

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 值得关注 (68.56) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Enfold 提出将世界生成模型（扩散/流匹配）在生成未来轨迹过程中产生的多层级中间状态，蒸馏到仅从当前视觉和语言指令推断的表征中。该表征用于条件化未来生成和任务头读取，推理时无需执行生成器，在 LIBERO、RoboTwin2.0 和真实机器人任务上实现 3.7×-10.1× 的延迟降低，同时保持强控制性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统世界生成模型在每一步都需要执行生成器来渲染未来或提供潜上下文，计算开销大、延迟高。作者认为生成器更可重用的资产是其构建未来的计算过程本身（中间状态蕴含了外观、空间布局和交互的多层级信息），而非最终生成的未来帧。因此希望将这种未来生成计算内化到仅从当前观测推断的表征中，实现高效控制。

Method: Enfold 包含三个组件：(1) 未来生成器（Future Generator），使用流匹配（flow matching）将带噪声的未来轨迹去噪为连贯的未来帧序列，暴露其多层级中间状态；(2) 当前编码器（Current Encoder），仅从当前视觉上下文和语言指令预测表征，受生成器中间状态监督；(3) 任务头（Task Heads），读取该表征进行动作预测。训练时，生成器处理观测到的未来轨迹，其多层级中间状态作为监督信号训练当前编码器；学习到的表征反馈给生成器条件化未来生成，同时被任务头读取，但任务梯度不反向传播到编码器。推理时，仅使用当前编码器和任务头，完全跳过生成器。

Result: 在 LIBERO、RoboTwin2.0 和真实机器人任务上，Enfold 在保持强控制性能的同时，动作延迟相比 Fast-WAM 降低 3.7 倍，Enfold-Flash 版本达到 10.1 倍。表征分析表明，Enfold 的表征抑制了无关变化，优先捕捉长时程中涌现的变化。当当前场景被人为干预改变时，生成的未来延续和执行的动作均能自适应调整，而非固定轨迹回放。

Conclusion: Enfold 证明世界生成器的计算过程可以被蒸馏到仅从当前观测推断的表征中，从而在推理时无需执行生成器即可实现高效控制。这重新定义了世界生成器的角色——它不仅是未来帧的生成器，更是预测性控制表征的来源。未来工作可探索更复杂的生成器架构和更丰富的任务场景。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Enfold 方法，将世界生成模型的计算过程内化到当前视觉上下文的表征中，用于机器人动作预测与控制，属于具身智能中世界模型用于规划控制的范畴，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制。

Importance reason: Novel method to distill world model computation into efficient representations, directly addressing model efficiency and world model priorities. Signals: world model distillation, efficient embodied control, diffusion/flow matching, 3.7x-10.1x latency reduction, representation analysis

Abstract: World generative models are typically used through what they produce: a rendered future, a video-conditioned action, or latent context computed by a costly generative branch. We argue that their more reusable asset is the computation that constructs a future. As a generator transforms a corrupted future into a coherent trajectory, its intermediate states organize appearance, spatial layout, and interaction across levels of abstraction. Can this future-generative computation be internalized in a representation inferred from the present alone? We present Enfold, which transfers this computation into a representation predicted from the current visual context and language instruction. During training, multi-level states exposed as the generator processes the observed future supervise a current-only encoder. The learned representation is fed back to condition future generation and is read by task heads without allowing task gradients to reshape the encoder. At deployment, action prediction no longer executes the generator. Across LIBERO, RoboTwin2.0, and real-robot tasks, Enfold supports strong control while reducing action latency by $3.7\times$ relative to Fast--WAM, Enfold-Flash reaches $10.1\times$. Representation analyses show that it suppresses nuisance variation and preferentially captures changes that emerge over longer horizons. When the current scene is altered by human intervention, both the generated continuation and the executed actions adapt, which is inconsistent with fixed trajectory replay. These results recast a world generator as a source of predictive control representations: its future need not be materialized at every step if its internal structure can be enfolded into the present.

</details>


### [5] [ActSWM: Action-Sensitive World Models for Long-Horizon Planning in Open-World Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26712)
*Zhenfeng Gan, ZiTong Zeng, Jiajun Cheng, Yeke Song, Yongyi Tang, Xueqian Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 高优先级 (93.65) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: ActSWM 提出了一种动作敏感的潜空间世界模型，通过过渡-分离原则约束潜空间 rollout 保持对不同动作序列的区分度，解决了现有潜空间预测器在长时域规划中出现的"上下文坍缩"问题，在 Minecraft 长时域规划和跨游戏动作恢复任务中取得更好效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潜空间世界模型在长时域 rollout 中会出现"上下文坍缩"——不同动作序列下预测的未来状态高度相似，导致模型对动作不敏感，无法有效支持长时域规划。作者旨在解决这一关键缺陷。

Method: 提出过渡-分离原则（transition-separation principle）：有用的规划潜动力学模型应保持不同动作序列的未来状态可区分，且每个局部过渡对应的动作应可恢复。基于此原则，将动作敏感性作为潜 rollout 的约束而非仅作为辅助预测目标，构建 ActSWM 模型。

Result: 在步进漂移分析、Minecraft 闭环规划和跨游戏局部动作恢复任务中，ActSWM 相比现有基线保持了更大的动作依赖 rollout 差距，提升了长时域交互场景中的任务成功率，并实现了基于世界模型从离线游戏视频中恢复动作。

Conclusion: 动作敏感性约束能有效缓解潜空间世界模型的上下文坍缩问题，提升长时域规划能力，且学到的模型具有跨游戏迁移的动作恢复能力。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 ActSWM，一种面向开放世界游戏（Minecraft）中长时域规划的动作敏感潜空间世界模型，属于具身智能/机器人规划控制范畴，与 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly addresses world model long-horizon planning with a novel action-sensitive latent dynamics principle, highly relevant to user's world_model priority. Signals: world model, long-horizon planning, latent space rollout, context collapse, Minecraft

Abstract: Latent world models support efficient model-predictive control by optimizing future control sequences in latent space and replanning in a receding-horizon manner. However, existing latent predictors often lack stable long-horizon rollout ability, and prediction accuracy alone does not ensure that rollouts remain responsive to the actions being planned. We identify Context Collapse, a failure mode in which autoregressive latent predictors maintain high similarity to future states while producing nearly indistinguishable futures under different action sequences. To address this issue, we propose ActSWM, an action-sensitive latent world model grounded in a transition-separation principle: a planning-useful latent dynamics model should keep alternative-action futures distinguishable and make the action associated with each local transition recoverable. Under this principle, action sensitivity is enforced as a constraint on latent rollouts rather than treated only as an auxiliary prediction target, encouraging predicted futures to preserve action-dependent differences over long horizons. Across step-drift analysis, closed-loop Minecraft planning, and cross-game local action recovery, ActSWM preserves larger action-dependent rollout gaps than existing baselines, improves task success in long-horizon interactive settings, and enables world-model-based action recovery from offline gameplay videos.

</details>


### [6] [CheckVLA: Execution-Time Verification with Action-Conditioned World Model for Long-Horizon Mobile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26789)
*Yushan Liu, Peibo Sun, Xintao Chao, Zhenyang Yang, Yifan Xie, Lingfeng Zhang, Shoujie Li, Chenyu Tang, Fang Chen, Xiao-Ping Zhang, Wenbo Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 高优先级 (88.85) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: CheckVLA 提出一种执行时验证方法，利用单独训练并冻结的动作条件世界模型，在 VLA 策略执行开环动作块时检测偏差并触发干预修复，在 RoboCasa365 上相比周期性重规划提升 8.5 个百分点的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 策略通常通过开环动作块执行长程移动操作，发出多个动作后不再接收新的高层视觉输入。一旦执行过程中发生意外偏差，剩余动作会持续传播错误，而提交时的策略置信度无法响应执行后的偏差，仅基于观测的异常分数也缺乏动作条件参考来区分预期变化与意外变化。

Method: CheckVLA 包含：(1) 一个单独训练并冻结的动作条件世界模型，用于预测动作执行后的预期观测演变；(2) 基于 conformal calibration 的风险阈值，控制首次不必要干预的 episode 级概率；(3) 当风险超过阈值时，通过重写后缀来替换失效的动作块，同时保留与已执行部分一致的语义；(4) 延迟感知的硬前缀机制，限制替换为仍可部署的动作；(5) 事件驱动的关键帧库，在多次修复间保留先前进展的证据。

Result: 在 RoboCasa365 上，CheckVLA 在相同训练配方和调用预算下达到 36.1% 的平均成功率，优于周期性重规划的 27.6%（+8.5 点）。在 5% episode 级假警报目标下，动作条件验证的及时召回率达到 77.9%，远高于仅观测控制的 48.6% 和动作打乱控制的 37.9%。

Conclusion: 动作条件验证能够有效恢复块式执行期间的反馈机制，同时保持修复与推理延迟的一致性，为长程移动操作中的执行时错误检测与修复提供了一种可行方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 CheckVLA，使用动作条件世界模型（action-conditioned world model）对 VLA 策略的长程移动操作进行执行时验证与干预。核心是面向机器人具身操作的世界模型用于规划控制与错误检测，与 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strong world model verification for VLA, but in robotics rather than core continuous multimodal or video generation. Signals: world model, action-conditioned verification, VLA policy, long-horizon manipulation, conformal calibration

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies commonly execute long-horizon mobile manipulation through open-loop action chunks, issuing multiple actions without receiving new high-level visual input. A committed chunk therefore implies how observations should evolve, but accidental deviations can violate this expectation while the remaining actions continue to propagate the error: commit-time policy confidence cannot react to a deviation that occurs after dispatch, and observation-only anomaly scores lack an action-conditioned reference for separating expected effects from unexplained changes. We propose CheckVLA, which verifies execution with a separately trained, frozen action-conditioned world model. A conformally calibrated risk threshold bounds the episode-level probability of an unnecessary first intervention and determines when to intervene, its exceedance controls how strongly the rewritten suffix retains the superseded chunk, latency-aware hard prefixing restricts replacement to actions that remain deployable, and an event-driven keyframe bank preserves evidence of prior progress across repairs. On RoboCasa365, under a common training recipe and a matched invocation budget, CheckVLA attains a 36.1% average success rate against 27.6% for periodic replanning (+8.5 points). At a matched 5% episode-level false-alarm target, action conditioning raises timely recall to 77.9%, against 48.6% for an observation-only control and 37.9% for an action-shuffled control. These simulation results support action-conditioned verification as a way to restore feedback during chunked execution while keeping the repair consistent with inference latency.

</details>

TL;DR: BioVLN 是一个面向生物医学实验室的视觉语言导航仿真平台，将仪器建模为物理体、安全净空区和操作区三个区域，支持程序化场景生成和强化学习接口，实验表明几何探索方法可达 74.4-87.5% 的成功率，采样多个有效操作位置可进一步提升至 83.3-92.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身导航平台主要面向家庭环境，将目标表示为物体中心或任意附近位置，这种表示不适用于实验室仪器——机器人必须从仪器的操作侧接近并保持安全距离。因此需要专门为生物医学实验室场景设计的导航仿真平台。

Method: BioVLN 将每个仪器建模为三个区域：物理体、安全净空区和操作区（位于可用侧前方）。该模型一致地应用于场景生成、目标放置、导航评估和安全分析。平台支持程序化场景生成和手动设计环境，共产生 47 个场景和 1667 个 episode。提供标准化的导航和强化学习接口，支持轨迹收集和策略训练。

Result: 几何探索方法达到 74.4-87.5% 的成功率；在操作区中采样多个有效位置可将成功率提升至 83.3-92.5%，并减少不安全接近的情况。

Conclusion: BioVLN 为生物医学实验室机器人导航提供了专门的仿真平台，其三区域仪器建模方式更符合实际操作需求，多位置采样策略能有效提升导航成功率和安全性。

Classification reason: 该论文构建了一个面向生物医学实验室的视觉语言导航仿真平台，属于具身智能中的机器人导航任务，与 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制 方向高度匹配。

Importance reason: Specialized biomedical navigation platform with low overlap with user's priority subtopics on continuous multimodal models and video generation. Signals: domain-specific simulation, no continuous token or flow matching, no video generation, no autoregressive generation, no world model for physics

Abstract: Biomedical laboratory robots must navigate to instruments before performing experimental procedures. Existing embodied navigation platforms are designed for household environments and treat a target as an object center or an arbitrary nearby position. This representation is inadequate for laboratory instruments, which must be approached from their operating side while maintaining safe clearance from surrounding equipment. We introduce BioVLN, a simulation platform for developing and evaluating visual-language navigation agents in biomedical laboratories. BioVLN represents each instrument with three regions: its physical body, a surrounding clearance region, and an operation area in front of the usable side. This model is applied consistently to scene generation, target placement, navigation evaluation, and safety analysis, so success depends on reaching a position from which the instrument can be accessed. BioVLN supports procedural scene generation and manually designed environments, producing 47 scenes and 1667 episodes. Standardized navigation and reinforcement-learning interfaces enable trajectory collection and policy training. Experiments show that geometric exploration reaches 74.4--87.5% success, while sampling multiple valid positions in the operation area improves success to 83.3--92.5% and reduces unsafe proximity.

</details>


### [8] [RL$^2$-VLA: Adaptive RL Latent Compositional Steering with Test-Time Scaling for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26991)
*Derek Ming Siang Tan, Shailesh Shailesh, Srikrishna Iyer, William Wei Jie Teo, Yuanliang Ju, Qiao Gu, Guillaume Sartoretti*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出RL²-VLA，一种自适应推理时引导框架，利用强化学习在VLA模型的潜空间中进行组合式引导。通过轻量级离线RL策略与冻结的VLA动作专家进行流速度组合，仅在预测失败时激活引导，在SIMPLER和PolaRiS基准上提升域外成功率最高达17.3%，并在真实机器人实验中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在域外和困难任务上性能下降严重。现有测试时引导方法虽然无需重新训练，但动作样本集中在相似行为上，继承了相关失败模式；且对所有时间步采用相同干预策略，未考虑基础策略是否已可能成功。

Method: 从VLA动作专家中提取表达性潜变量，训练轻量级离线RL策略；推理时将RL策略的流速度与冻结VLA的流速度进行组合引导。进一步发现推理时引导在成功与失败状态下遵循不同的缩放定律，因此仅在预测失败时激活组合引导，避免扰动已准确的动作。

Result: 在SIMPLER和PolaRiS基准上，域外设置成功率提升最高达17.3%。消融和缩放实验证明了潜表征和RL训练的重要性。真实世界实验验证了仿真到现实的迁移能力。

Conclusion: RL²-VLA作为一种实用且模块化的VLA部署引导框架，通过自适应激活组合引导有效提升了VLA模型在域外场景中的鲁棒性和成功率。

Classification reason: 该论文提出RL²-VLA框架，通过强化学习对VLA模型的潜空间进行自适应推理时组合引导，提升机器人操作策略在域外场景中的成功率。论文聚焦于具身智能中的视觉-语言-动作模型推理时优化与规划控制，属于具身世界模型与规划控制方向。

Importance reason: Focuses on robot control (cs.RO) and world models for embodied agents, which is a lower-priority direction compared to continuous language/multimodal models and video generation. Signals: RL-based latent steering, test-time scaling for VLA, adaptive failure-aware intervention, real-world robot validation, modular framework for VLA deployment

Abstract: Despite the impressive visuomotor capabilities enabled by Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models, their performance often degrades on challenging and out-of-domain tasks. Recent test-time steering and scaling methods improve performance without extensive data collection and retraining, but action samples often remain concentrated around similar behaviors and therefore inherit correlated failure modes. Moreover, existing methods apply the same intervention strategy at every timestep, regardless of whether the base policy is already likely to succeed. To address these limitations, we introduce $RL^2$, an adaptive inference-time steering framework that leverages Reinforcement Learning on VLA Latents. First, we train a lightweight offline RL policy conditioned on expressive latents extracted from the VLA action expert and compose its flow velocity with that of the frozen VLA during inference. This compositional steering strategy combines the behavioral priors of large-scale imitation learning with the action diversity induced by offline RL beyond dominant demonstration modes. We further discover that inference-time steering follows fundamentally different scaling laws under success and failure states, revealing that action diversity is most beneficial when the base VLA is likely to fail, but can unnecessarily perturb already-accurate actions when success is likely. Building on this insight, $RL^2$ activates compositional steering only when failure is predicted. Across the SIMPLER and PolaRiS benchmarks, $RL^2$ improves success rates by up to +17.3% in out-of-domain settings, while ablations and scaling studies demonstrate the importance of latent representations and RL training. Finally, real-world experiments demonstrate that these gains transfer beyond simulation, establishing $RL^2$ as a practical and modular steering framework for VLA deployment.

</details>


### [9] [DLAM: Distributional Latent Actions with Temporal Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27138)
*Zuojin Tang, Feifan Luo, Haoyun Liu, Botai Yuan, Dekang Qi, Ronghan Chen, Yandan Yang, Tong Lin, Xinyuan Chang, Mu Xu, Bin Liu, De Ma, Zhiheng Ma*

Main category: cs.RO, cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 值得关注 (65.06) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: DLAM 提出一种分布性潜动作模型，将视频中的帧间过渡表示为对角高斯分布，通过归一化组合与反转约束实现时序一致的潜动力学建模，并联合 flow-matching 策略生成潜动作序列与机器人动作，在多个机器人操作任务上提升了策略性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 模型受限于稀缺的动作标注机器人数据，而无动作视频蕴含丰富的物理变化先验。潜动作模型可从无动作视频中提取先验，但现有方法重建训练得到的编码缺乏结构化约束，难以与机器人动作联合生成；而引入时序约束的结构化方法仍保留确定性过渡点，局部残差误差在递归组合中会累积传播。

Method: DLAM 将每个帧间过渡建模为对角高斯分布。通过参考帧条件重建将均值锚定在观测视觉变化上；利用等间隔三元组的归一化组合与反转约束来约束均值和逐维方差。方差组合使用轻量共享相关系数来建模相邻过渡间的依赖关系，反转操作则取反均值并保持方差。下游策略学习时，冻结编码器，训练 flow-matching 策略联合生成均值过渡序列与机器人动作。

Result: 在保留的过渡上，DLAM 学习到比现有潜动作基线更时序一致的潜动力学，在直接重建和累积重建上均表现更强。在 MetaWorld MT50、LIBERO 和真实操作任务上，DLAM 在相同 π₀ 迁移协议下提升了策略性能。消融实验表明归一化均值约束贡献了大部分重建增益，而学习方差和相关性感知组合在下游控制中提供了互补改进。

Conclusion: DLAM 通过分布性建模和时序约束有效提升了潜动作模型的时序一致性和下游策略性能，验证了从无动作视频中提取结构化潜先验用于机器人学习的可行性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出分布性潜动作模型 DLAM，从无动作视频中提取潜动作先验，并用于机器人策略学习（MetaWorld、LIBERO、真实操作任务），属于具身世界模型与规划控制方向，与 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Proposes distributional latent actions with temporal constraints for world model learning, aligning with user's high-priority world model and continuous representation subtopics. Signals: world model, latent action model, flow matching, temporal consistency, robot manipulation

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models remain constrained by scarce action-labeled robot data, whereas action-free videos offer abundant observations of physical change. Latent action models can extract such priors, but reconstruction-trained codes may predict future observations without the structure required for joint generation with robot actions. Existing structured methods add temporal constraints but retain deterministic transition points, so residual errors in locally inferred transitions may propagate and compound under recursive composition. We introduce DLAM, a distributional latent-action model that represents each transition as a diagonal Gaussian. Reconstruction conditioned on the reference frame grounds the mean in observed visual change, while normalized composition and reversal over equal-gap triplets constrain both the mean and dimension-wise variance. Variance composition uses a lightweight shared-correlation coefficient to account for dependence between adjacent transitions that share an intermediate frame, whereas reversal negates the mean and preserves the variance. For downstream policy learning, we freeze the encoder and train a flow-matching policy to jointly generate mean transition sequences and robot actions. On held-out transitions, DLAM learns more temporally consistent latent dynamics than existing latent-action baselines and achieves stronger direct and cumulative reconstruction on held-out videos. Under the same controlled $\pi_0$ transfer protocol, it also improves policy performance on MetaWorld MT50, LIBERO, and real-world manipulation tasks. Controlled ablations show that normalized mean constraints account for most of the reconstruction gain, while learned variance and correlation-aware composition provide complementary improvements in downstream control.

</details>


### [10] [From Passive Video to Editable Experience: Physically Grounded Experience Synthesis for Embodied Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26903)
*Jia Luo*

Main category: cs.RO, cs.AI

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 具身世界模型与规划控制

Importance: 值得关注 (78.74) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Pegasus是一个低资源框架，通过构建图结构中间表示（任务图→可供性图→约束图→机器人规划图）将人类演示视频转化为机器人可学习的数据，并利用层次化可供性潜空间和闭环物理验证器实现跨形态迁移，在GTEA Gaze+和EPIC-KITCHENS-100等基准上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能的关键瓶颈在于数据而非模型架构。虽然互联网上有海量人类操作视频，但由于人类与机器人形态之间的具身鸿沟（embodiment gap），机器人无法直接从中学习。现有方法依赖昂贵的机器人遥操作数据采集，难以规模化。因此，需要一种低资源的方法将人类演示转化为机器人可学习的数据。

Method: Pegasus框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 图结构中间表示——从人类视频中提取任务图（Task Graph），通过可供性图（Affordance Graph）和约束图（Constraint Graph）转换为机器人规划图（Robot Planning Graph）；(2) 层次化可供性潜空间（hierarchical affordance latent space），建模物体状态、可供性与任务之间的关系，实现超越物体身份泛化；(3) 闭环物理验证器（closed-loop physics verifier），通过运动学可行性、碰撞约束和关节限制过滤无效生成。

Result: 在GTEA Gaze+和EPIC-KITCHENS-100等自我中心操作基准上，以及多种机器人形态上评估了任务正确性、可执行性、状态一致性和可学习性指标。结果表明Pegasus能够实现可靠的跨形态翻译，将机器人数据生成从硬件采集问题重新定义为可扩展的低资源知识迁移问题。

Conclusion: Pegasus证明了通过结构化知识表示和层次化潜空间建模，可以将人类演示视频有效转化为机器人可学习的数据，为具身智能提供了一种低资源、可扩展的数据生成范式，有望缓解具身AI中的数据瓶颈问题。

Classification reason: 该论文提出Pegasus框架，通过任务图、可供性图、约束图等结构化知识表示，将人类演示视频转化为机器人可学习的数据，核心关注具身智能中跨形态数据生成与知识迁移，属于具身世界模型与规划控制方向。

Importance reason: Focuses on robot embodiment and planning graphs, not on core priority subtopics like continuous language models or autoregressive generation. Signals: world model, video-to-robot transfer, graph-based representation, low-resource data generation, cross-embodiment

Abstract: The key bottleneck in embodied AI is not model architecture but data. Although billions of human manipulation videos exist online, robots cannot directly learn from them due to the embodiment gap between human morphology and robot hardware. We introduce Pegasus, a low-resource framework that bridges this gap by translating human demonstrations into robot-learnable data through structured knowledge transfer. Instead of relying on raw video prompts, Pegasus constructs a graph-based intermediate representation: a Task Graph extracted from human videos is transformed through Affordance and Constraint Graphs into a Robot Planning Graph for robot-conditioned video generation. A hierarchical affordance latent space models the relationship between object states, affordances, and tasks, enabling generalization beyond object identities. A closed-loop physics verifier further filters invalid generations using kinematic feasibility, collision constraints, and joint limits. We evaluate Pegasus across a range of egocentric manipulation benchmarks, including GTEA Gaze+ and EPIC-KITCHENS-100, and diverse robot embodiments, assessing Task Correctness, Executability, State Consistency, and Learnability. Results demonstrate reliable cross-embodiment translation and show that robot data generation can be reframed from a hardware collection problem into a scalable, low-resource knowledge transfer problem.

</details>

TL;DR: HumanCLAW 是一个解耦动作决策与底层执行的具身评测框架，通过将 VLM 的高层技能指令转化为物理世界中的连续全身运动，评估模型的"动作智能"。基于该框架构建的 HumanCLAW-Bench 包含 1,218 个长程导航-交互任务，测试了 9 个 SOTA VLM，最佳模型成功率仅 16.8%，发现当前 VLM 缺乏具身自我意识（无法感知自身位置、是否到达目标或碰到障碍）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLM 在物理世界中行动能力的评估存在根本性困难：任务失败时无法区分是 VLM 决策错误还是底层运动控制失败（如失去平衡）。需要一种能解耦高层决策与底层执行的评测方法，以单独衡量 VLM 的动作智能。

Method: 提出 HumanCLAW 框架，将 VLM 的高层动作决策与底层运动控制解耦。在每个时间步，VLM 发出原子技能指令（如"向前走"、"抓取"），该指令被翻译为亚秒级的连续全身运动，在物理世界中执行（包含重力、碰撞等真实物理效应）。通过 harness 系统隔离执行侧的干扰（平衡、电机误差），仅测量 VLM 的逐时刻动作选择。基于此构建 HumanCLAW-Bench，包含 1,218 个第一人称视角的长程"发现-导航-交互"任务，覆盖 41 个室内场景。

Result: 测试了 9 个 SOTA VLM（包括 GPT-4o、Claude 3.5 Sonnet、Gemini 1.5 Pro 等），最佳模型成功率仅 16.8%。分析表明，目标识别并非瓶颈，VLM 缺乏的是具身自我意识——无法跟踪自身身体位置、判断是否到达目标或是否碰到障碍。

Conclusion: 当前 VLM 在具身物理行动中表现极差，核心短板是缺乏具身自我意识。HumanCLAW 提供了一个干净、可复现的基准来单独评估 VLM 的动作决策能力，为未来具身 VLM 研究提供了明确的方向。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 HumanCLAW 评测框架，评估 VLM 在具身物理体中的动作决策能力，涉及具身智能、动作规划与控制，与 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制 高度匹配。同时涉及 VLM 的具身推理能力，也匹配 multimodal_understanding_generation 方向下的 multimodal_reasoning_cot。

Importance reason: Directly evaluates VLM embodied action intelligence and self-awareness, aligning with world model and multimodal understanding priorities. Signals: world model evaluation, embodied self-awareness, VLM action intelligence, long-horizon navigation-interaction, decoupled action-execution framework

Abstract: Evaluating whether a vision-language model (VLM) can act through a physical body is challenging. The outcome of an action couples the VLM's decision with motor control. When a task fails, it is hard to tell whether the VLM made a bad choice or the motor controller simply failed to execute it, e.g., losing balance and falling. In this work, we introduce HumanCLAW, an evaluation framework that decouples action decision-making from low-level execution. At every step, a harnessed, off-the-shelf VLM issues an atomic skill command, and the command is translated into a sub-second chunk of continuous full-body motion with real physical consequences, including gravity and collisions. The body can therefore act freely in the physical world, while execution-side disturbances, balance and motor errors, are factored out. What remains measurable is the model's action intelligence: its moment-to-moment choice of what the body should execute next. Based on this framework, we build HumanCLAW-Bench: 1,218 long-horizon, egocentric find-navigate-interact episodes across 41 indoor scenes. We test nine state-of-the-art VLMs and find that none solves the benchmark; the best model reaches only a 16.8% success rate. Recognizing the target is not the bottleneck. What current VLMs lack is embodied self-awareness: they lose track of their own body, failing to tell where it is, whether it has reached the goal, or whether it has hit an obstacle.

</details>

*Hengyi Xie, Chenfei Yao, Xianjin Wu, Xuanyang Xi, Yiping Tang, Di Xu, Yingying Zhu, Dingkang Liang, Xiang Bai, Han Ding*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 VLA 模型通常采用以 LLM 为中心的 V→L→A 路径，将视觉观测投影到大语言模型的表示空间后再解码为机器人动作。这种设计虽然有效，但在每次策略调用时会产生大量的计算和内存开销，难以在消费级 GPU 上实现实时推理。作者希望探索一种更高效的 VLA 范式，降低计算和内存成本，使机器人操作策略能够在资源受限的硬件上实时运行。

Method: TurboVLA 摒弃了以 LLM 为中心的架构，采用直接 V+L→A 映射。具体地：1）独立编码视觉观测和语言指令；2）通过轻量级双向视觉-语言交互模块在两者之间直接交换信息；3）使用紧凑的解码器预测连续动作块。该设计直接从视觉和语言特征构建任务条件化表示，避免了 LLM 带来的巨大计算和内存开销。模型仅 0.2B 参数。

Result: 在 LIBERO 基准上，TurboVLA 以 0.2B 参数实现了 97.7% 的平均成功率，推理延迟仅 31.2ms（32Hz），推理 VRAM 仅 0.9GB，在消费级 RTX 4090 上运行，性能匹配或超越了显著更大的 VLA 策略模型。

Conclusion: TurboVLA 证明，以 LLM 为中心的 VLA 范式并非实现高性能机器人操作的唯一途径。通过直接 V+L→A 映射的简洁设计，可以在极低的计算和内存成本下达到甚至超越大型 VLA 模型的性能，为高效机器人操作策略提供了新的视角。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 TurboVLA，一种高效的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型，用于机器人操作任务。它属于世界模型方向下的具身世界模型与规划控制（embodied_world_model）子主题，因为该子主题涵盖面向机器人、具身智能、动作规划和控制的世界模型，而 TurboVLA 正是针对机器人操作的高效 VLA 策略。

Importance reason: Directly addresses world model and efficient inference priorities with a novel V+L→A paradigm achieving real-time 32Hz on consumer hardware. Signals: world model, efficient inference, real-time VLA, 32Hz on RTX 4090, <1GB VRAM

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models commonly adopt an LLM-centric $V \to L \to A$ pathway, where visual observations are projected into the representation space of a large language model before being decoded into robot actions. Although effective, this design incurs substantial computation and memory overhead at every policy invocation. In this work, we introduce TurboVLA, a new VLA paradigm that reformulates the conventional $V \to L \to A$ pathway as a direct $V + L \to A$ mapping. Instead of using a large language model as the central interface between perception and action, TurboVLA independently encodes visual observations and language instructions, directly exchanges information between them through lightweight bidirectional vision-language interaction, and predicts continuous action chunks with a compact decoder. This simple design constructs task-conditioned representations directly from visual and linguistic features, significantly reducing the computational and memory costs of VLA inference. On LIBERO, TurboVLA achieves 97.7% average success with only 0.2B parameters, 31.2 ms inference latency, and 0.9 GB inference VRAM on a consumer-grade RTX 4090, matching or outperforming substantially larger VLA policies. These results establish TurboVLA as a simple and effective alternative to the prevailing LLM-centric VLA paradigm, offering a new perspective on how vision, language, and action can be connected for efficient robotic manipulation. Code is available at .

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出 Mental World Modeling (MWM) 框架，将心理状态变量（信念、意图、情感等）作为世界模型的核心组成部分，而非事后解释。通过维护耦合的物理-心理世界状态、生成目标特定部分观察、模拟候选动作对两者的联合更新，MWM 能够更准确地预测人类决策。作者在文本、图像和视频故事场景数据集上验证了该方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型仅关注物理场景（物体是什么、在哪里、如何演化），但人类行为由隐藏的心理状态驱动（信念、欲望、意图、情感、社会规范等）。仅追踪物理场景而不追踪每个智能体对场景的认知和信念，会导致对看似正确的场景预测出错误的行为。因此需要将心理变量纳入世界模型的核心框架。

Method: 提出 Mental World Modeling (MWM) 理论框架，维护耦合的物理-心理世界状态，渲染目标特定部分观察，模拟候选动作对两者的联合更新。实例化为 MENTIS 基线系统，分解为：状态解析、目标观察生成、动作分解、耦合物理-心理转移、分支级价值评估五个步骤。使用 8 个现代 LLM 作为基础世界模型，在人工构建的文本/图像/视频故事决策场景数据集上进行实验。

Result: 实验表明，显式建模心理状态对于预测人类决策至关重要。8 个 LLM 基线的结果一致显示，加入心理状态建模后决策预测准确率显著提升。深入分析进一步揭示了当前心理世界建模的瓶颈所在。

Conclusion: MWM 代表了世界建模的下一阶段——从模拟物理场景到模拟在场景中行动的智能体心智。将心理变量作为世界模型的核心组件而非事后解释，能够更准确地预测人类行为，为更智能的交互式 AI 系统奠定基础。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Mental World Modeling (MWM) 框架，将心理状态（信念、意图、情感等）作为世界模型的核心组成部分，用于预测人类决策。这属于世界模型方向，与 [CANONICAL] 具身世界模型与规划控制 高度相关，因为该框架关注智能体（人类）的规划、决策与行动模拟，且论文明确将其定位为"世界建模的下一阶段"。

Importance reason: Novel framework integrating mental state into world models, highly relevant to world_model and continuous multimodal reasoning. Signals: world model, mental state modeling, physical-mental coupling, human decision prediction, training-free baseline

Abstract: World models enable a predictive substrate for planning and action, yet existing formulations merely answer a physical question: what/where it is, and how will it evolve. Human behavior, however, is driven by hidden mental state (what a person believes, wants, intends, feels, and considers socially permissible), so a model that tracks the physical scene but not what each agent knows and believes about it predicts the wrong action for the right-looking scene. We formulate Mental World Modeling (MWM), a generic theoretical framework that makes mental variables core components of a world model rather than posthoc rationales: MWM aintains a coupled physical-mental world state, renders a target-specific partial observation, and simulates how candidate actions jointly update both components. We instantiate the framework in MENTIS, a training-free and fully inspectable baseline that decomposes the process into state parsing, target-observation generation, action decomposition, coupled physical and mental transition, and branch-level value evaluation. On a manually constructed, quality-controlled dataset of situated decision scenarios spanning text, image, and sounding-video stories, experiments with 8 modern LLM-based world models demonstrate that explicitly modeling the mental state is essential for predicting human decisions. Deeper analyses further expose the bottlenecks of current mental world modeling. We expect MWM as a next stage of world modeling, from simulating physical scenes to simulating the minds that act in them.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多智能体系统中，世界模型的训练常混淆统计相关性与因果机制，导致在分布偏移下失效。现有方法需要预定义因果图，限制了实用性。

Method: 提出隐式因果世界模型（Implicit Causal World Models），通过引入策略方差（policy variance），利用序列后门条件（sequential backdoor condition）从离线演示中自动发现因果结构，无需预定义因果图。

Result: 在Two-Door、Navigation和Giveway三个协调任务上的实验表明，该模型在完全可观测和部分可观测条件下均能提供可解释的因果表征，且模型精度与干预强度直接相关。

Conclusion: 隐式因果世界模型能够从离线多智能体演示中有效恢复环境动力学因果结构，为模型基强化学习中的因果建模提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文提出隐式因果世界模型（Implicit Causal World Models），从多智能体离线演示中恢复环境动力学，关注物理世界建模与因果机制发现，属于世界模型方向下的可交互与物理世界建模（interactive_physical_world）这一规范主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on multi-agent causal world models, which is tangentially related to world model priority but lacks direct connection to video generation or continuous multimodal models. Signals: Multi-agent world model, Causal discovery from offline data, No pre-defined causal graph, Coordination tasks, Interpretable causal representations

Abstract: In model-based reinforcement learning, world models exist as internal simulators, but their training often conflates statistical correlations with causal mechanisms. This problem is exacerbated in multi-agent systems where physical transitions are intertwined with strategic agent intents, causing world models to fail under distribution shift. We introduce Implicit Causal World Models to recover environmental dynamics from offline demonstrations without requiring pre-defined causal graphs. By incorporating policy variance, we render world models discoverable via the sequential backdoor condition. Evaluations across coordination tasks (Two-Door, Navigation, and Giveway) demonstrate that these models provide interpretable causal representations under both full and partial observability, with model accuracy scaling directly with interventional strength.

</details>


### [15] [CalTwin: Towards Calibrated, Shift-Robust Medical World Models via Fisher-Information Regularisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26752)
*Behraj Khan, Shabir Ahmad, Syed Ahmad Chan Bukhari, Tahir Qasim Syed*

Main category: cs.LG

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 可交互与物理世界建模

Importance: 值得关注 (68.49) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 CalTwin，一种基于 Fisher 信息正则化的医疗世界模型校准方法，通过统一处理协变量偏移和置信度错位两个问题，在 PhysioNet 2019 脓毒症数据集上降低了 OOD 预测误差并改善了置信度校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗世界模型在临床部署中面临两个关键失败模式：(i) 协变量偏移——训练数据分散在不同医院、扫描仪和时间段，导致潜状态动力学预测器在分布外表现差；(ii) 置信度错位——多步预测在临床风险最高的区域往往过度自信。现有方法分别处理这两个问题，缺乏统一解决方案。

Method: CalTwin 将两个轻量级正则化目标结合到 GRU 医疗世界模型的潜状态转移预测器中：(1) 基于 Fisher 信息矩阵的协变量偏移惩罚（FIM penalty），用于缓解碎片化数据带来的分布偏移；(2) 置信度错位惩罚（CMP），用于校准多步预测的置信度。作者推导了组合目标函数，并分析了哪些证明步骤可以直接从分类设置迁移、哪些需要调整。

Result: 在 PhysioNet 2019 脓毒症挑战数据集上，将两个医院系统作为顺序训练片段、未见系统作为 OOD 测试。CalTwin 使 OOD 下一步潜状态 MSE 相对无惩罚基线降低 9.1%（其中 FIM 惩罚单独贡献 7.0%）；CMP 带来的 ECE 降低虽真实但较小（CalTwin 降低 0.7%，CMP 单独降低 1.3%）。

Conclusion: CalTwin 通过 Fisher 信息正则化统一处理了医疗世界模型中的协变量偏移和置信度错位问题，在 OOD 预测精度和置信度校准方面均取得改进，为医疗数字孪生等临床部署场景提供了更可靠的建模方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出面向医疗领域的世界模型（medical world model），关注患者生理状态的潜空间建模与干预下的状态转移预测，属于世界模型方向。其核心关注点在于协变量偏移下的鲁棒预测和置信度校准，与[可交互与物理世界建模]（interactive_physical_world）中环境动力学建模、状态转移预测的主题高度吻合，因此复用该规范子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on medical world model calibration, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and contains negative keywords. Signals: Medical world model, Fisher-Information regularization, covariate shift, confidence calibration, OOD prediction

Abstract: Medical world models aim to learn a latent state of patient or organ physiology and a transition function that forecasts how that state evolves under interventions, supporting downstream tasks from imaging-based diagnosis to digital-twin treatment planning. Two failure modes threaten the reliability of such models in clinical deployment: (i)~\emph{covariate shift}, because training data are fragmented across hospitals, scanners, and time, so the feature distribution seen by the latent-dynamics predictor differs across fragments and from the distribution at deployment; and (ii)~\emph{confidence misalignment}, because multi-step forecasts are often overconfident exactly where clinical risk is highest. We argue that both problems admit a unified treatment via a single lightweight regularisation objective, \textbf{CalTwin}, which combines a Fisher-Information-based shift penalty adapted from our prior work on fragmented covariate-shift remediation~\cite{khan2025mitigating,khan2025causal} with a Confidence Misalignment Penalty adapted from our prior work on calibrated vision-language classification~\cite{khan2025confidence}, applied here to a GRU-based medical world model's latent transition predictor. We derive the combined objective, establish which proof steps transfer from the classification setting without modification and which require adaptation, and evaluate it on the PhysioNet 2019 Sepsis Challenge, treating the two hospital systems as sequential training fragments and the unseen system as an out-of-distribution test. CalTwin reduces OOD next-step latent-state MSE by 9.1\% relative to the no-penalty baseline (FIM penalty alone accounts for 7.0\%); the ECE reduction from the Confidence Misalignment Penalty is real but small (0.7\% for CalTwin, 1.3\% for CMP alone).

</details>


### [16] [What Can Latent World Models Know? Physical Parameter Identifiability in Multimodal Predictive Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27017)
*Kaizhen Tan, Xin Xu, Siru Tao, Hanzhe Hong, Yang Feng, Heqing Du*

Main category: cs.RO, cs.LG

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 可交互与物理世界建模

Importance: 高优先级 (94.86) - Top 3 精读候选

TL;DR: 本文通过受控实验研究潜世界模型中学到的物理参数可识别性，发现预测目标（而非仅输入）决定了哪些物理量进入潜表征：刚度仅在预测触觉时被编码，阻力在所有确定性预测目标下均无法有效学习，而视觉状态在单步预测下也会被丢弃。目标结构决定了潜表征获取哪些物理参数，更多数据仅能改善已获取的参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜世界模型的核心假设是通过预测未来迫使表征内化环境的物理规律，但哪些物理量实际被编码以及由什么因素决定尚不清楚。本文旨在系统性地回答这一问题。

Method: 在POKEWORLD交互式环境中设计受控干预实验：使用证书门控协议（certificate-gated protocol）先验证每个物理参数是否可从原始观测中恢复，再测量其是否进入潜表征。通过对比不同预测目标（视觉预测、触觉预测、单步预测等）下潜表征对质量、阻力、接触刚度等参数的编码效果，并在RH20T真实机器人数据集上跨两个机器人、4258个片段进行验证。

Result: 刚度仅在预测触觉时进入潜表征（R²=0.50 vs -0.02）；单步预测下纯视觉潜表征丢弃了完全可见的物体状态；阻力在所有确定性预测目标下均无法有效学习（R²≈0.13），而监督头可达0.45；RH20T上缺失信息或预测压力的手臂在五倍数据范围内保持平坦，仅完整多模态目标能预测超出持久性基线的力。

Conclusion: 目标结构决定了潜表征获取哪些物理参数：输入限制可知性，预测目标决定保留性。更多数据仅能改善已获取的参数，无法使模型学习到预测目标未要求的物理量。这为设计更有效的世界模型训练目标提供了指导。

Classification reason: 该论文研究潜世界模型（latent world model）中物理参数的可识别性，探讨预测目标如何决定潜表征中保留哪些物理量（质量、阻力、接触刚度）。论文在POKEWORLD交互式环境中进行受控干预实验，属于物理世界建模与动力学建模范畴，与[交互式与物理世界建模]子主题高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly addresses world model identifiability and multimodal predictive representations, highly relevant to continuous multimodal models and world model priorities. Signals: world model, physical parameter identifiability, multimodal predictive representations, latent representation analysis, controlled intervention experiments

Abstract: A central premise of latent world models is that predicting the future forces a representation to internalize the physics of its environment. Which physical quantities does a trained latent actually contain, and what decides this? We answer with controlled interventions in POKEWORLD, an interactive environment whose visually identical objects hide mass, drag, and contact stiffness. A certificate-gated protocol first certifies each parameter as recoverable from raw observations, then measures whether it enters the latent, so a null result can be attributed to the objective rather than to the environment. The resulting identifiability map has two organizing mechanisms and one frontier. Inputs limit what can be known, while prediction targets decide what is retained. Stiffness enters the latent only when touch is forecast ($R^2=0.50$, compared with $-0.02$ when the same signal is merely fused into the input), and under single-step prediction a vision-only latent discards even perfectly visible object state. Drag marks the frontier. It carries a recoverability certificate of 0.89 yet plateaus near 0.13 under every deterministic prediction objective we test, while a supervised head on the same trunk reaches 0.45. Parameters whose readout is slow and ratio-type under the sensed coordinates fall outside what these objectives acquire. On RH20T, an input-target factorial across scaling curves reproduces both mechanisms across two robots and 4,258 episodes. Every arm missing information or prediction pressure stays flat over a fivefold data range, and only the full multimodal objective forecasts force beyond a persistence baseline, with held-out gains that grow with scale. Objective structure determines which physical parameters a latent acquires, and additional data improves only the parameters it already acquires.

</details>

### [17] [CG-World: A Large-Scale World-State Dataset and Protocol for World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26452)
*Yiming Cai, Fangjie Yu, Meiqing Yu, Ziyue Shi, Pengfei Yuan, Yong Guo*

Main category: cs.GR, cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 生成模型评测与数据集

Importance: 高优先级 (92.93) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: CG-World 是一个从工业 CG 管线导出的大规模世界状态数据集，包含约 85 万段 1-5 秒的时序样本，记录了多模态语义、空间结构、骨骼/控制器状态、运动曲线、相机/灯光参数、物理缓存、接触事件等多层次中间状态，并定义了事实轨迹、观测干预、动作干预、机制干预和严格反事实分支，支持世界模型的干预学习与反事实推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频、机器人、仿真数据集通常只捕获状态、动作、事件和观测中的部分结构，缺乏显式记录中间状态和反事实分支的数据，限制了世界模型在可控生成、动作建模和具身策略迁移方面的发展。

Method: 从工业计算机图形学管线中导出数据，显式记录中间状态（多模态语义、空间结构、骨骼/控制器状态、运动曲线、相机/灯光参数、物理缓存、接触事件、多通道渲染），构建约 85 万段 1-5 秒的时序样本。定义分支谱系（事实轨迹、观测干预、动作干预、机制干预、严格反事实分支），并记录干预目标、不变量和替代结果。

Result: 在几何条件视频生成、动作预测和闭环视觉-语言-动作策略迁移三个任务上验证了数据集的有效性，表明 CG-World 为可控生成、动作建模和具身策略迁移提供了可复用的结构化监督。

Conclusion: CG-World 为世界模型、物理 AI 和具身智能提供了共享数据基础设施，未来将通过持续数据收集和社区协作进行扩展。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一个大规模世界状态数据集 CG-World，属于世界模型方向的数据集贡献。在 world_model 方向下存在 generation_benchmark（生成模型评测与数据集）这一非 canonical 但已存在的 subtopic，且该 subtopic 明确包含数据集。同时 multimodal_generation 方向下也有同名的 generation_benchmark subtopic。因此复用该 subtopic。

Importance reason: Provides a large-scale, structured world-state dataset with explicit intervention branches, directly supporting world model and continuous multimodal reasoning research. Signals: world model dataset, intervention learning, counterfactual reasoning, continuous state recording, geometry-conditioned video generation

Abstract: World models must learn the joint dynamics of states, actions, events, and observations, yet existing video, robotics, and simulation datasets usually capture only part of this structure. We introduce CG-World, a large-scale world-state dataset and protocol derived from industrial computer graphics production pipelines. CG-World explicitly records intermediate states, including multimodal semantics, spatial structure, skeletal and controller states, motion curves, camera and lighting parameters, physics caches, contact events, and multi-pass renderings. CG-World v1 contains approximately 850,000 temporally aligned segments of 1-5 seconds. It separates latent states, observations, relations, events, and branch metadata, and organizes them into unified spatiotemporal samples. To support intervention learning and counterfactual reasoning, CG-World defines a branch lineage covering factual trajectories, observation interventions, action interventions, mechanism interventions, and strict counterfactual branches, with intervention targets, invariants, and alternative outcomes explicitly recorded. We evaluate the dataset on geometry-conditioned video generation, action prediction, and closed-loop vision-language-action policy transfer. Results show that CG-World provides reusable structured supervision for controlled generation, action modeling, and embodied policy transfer. We plan to expand CG-World through continued data collection and community collaboration toward a shared data infrastructure for world models, Physical AI, and embodied intelligence.

</details>

### [18] [ContactFlow: A video action conditioning that transfers across embodiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26579)
*Sami Azirar, Enrico Pallotta, Jan Nogga, Jürgen Gall, Sven Behnke, Hermann Blum*

Main category: cs.RO, cs.CV

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 视频世界模型

Importance: 高优先级 (93.65) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Contact Flow 提出了一种与具体机器人形态无关的动作表征方法，通过编码操作过程中3D接触点的轨迹，将人类演示和机器人执行统一到同一条件信号下，训练视频生成式世界模型来预测物理合理的操作结果，并集成到"提议-想象-验证-执行"管线中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于视频的世界模型难以捕捉操作中的物理约束（尤其是接触），且动作条件通常局限于特定机器人形态（如平行夹爪），无法跨形态迁移。作者希望构建一种与具体形态无关的动作表征，使世界模型能同时从人类演示和不同机器人数据中学习。

Method: 提出 Contact Flow，通过追踪操作者与目标物体之间3D接触点的轨迹来编码操作动作，丢弃具体形态的外观和运动学信息。基于此表征，在包含人类和机器人交互视频的大规模数据上训练视频生成模型，条件为 Contact Flow。生成的视频由视觉语言模型评估其物理合理性，形成"提议-想象-验证-执行"的规划管线。

Result: 在 DROID 数据集和真实桌面操作任务上验证，Contact Flow 能够实现人类演示与不同机器人形态之间的迁移，生成物理合理的操作结果视频。

Conclusion: Contact Flow 提供了一种有效的跨形态动作表征，使视频世界模型能够从异构数据源（人类+多种机器人）中学习，为机器人规划中的"想象与验证"提供了可行的方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Contact Flow，一种与具体具身形态无关的动作表征，用于训练视频生成式世界模型，预测物理合理的操作结果。这属于视频世界模型（video_world_model）的典型工作，即动作条件视频生成与未来预测。

Importance reason: Embodiment-agnostic action representation for video world models aligns with user's high-priority world model and continuous multimodal subtopics. Signals: world model, embodiment-agnostic, video generation, manipulation, contact flow

Abstract: World models offer a promising route toward robot planning by enabling agents to imagine and verify the consequences of actions before execution. However, current video-based world models often struggle to capture the physical constraints that govern manipulation, particularly contact. Further, their action conditioning is often constrained to specific embodiments such as parallel grippers. We propose \emph{Contact Flow}, an embodiment-agnostic action representation that encodes manipulation through the trajectory of 3D contact points between an actor and a target object. By discarding actor-specific appearance and kinematics, Contact Flow provides a shared conditioning signal for both human demonstrations and robotic execution. Therefore, we can train a large-scale video generative model on both human and robotic object interaction videos conditioned on Contact Flow, yielding a world model that predicts physically plausible manipulation outcomes. We integrate this model into a propose-imagine-verify-act pipeline, where generated rollouts are assessed by a vision-language model before execution. Experiments on the DROID dataset and real-world tabletop manipulation tasks demonstrate that Contact Flow enables transfer between human demonstrations and different robotic embodiments.

</details>


### [19] [StatePlay: State-Aware Game World Models for Mechanics-Consistent Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26754)
*Zijun Lin, Zeqing Wang, Cheston Tan, Bihan Wen, Yeying Jin*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 视频世界模型

Importance: 高优先级 (93.65) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: StatePlay 是一种状态感知的游戏世界模型，采用 MoT 架构联合建模视觉内容与游戏内部状态（如血量、技能条），在生成游戏视频时保持机制一致性，状态预测误差低于 0.06，机制保真度提升 18.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有游戏世界模型仅关注像素级视觉生成，忽略了游戏由状态依赖规则（如血量减少、技能激活、游戏终止）驱动的本质，导致生成的游戏画面视觉合理但违反底层游戏机制。因此需要显式建模游戏状态以实现机制一致的生成。

Method: 提出 StatePlay，采用 Mixture-of-Transformers (MoT) 风格架构，包含视觉分支和状态分支，分别处理视觉内容与游戏状态，同时通过跨模态交互使预测的状态指导帧生成。每个分支使用适合其模态的独立优化目标进行训练。

Result: 状态预测的平均归一化 L1 距离低于 0.06；相比无显式状态建模的模型，生成游戏 rollouts 的机制保真度提升 18.6%。

Conclusion: StatePlay 证明了状态感知的游戏世界建模的重要性，超越了像素级真实感，向完整且机制忠实的游戏生成迈进了重要一步。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 StatePlay，一种状态感知的游戏世界模型，联合预测视觉内容与游戏状态以生成符合游戏机制的交互式视频。这属于动作条件视频生成与未来预测，与 [CANONICAL] 视频世界模型 (video_world_model) 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly advances world model research with state-aware generation, a high-priority subtopic. Signals: world model, game world model, state-aware generation, mechanics consistency, mixture-of-transformers

Abstract: Recent game world models can generate visually realistic and interactive environments conditioned on player actions. However, games are not defined by pixels alone; they are governed by explicit mechanics, namely state-dependent rules that control health reduction, skill activation, and game termination. These mechanics depend on precise internal states, such as health points, skill meters, and timers, which are tightly coupled with visual observations and determine how gameplay evolves. Without modeling these state dynamics, existing game world models may generate visually plausible rollouts but violate the underlying game rules. In this paper, we propose StatePlay, a novel state-aware game world model that jointly predicts visual content and game states to promote mechanics-consistent generation. StatePlay adopts a mixture-of-transformers (MoT)-style architecture that preserves specialized visual and state representations while enabling cross-modal interaction, allowing predicted states to guide frame generation. Each branch is further optimized with a distinct objective suited to its modality. Experiments show that StatePlay achieves an average normalized L1 distance below 0.06 for state prediction. Furthermore, compared with models without explicit state modeling, our method improves mechanics fidelity in generated game rollouts by 18.6%. Overall, our work highlights the importance of state-aware game world modeling and advances beyond pixel-level realism toward complete and mechanically faithful game generation.

</details>


### [20] [Mitigating Compounding Error via Video Representation Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27036)
*Taiye Chen, Qi Zhang, Yisen Wang*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.CV

Direction: 世界模型

Subtopic: 视频世界模型

Importance: 高优先级 (96.67) - Top 1 精读候选

TL;DR: 本文发现视频扩散世界模型在自回归长视频生成中的误差累积与隐藏表征的维度坍塌（有效秩下降）密切相关，并提出视频表征正则化（Video Representation Regularization）这一轻量级训练约束来稳定潜空间表征、抑制迭代误差累积，在VBench指标上显著超越Diffusion Forcing。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频扩散世界模型在滑动窗口自回归推理中面临严重的误差累积问题，导致长视频生成质量随时间急剧下降。尽管该现象被广泛观察，但其内在机制和稳定长时序生成的方法仍不清楚。本文旨在揭示误差累积与模型内部表征退化之间的关联，并提出有效的缓解策略。

Method: 1) 通过分析视频世界模型的隐藏表征动力学，发现误差累积与表征的维度坍塌（dimensional collapse）紧密耦合——有效秩（effective rank）在生成漂移开始时急剧下降。2) 发现单纯扩大训练数据无法提升模型抗误差漂移能力，挑战了主流scaling范式。3) 提出视频表征正则化（Video Representation Regularization），一种轻量级训练约束，通过稳定潜空间表征来抑制迭代误差累积。

Result: 在VBench的Aesthetic Quality指标上从38.65提升至55.56，Imaging Quality指标上从44.37提升至72.08，显著优于Diffusion Forcing基线方法。

Conclusion: 本文首次建立了自回归视频漂移与模型内部表征之间的关联，将有效秩（erank）作为误差累积的量化指标，揭示了视频世界模型在scaling上的反直觉局限性，并提出了一种简单有效的正则化策略来提升长视频生成的鲁棒性。

Classification reason: 论文研究视频扩散世界模型在自回归长视频生成中的误差累积问题，属于视频世界模型（video_world_model）范畴，该子主题是[CANONICAL]条目，直接复用。

Importance reason: Directly addresses error accumulation in autoregressive video world models, a core challenge for the user's high-priority video generation and world model subtopics. Signals: video world model, autoregressive video generation, error accumulation, representation regularization, dimensional collapse

Abstract: Video diffusion-based world models enable long autoregressive video generation for robotics, autonomous driving and simulation tasks, yet sliding-window autoregressive inference suffers from severe error accumulation that degrades frame quality over time. Although this phenomenon has been widely observed, the underlying mechanism of compounding error and how to achieve stable long-horizon generation remain largely unresolved. In this paper, we investigate the internal representation dynamics of video world models and discover that compounding error is tightly coupled with dimensional collapse of hidden representations. Specifically, the effective rank of model representations sharply decreases at the onset of generation drift, revealing a strong connection between representational degradation and long-term rollout instability. Furthermore, we find that pure training data scaling fails to boost model resistance to error drift, contradicting mainstream scaling paradigms. To address this problem, we propose video representation regularization, a lightweight training constraint that stabilizes latent representations and suppresses iterative error accumulation. Compared with Diffusion Forcing, our method achieves improvements from 38.65 to 55.56 and from 44.37 to 72.08 on the Aesthetic Quality and Imaging Quality metrics of VBench. Our work establishes the first connection between autoregressive video drifting and model internal representations, adopts erank as a quantitative metric for error accumulation, reveals counterintuitive scaling limitations for video world models, and presents a simple yet effective regularization strategy to improve long video generation robustness.

</details>

### [21] [Genie Sim PanoWorld: An Infinite Indoor 3D World Generation Pipeline via Panoramic Scene Modeling and Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26646)
*Yongxin Su, Linjie Hou, Feng Wang, Jialin Tang, Zhijun Li, Qian Wang, Maoqing Yao*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 3D 生成与重建

Importance: 高优先级 (86.93) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Genie Sim PanoWorld 提出一种两阶段前馈流水线，从单张360°全景图生成高保真、可自由导航的3D场景。第一阶段通过NavMesh规划的SE(3)轨迹注入潜视频扩散模型，生成轨迹可控的全景视频；第二阶段用前馈全景重建器将视频提升为3D高斯场景，支持实时自由视角漫游，无需逐场景优化或多视角输入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有从单张全景图重建3D场景的方法要么缺乏度量轨迹控制导致下游3D重建不可靠，要么在长距离相机运动下难以处理大范围遮挡且需要高端多GPU资源。本文旨在实现无需逐场景优化、无需多视角输入、支持自由导航的高保真3D场景重建。

Method: 两阶段流水线：(1) 全景视频生成阶段：NavMesh规划SE(3)漫游轨迹，通过密集几何变形条件注入潜视频扩散模型；采用长短轨迹混合训练和基于shortcut模型的自一致性目标，仅需4步无CFG去噪即可生成高保真全景视频。(2) 3D重建阶段：前馈全景重建器将生成视频提升为3D高斯场景，支持实时自由视角漫游。

Result: 在几何条件全景视频生成和下游3D重建任务上均优于基线方法，且能零样本泛化到未见过的室内场景。

Conclusion: Genie Sim PanoWorld 通过生成-重建两阶段流水线，从单张全景图高效构建可导航3D场景，生成的3D高斯资产可直接用于具身AI模拟，为室内场景的快速3D内容创建提供了实用方案。

Classification reason: 该论文从单张360°全景图生成可自由导航的3D场景，核心是3D场景生成与重建（3DGS），属于[多模态生成]方向下的[3D 生成与重建]（CANONICAL）子主题。同时，生成的3D场景可直接用于具身AI模拟，也与[世界模型]方向相关。

Importance reason: Strong 3D world generation pipeline but only tangential to your core focus on continuous language/multimodal models and autoregressive video generation. Signals: 3D scene generation from single panorama, trajectory-controllable video diffusion, feed-forward 3D Gaussian reconstruction, zero-shot generalization to unseen indoor scenes, simulation-ready for embodied AI

Abstract: We address the problem of reconstructing a high-fidelity, freely navigable 3D scene from a single $360^\circ$ panorama, without per-scene optimization or multi-view capture. Existing methods either lack metric trajectory control, which hinders reliable downstream 3D reconstruction, or struggle with large disocclusions under long-range camera motion while requiring high-end multi-GPU present Genie Sim PanoWorld, a two-stage feed-forward pipeline that bridges generation and reconstruction via an explicit, trajectory-controllable panoramic video. A NavMesh-planned $\mathrm{SE}(3)$ roaming trajectory is injected into a latent video diffusion model through dense geometry-warped conditioning; long--short trajectory mixed training and a self-consistency objective based on shortcut models together yield high-fidelity video in four CFG-free denoising steps. A feed-forward panoramic reconstructor then lifts the generated video into a high-fidelity 3D Gaussian scene that supports real-time, free-viewpoint roaming and can be directly used as a simulation-ready asset for embodied AI applications. Experiments show that Genie Sim PanoWorld outperforms geometry-conditioned baselines in both panoramic video generation and downstream 3D reconstruction, while generalizing zero-shot to unseen indoor scenes.

</details>


### [22] [CinemaTraj: Composing Atomic Camera Trajectories for 3D Scenes with LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26910)
*Qianru Li, Xuyang Chen, Erkin Türköz, Lu Liu, Xuqin Wang, Liqiu Meng, Tao Wu, Yanfeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 3D 生成与重建

Importance: 普通相关 (47.99) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: CinemaTraj 是一个利用 LLM agent 和 3D 场景图，将自然语言描述分解为原子摄影运动序列，从而在 3D 场景中自动生成电影级相机轨迹的框架，同时支持同步旁白和字幕生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从自然语言描述自动生成具有电影表现力的相机轨迹具有很高的实用价值（如房产广告、虚拟导览），但现有方法要么缺乏真正的 3D 空间感知（依赖 2D 图像先验），要么将轨迹生成视为脱离电影语义的几何路径规划问题。

Method: CinemaTraj 将相机轨迹规划重构为语言驱动的空间推理问题。给定 RGB-D 图像和用户提示，框架为 LLM agent 配备结构化的 3D 场景图；agent 将提示分解为一系列原子摄影运动（dolly、orbit、crane、pan、tilt、zoom、arc），每种运动通过一种新颖的参数化轨迹表示实例化，该表示兼具电影表现力和碰撞避免可优化性。场景图作为结构化空间先验，将 agent 的推理锚定在环境的精确几何和语义知识中。此外，CinemaTraj 还生成与相机运动同步的旁白和字幕，输出带旁白的电影级视频。

Result: 在真实世界的 ScanNet++ 场景上评估，CinemaTraj 生成的轨迹具有提示忠实性、无碰撞和高电影质量，在提示对齐、轨迹质量和安全性指标上优于现有方法。

Conclusion: CinemaTraj 通过将 LLM agent 与 3D 场景图结合，成功实现了从自然语言到电影级相机轨迹的自动生成，验证了语言驱动的空间推理在 3D 场景轨迹规划中的有效性。

Classification reason: 该论文关注从自然语言描述自动生成3D场景中的相机轨迹，属于3D场景相关的生成任务，与[3D 生成与重建]（three_d_generation）方向高度相关。

Importance reason: Focuses on 3D camera trajectory generation with LLM agents, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and lacks alignment with key continuous multimodal or video generation directions. Signals: 3D scene graph, LLM agent, camera trajectory, cinematographic movement, ScanNet++ evaluation

Abstract: Automatically generating cinematically expressive camera trajectories through 3D scenes from natural language descriptions is a challenging task of high practical value, with applications ranging from real-estate advertising to virtual tour creation. Existing methods either lack true 3D spatial awareness by relying on 2D image priors, or treat trajectory generation as a geometric path planning problem divorced from cinematographic semantics. We present CinemaTraj, a framework that reframes camera trajectory planning as a language-grounded spatial reasoning problem. Given a set of RGB-D images and a user prompt, CinemaTraj equips an LLM agent with a structured 3D scene graph: the agent decomposes the prompt into a sequence of atomic cinematographic movements (dolly, orbit, crane, pan, tilt, zoom, arc). Each movement is instantiated via a novel parametric trajectory representation that is both cinematographically expressive and optimizable for collision avoidance. The scene graph acts as a structured spatial prior, grounding the agent's reasoning in accurate geometric and semantic knowledge of the environment. CinemaTraj further generates synchronized voiceover and subtitles aligned with camera motion, producing narrated cinematic video outputs. We evaluate CinemaTraj on real-world ScanNet++ environments, and show that it produces prompt-faithful, collision-free trajectories with high cinematographic quality, outperforming existing approaches on prompt alignment, trajectory quality, and safety metrics.

</details>

TL;DR: ScalablePromptus 提出了一种可扩展的基于提示的视频流传输框架，通过语义与颜色感知的提示反转、球面线性插值以及 dropout 训练策略，生成有序的提示表示，使得接收端即使收到任意截断的提示也能重建出有意义的视频，在丢包网络条件下将质量退化降低 82%-95%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 Promptus 框架在传输紧凑语义提示进行视频重建时，对网络波动非常敏感——部分提示丢失会导致重建质量灾难性下降。作者希望解决这一鲁棒性问题，使基于提示的视频流传输能够在真实网络环境中可靠部署。

Method: 1) 语义与颜色感知的提示反转（Semantic and Color-aware Prompt Inversion）；2) 球面线性插值（Spherical Linear Interpolation）用于中间帧生成；3) 核心创新：dropout 训练策略，使提示表示具有秩排序特性（rank-ordered），从而接收端可以从任意截断的提示中重建视频，无需任何适配。

Result: 在稳定网络下，ScalablePromptus 相比基线有适度质量提升；在丢包条件下，将截断导致的质量退化降低 82%-95%，使基于提示的流传输具备实际部署的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: ScalablePromptus 通过 dropout 训练策略有效解决了提示流传输中网络波动导致的质量崩溃问题，大幅提升了鲁棒性，为超低码率视频通信的实际部署提供了可行方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究基于提示（prompt）的视频流传输与生成式重建，属于视频内容的高效传输与重建范畴，与视频编辑/重建方向相关。虽然核心是视频流传输，但其方法涉及视频帧的生成式重建，可归入多模态生成下的"图像与视频编辑"（visual_editing）这一规范主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on video streaming robustness, not aligned with user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or autoregressive generation. Signals: video streaming, prompt-based, dropout training, network loss, not autoregressive

Abstract: Prompt-based video streaming transmits compact semantic prompts instead of pixel-level content for generative reconstruction, enabling ultra-low-bitrate communication. However, the state-of-the-art Promptus framework is vulnerable to network fluctuation, where partially received prompts lead to catastrophic quality collapse. We propose ScalablePromptus, which enhances Promptus with semantic and color-aware prompt inversion, spherical linear interpolation for intermediate frames, and--most critically--a dropout training strategy that produces rank-ordered prompt representations. This allows the receiver to reconstruct meaningful video from arbitrarily truncated prompts without any adaptation. Under stable networks, ScalablePromptus achieves modest quality gains. Under lossy conditions, it reduces the performance degradation caused by truncation by 82%-95% compared to the baseline, making prompt-based streaming robust enough for real-world deployment.

</details>

*Xiaolong Liu, Junjian Li, Yuan Xiao, Jiaqi Deng, Dayong Ye, Tianqing Zhu, Huan Huo*

Main category: cs.MM, cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态生成

TL;DR: 本文提出 Dualin（双重反演），一种两阶段方法，同时恢复目标图像的语义提示词和潜空间噪声，从而在不需重新优化的前提下实现高保真图像生成与灵活可控的图像编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有提示词反演方法（梯度法不稳定且产生伪影，无梯度法缺乏细粒度细节对齐）仅关注提示词反演而忽略了编码结构信息的潜噪声，导致生成图像保真度不足。本文旨在同时恢复语义提示词和潜噪声以提升图像保真度和编辑灵活性。

Method: Dualin 包含两阶段：第一阶段，集成视觉语言模型 CLIP 和大语言模型，将目标图像反演为忠实、可解释的硬提示词；第二阶段，使用无条件 DDIM 反演重建目标图像的精确潜噪声，保证结构信息一致性。理论上证明了反演得到的噪声无需重新优化即可实现灵活的图像编辑。

Result: 在多个数据集上的实验表明，Dualin 同时生成了高质量的反演提示词，并在图像保真度上达到了最先进水平。此外，Dualin 为精确可控的图像编辑建立了稳健的基础。

Conclusion: 本文证明了同时恢复提示词和潜噪声对于文生图扩散模型反演的重要性，Dualin 在图像生成保真度和编辑灵活性方面均优于现有方法，为可控图像编辑提供了新的范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Dualin 方法，通过 prompt 和噪声双重反演实现文生图扩散模型的高保真图像编辑。核心应用是精确可控的图像编辑，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "图像与视频编辑" (visual_editing)。

Importance reason: Focuses on image editing inversion, not aligned with user's priority on continuous multimodal models or autoregressive generation. Signals: diffusion inversion, image editing, prompt inversion, CLIP, DDIM inversion

Abstract: Prompt inversion, as a typical reverse engineering technique, enables text-to-image (T2I) diffusion models to generate the desired target images without extensive prompt engineering. However, existing prompt inversion methods suffer from significant limitations: (1) gradient-based methods are unstable and uninterpretable, often resulting in generated images with severe artifacts; (2) gradient-free methods yield human-readable prompts but still fail to preserve visual fidelity due to the lack of fine-grained detail alignment. We contend that the limitations stem from treating prompt inversion as a sufficient condition for reverse engineering, ignoring the critical role of the latent noise that encodes structural information. Consequently, we propose Dualin (Dual inversion), a two-stage method that jointly recovers both the semantic prompt and latent noise of the target image. In the first stage, we integrate vision-language model, CLIP and large language model to invert a faithful, human-interpretable hard prompt. In the second stage, unconditional DDIM inversion reconstructs the exact latent noise of the target image, guaranteeing the consistency at the structural information level. Theoretically, we prove that the inverted noise enables flexible image editing without re-optimization. Extensive experiments on diverse datasets demonstrate that Dualin simultaneously generates high-quality inverted prompts and achieves state-of-the-art image fidelity. Additionally, Dualin can establish a robust foundation for the precise and controllable image editing.

</details>

*Jiamin Xu, Cong Wang, Zheng Dong, Chi Wang, Renshu Gu, Weiwei Xu, Gang Xu*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 野外视频阴影去除因复杂光照、多样阴影外观和有限训练数据而极具挑战性，现有方法在非约束真实场景中效果有限，亟需一种鲁棒且能保持细节的解决方案。

Method: 基于预训练视频扩散模型，通过 LoRA 进行微调；在冻结的 VAE 解码器中加入细节注入模块以保留图像细节；引入阴影掩码引导的频率分解调制模块，选择性恢复高频纹理并抑制阴影伪影；利用 Depth Anything 3 的单目深度先验提供几何感知引导；同时构建了大规模配对视频阴影去除数据集 WildShadow。

Result: 在多个场景上的大量实验表明，该方法在阴影去除质量和时序一致性上优于现有方法，能生成时序连贯的无阴影视频，具有优越的视觉质量和强泛化能力。

Conclusion: WildShadowRemover 有效解决了野外视频阴影去除难题，通过结合视频扩散模型、细节保持模块和深度先验，实现了高质量、时序一致的阴影去除，且泛化能力强。

Classification reason: 该论文研究视频阴影去除（video shadow removal），属于视频编辑任务。现有规范子主题"图像与视频编辑"（visual_editing）明确涵盖视频编辑、可控编辑等方向，因此直接复用该规范子主题。

Importance reason: Video shadow removal is a niche editing task with no direct alignment to priority subtopics like continuous language models or autoregressive generation. Signals: video diffusion, shadow removal, detail preservation, LoRA fine-tuning, dataset contribution

Abstract: Video shadow removal in the wild remains challenging due to complex illumination, diverse shadow appearances, and limited training data. Despite its importance to numerous vision and graphics applications, it remains largely unexplored in unconstrained real-world scenarios. To address this gap, we present WildShadowRemover, a framework that adapts a pretrained video diffusion model for robust video shadow removal via LoRA fine-tuning. To preserve fine image details while retaining the model's powerful generative prior, we augment the frozen VAE decoder with a detail injection module and introduce a shadow-mask-guided frequency-decomposed modulation module to selectively restore high-frequency textures while suppressing shadow artifacts. Monocular depth priors from Depth Anything 3 further provide geometry-aware guidance under challenging lighting conditions. We also construct WildShadow, a large-scale paired video shadow removal dataset and benchmark, covering diverse synthetic scenes. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method outperforms existing approaches in shadow removal quality and temporal consistency, producing temporally coherent shadow-free videos with superior visual quality and strong generalization across challenging in-the-wild scenarios.

</details>

*Bowen Chen, Shreshth Saini, Balu Adsumilli, Alan C. Bovik*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LumaGuide 提出一种无需训练的扩散模型分布塑造框架，通过在采样过程中施加可微能量引导，控制输出图像的亮度分布（在感知均匀的PQ空间中），从而实现高动态范围（HDR）图像生成。该方法无需修改模型参数，仅通过对齐亮度直方图即可诱导出HDR一致的行为（高光细节、阴影保留），并支持数据驱动预设、参考图像或文本驱动的目标分布指定，还可扩展到视频生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练扩散模型受限于训练数据的统计偏差，难以生成高动态范围（HDR）内容。现有HDR生成方法通常需要重新训练或微调模型，成本高昂。作者希望在不修改模型参数的前提下，通过引导采样过程来突破训练数据的分布限制，实现可控的HDR生成。

Method: 提出LumaGuide框架，核心思想是在扩散采样过程中通过可微能量引导（differentiable energy-based guidance）来塑造输出分布。具体实现上，将亮度分布控制转化为在感知均匀的PQ空间中对亮度直方图的对齐问题，通过梯度引导采样过程向目标亮度分布靠近。该方法支持多种目标分布指定方式：数据驱动预设、参考图像直方图、或文本驱动的亮度预测器。对于视频生成，还引入了时序一致性约束。

Result: 实验表明，仅通过对齐亮度直方图即可有效诱导HDR一致的行为，包括生成合理的高光细节和保留阴影细节，同时保持语义保真度。该方法在图像和视频生成任务上均取得了良好的HDR效果，且无需重新训练模型。

Conclusion: LumaGuide证明了可控生成可以通过在采样时直接塑造输出分布来实现，无需重新训练扩散模型。该框架不仅适用于HDR生成，还可推广到更广泛的分布控制场景，为扩散模型的可控生成提供了一种新的范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出LumaGuide，一种无需训练的扩散模型采样引导框架，通过控制亮度分布实现HDR图像生成。这属于扩散模型的可控生成/编辑范畴，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "图像与视频编辑"（visual_editing）中的可控编辑方向高度吻合。论文也提到该方法可自然扩展到视频生成，进一步支持该分类。

Importance reason: Training-free HDR generation via distribution shaping is technically solid but falls outside your priority subtopics and negative keywords. Signals: training-free diffusion guidance, HDR image generation, luminance distribution shaping, PQ space, video extension

Abstract: Pretrained diffusion models generate realistic images but are constrained by the statistical biases of their training data, limiting their ability to produce high dynamic range (HDR) content. In this work, we introduce LumaGuide, a training-free framework for distribution shaping in diffusion models. Instead of modifying model parameters, LumaGuide steers the sampling process to match target feature distributions via differentiable energy-based guidance. We instantiate this framework for HDR generation by controlling luminance distributions in perceptually uniform PQ space. Our results show that aligning luminance histograms is sufficient to induce HDR-consistent behavior, including coherent highlights and preserved shadow detail, while maintaining semantic fidelity. Beyond HDR, LumaGuide enables flexible specification of target distributions through data-driven presets, reference images, or text-driven predictors, and extends naturally to video generation with temporal consistency constraints. More broadly, our work demonstrates that controllable generation can be achieved by directly shaping output distributions at sampling time, without retraining diffusion models.

</details>

*Yinan Wang, Yan Huang, Yong Xu, Patrick Le Callet*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 图像与视频编辑

TL;DR: FreeShadow 是一种无需训练的无监督阴影去除方法，利用预训练扩散模型的先验知识，通过光照转移注意力（ITA）机制将非阴影区域的光照信息迁移到阴影区域，并选择性保留光照不变的内容特征以保持图像保真度，同时提出局部纹理保持重光照（LTPR）缓解 VAE 压缩导致的纹理错位问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有有监督和无监督阴影去除方法因训练数据多样性不足而泛化能力有限，零样本方法则容易产生伪影且需要耗时的测试时优化。作者希望利用预训练扩散模型的强大先验，实现无需任何训练或优化的阴影去除方法。

Method: 提出 FreeShadow，包含三个核心组件：(1) 光照转移注意力（ITA），通过重新加权扩散模型的自注意力图，将非阴影区域的光照线索转移到阴影区域；(2) 选择性内容保留，分析光照变化对自注意力图和潜在高频特征的影响，保留光照不变成分以维持内容保真度并抑制残留阴影；(3) 局部纹理保持重光照（LTPR），缓解 VAE 压缩导致的局部纹理错位。

Result: 大量实验表明，FreeShadow 在多个阴影去除基准上取得了强泛化能力，生成逼真的无阴影图像，无需训练或测试时优化即可达到与有监督方法竞争的性能。

Conclusion: FreeShadow 证明了预训练扩散模型可以有效地用于训练无关的阴影去除任务，通过光照转移和选择性内容保留策略，在无需额外训练的情况下实现了良好的泛化性和图像质量。

Classification reason: 该论文研究基于扩散模型的阴影去除（shadow removal），属于图像编辑任务。阴影去除是图像编辑的一个子方向，与 canonical 子主题 "visual_editing（图像与视频编辑）" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Training-free shadow removal is a niche application, not aligned with user's priority on autoregressive generation or continuous multimodal models. Signals: training-free, shadow removal, diffusion model, illumination transfer, content preservation

Abstract: Existing supervised and unsupervised shadow removal methods often suffer from limited generalization due to the insufficient diversity of available training datasets, while zero-shot methods tend to produce artifacts and require time-consuming test-time optimization. To address these issues, we propose FreeShadow, a training-free shadow removal method built upon pretrained diffusion models, which exploits diffusion priors for shadow removal without any training or optimization. For illumination recovery, we propose an illumination transfer attention (ITA), which re-weights the self-attention maps in diffusion model to transfer illumination cues from non-shadow to shadow regions. For content preservation, we analyze the effects of illumination variations on self-attention maps and latent high-frequency features in diffusion model, and selectively preserve illumination-invariant components to maintain content fidelity while suppressing residual shadows. We further propose local texture-preserving relighting (LTPR) to mitigate local texture misalignment caused by VAE compression. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method achieves strong generalization and produces realistic shadow-free images.

</details>
