### [28] [MoSAIC: Aligned Intervention Supervision for Part-Local Motion Style Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26304)
*Nazanin Amini, Kevin Desai*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 数字人与动作生成

Importance: 值得关注 (72.04) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: MoSAIC 提出了一种基于潜扩散框架的部分局部参考条件动作风格迁移方法，通过对齐干预监督（aligned intervention supervision）构造同步参考与反事实目标，实现对人体不同部位的选择性动作风格迁移，在保持未选中区域的同时有效迁移参考风格。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动作数据集缺乏针对任意局部-参考组合的配对目标，自重建训练可能导致扩散模型在利用参考信息时不足，难以实现精细的局部动作风格迁移。

Method: 提出 MoSAIC 潜扩散框架，按解剖区域分解内容与参考特征，通过独立条件路径保留根轨迹，将用户选择的参考路由到特定身体部位。核心贡献是对齐干预监督（aligned intervention supervision），通过受控局部变换构造同步参考与反事实目标，使训练中可直接观察到请求的区域响应和需保留的动作。

Result: 在包含128个动作和896个路由条件的冻结评估中，部分掩码路由相比全身路由将保留区域误差从70.64降至66.45mm，匹配噪声离域泄漏从18.08降至9.88mm，同时保持正向的选中区域响应。消融实验表明保留对齐干预监督使选中目标响应提升8.8%，请求路由影响集中度提升2.0个百分点。

Conclusion: MoSAIC 有效改善了选择性可控局部动作编辑所需的响应-保留权衡，为精细化的部分局部动作风格迁移提供了可行方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究人体动作风格迁移（motion style transfer），属于人体动作生成范畴，与 [CANONICAL] 子主题 "数字人与动作生成"（avatar_motion_generation）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on motion style transfer, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks alignment with user's key interests in continuous models, video generation, or world models. Signals: motion style transfer, latent diffusion, part-local editing, aligned intervention supervision, cs.CV

Abstract: Editing character motion often requires transferring a gesture or gait from one or more reference motions while preserving the source action, timing, root trajectory, and unselected body regions. Existing motion datasets, however, rarely provide paired targets for arbitrary part-local content--reference combinations, and self-reconstruction training may allow a diffusion model to reproduce the content motion while underusing the routed reference. We present MoSAIC, a latent diffusion framework for part-local reference-conditioned motion style transfer. MoSAIC factorizes content and reference features by anatomical region, preserves the root trajectory through a separate conditioning pathway, and routes user-selected references to individual body parts. Its central contribution is aligned intervention supervision, which constructs synchronized references and counterfactual targets through controlled local transformations, making both the requested regional response and the motion to be preserved directly observable during training. In a frozen evaluation comprising 128 motions and 896 routed conditions, part-masked routing reduces preserved-region error from 70.64 to 66.45~mm and matched-noise off-target leakage from 18.08 to 9.88~mm relative to whole-body routing, while retaining a positive selected-region response. A matched-budget continuation study further shows that retaining aligned intervention supervision produces an 8.8\% relative increase in selected-target response and a 2.0-percentage-point increase in requested-route influence concentration. These results demonstrate that MoSAIC improves the response--preservation trade-off required for selective and controllable part-local motion editing.

</details>

Method: 提出两阶段生成式扩散模型框架：第一阶段生成全局根轨迹（root trajectory），第二阶段在根轨迹条件下生成完整的四足动物运动序列。采用结构化的条件注入和修补（inpainting）策略，支持多种动作类型，并允许对头部运动和单个肢体进行细粒度控制。模型仅使用四足动物运动数据进行训练。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法在运动真实感和可控性方面优于现有的重定向方法，能够生成自然、多样的四足动物运动，并支持直观的交互控制。

Conclusion: Two2Four 框架为从普通人体运动生成可控、逼真的四足动物运动提供了一种有效的数据驱动方案，在动画和虚拟制作领域具有实用价值。

Classification reason: 该论文研究从人体运动生成四足动物运动（human-to-quadruped puppeteering），属于动作生成与重定向范畴。现有 [CANONICAL] 子主题 "avatar_motion_generation"（数字人与动作生成）涵盖虚拟人、人体动作、面部重演等，虽然论文输出是四足动物而非人体，但其核心是动作生成与重定向（retargeting），与该子主题高度相关，因此复用该子主题。

Abstract: Realistic animal motion for virtual production is typically obtained either through motion capture of highly trained performers who accurately mimic animal behavior, or by retargeting ordinary human motion using complex control setups. Both approaches are challenging and often fail to fully reproduce the nuances of natural animal motion, motivating data-driven alternatives. We present an automatic human-to-quadruped puppeteering framework that produces plausible and controllable quadruped motions from ordinary human motion data. Our approach employs a two-stage generative diffusion model trained purely on quadruped motion data. By introducing a structured conditioning and inpainting strategy, our method supports a wide range of actions, including walking, running, jumping, sitting, and lying. Furthermore, we enable fine-grained intuitive control of the quadruped motion such as head movement control and individual limb puppeteering. Experimental results demonstrate improved motion realism and controllability compared to existing retargeting approaches, highlighting the effectiveness of our framework as a tool for animation and virtual production applications.

Result: 实验表明 CineWeaver 能生成高质量的长时长电影级视频，具有一致的身份特征、稳定的全局外观和清晰的镜头切换，在多项指标上优于现有方法。

Conclusion: CineWeaver 是首个同时实现长视频、参考可控和多镜头生成的统一无训练框架，为电影级视频生成提供了一种高效且实用的解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 CineWeaver，基于预训练文生视频扩散模型实现无需训练的多镜头长视频生成，属于文本到视频生成范畴，与 [CANONICAL] 文生视频与视频扩散 高度匹配。

Signals: training-free video generation, multi-shot long video, reference controllability, pretrained video diffusion, cinematic storytelling

Abstract: Cinematic video generation is challenging for text-to-video diffusion models due to concurrent requirements on multi-shot generation, fine-grained controllability over characters and scenes, and long-form generation across extended temporal horizons. Existing methods rely on customization and retraining to separately address specific requirements, and cannot simultaneously fulfill all the requirements with a unified framework. In this paper, we shed light on the training-free paradigm with the key insight that the difficulty of multi-shot generation arises from a structural bias toward temporal continuity in pretrained video diffusion models, and consequently, propose a unified framework named CineWeaver to achieve reference-controllable multi-shot long-video generation without retraining. We manipulate positional encoding and attention patterns to break temporal continuity during inference to enable clear shot transitions using pretrained video diffusion models. Furthermore, we extend the proposed framework with a shot-routed reference conditioning mechanism for per-shot fine-grained controllability, and develop an anchor memory mechanism to allow long-form generation with consistent global appearance cues. To our best knowledge, CineWeaver is the first unified framework to simultaneously enable \textbf{long-form}, \textbf{reference-controllable}, and \textbf{multi-shot} video generation in a training-free fashion. Experimental results demonstrate that CineWeaver produces high-quality cinematic videos of long durations with consistent identities, stable global appearance, and clear shot transitions. The project page is available at: .

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 在长视频 Arena 对比评测中，Visko Orbis 1.0 在整体偏好和时序稳定性评分上均优于现有的实时交互式视频生成系统。系统能够生成长达小时级、24FPS 的 4K 视频，且无明显质量或颜色漂移。

Conclusion: Visko Orbis 1.0 证明了实时交互式长视频生成的可行性，通过流式生成架构和多尺度记忆机制有效解决了长视频一致性和实时交互的挑战，为视频生成领域提供了新的范式。

Abstract: We present Visko Orbis 1.0, a Live Model for real-time, interactive long-video generation. Users can change the prompt at any moment during generation, and the update becomes visible in real time. Visko Orbis 1.0 supports long-form text-to-video, image-to-video, and video continuation, with multilingual prompts and prompt switching while generation is in progress. A bounded multi-scale memory preserves subjects, scenes, and style across chunks, sustaining hour-scale rollouts without evident quality or color drift. Built on a distilled chunk-wise streaming generator and a streaming video upscaler, Visko Orbis 1.0 delivers real-time 4K video generation at 24 FPS using an optimized GPU serving engine. In long-form Arena comparisons, Visko Orbis 1.0 obtains the highest overall-preference and temporal-stability ratings among state-of-the-art real-time interactive video-generation systems.

### [32] [TPD: Temporal Prior Decoupling for Text-to-Video Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26706)
*Taewon Kang, Matthias Zwicker*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 文生视频与视频扩散

Importance: 高优先级 (92.7) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 Temporal Prior Decoupling (TPD)，一种无需训练的框架，通过构建时序反事实并恢复被抑制的后期事件信号，解决文生视频扩散模型中早期场景主导导致后期事件无法生成的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文生视频扩散模型在处理"早期场景持续存在，后期新事件在此基础上发生"的提示时，后期事件往往无法在对应帧中生成。作者将此失败归因于 Temporal Prior Suppression (TPS)：早期片段的强先验主导了跨注意力轨迹，抑制了后期事件所需的引导信号，而现有引导机制无法建模这种竞争关系。

Method: TPD 是一个无需训练的框架，在扩散采样过程中恢复被抑制的后期事件信号。具体地：(1) 构建时序反事实——仅以早期片段为条件生成轨迹；(2) 将完整提示轨迹与反事实轨迹之间的差异定义为被抑制的信号方向；(3) 通过帧选择性的下界约束（lower-bound constraint），在扩散时间步和视频帧上联合优化，恢复被抑制的事件信号到后期帧中，同时保持早期片段的连贯性。该方法完全运行在标准扩散采样流程中，仅基于 classifier-free guidance 空间，与具体骨干网络无关。

Result: 实验表明，TPD 显著提升了后期概念在视频中的实现效果，同时保持了时序连贯性和视觉保真度。该抑制现象在多个不同的文生视频骨干网络中都存在，TPD 均能有效缓解。

Conclusion: TPD 是一种简单有效的训练无关框架，通过时序先验解耦恢复了被抑制的后期事件信号，为文生视频扩散模型中的时序竞争问题提供了新的解决思路。

Classification reason: 该论文提出一种无需训练的框架 TPD，用于解决文生视频扩散模型中后期事件被早期场景主导抑制的问题，属于文本到视频生成方法，与 [CANONICAL] 文生视频与视频扩散 子主题高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly addresses a key failure mode in text-to-video diffusion, a high-priority subtopic, with a training-free method. Signals: text-to-video diffusion, training-free, temporal prior suppression, late-event generation, classifier-free guidance

Abstract: Text-to-video diffusion models generate temporally coherent content from natural language, yet when a prompt describes an early scene that persists while a new event emerges on top of it---such as "a tall sandcastle standing on a beach where a wave rushes in and washes it away"---generation frequently fails to realize the late-segment event in the corresponding frames. We identify this failure as Temporal Prior Suppression (TPS): the dominant prior of the early segment captures the cross-attention trajectory across the temporal axis and suppresses the guidance signal needed for late-segment realization, a competing tendency existing guidance mechanisms do not model. We introduce Temporal Prior Decoupling (TPD), a training-free framework that restores suppressed late-segment signals during diffusion sampling. TPD constructs a temporal counterfactual by conditioning on the early segment alone, and defines the discrepancy between the full-prompt and counterfactual trajectories as a suppressed signal direction. Rather than removing this direction as in prior subtractive projection methods, TPD restores it through a frame-selective lower-bound constraint resolved jointly over diffusion timestep and video frame, realizing the suppressed event in the late frames without disrupting early-segment coherence: where prior work enforces upper-bound feasibility to remove unwanted semantics, TPD enforces lower-bound feasibility to guarantee suppressed-signal contribution. TPD runs entirely within standard diffusion sampling without retraining, and is defined purely in classifier-free guidance space, making it backbone-agnostic by construction. Experiments show that TPD significantly improves late-concept realization while preserving temporal coherence and visual fidelity, and that the targeted suppression recurs across distinct text-to-video backbones.

</details>


### [33] [FreqForcing: Autoregressive Long Video Generation via Spectral Self-Anchoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27110)
*Jiatong Li, Leo Liang, Linghe Kong, Yulun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 文生视频与视频扩散

Importance: 高优先级 (95.24) - Top 2 精读候选

TL;DR: FreqForcing 提出了一种无需训练的频谱自锚定（SSA）框架，通过利用锚点注意力的低频分量维持长程视觉稳定性、局部注意力的高频分量保留动态运动，解决了自回归视频扩散模型在长视频生成中的误差累积问题，实现了从5秒到2分钟的24倍外推。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归视频扩散模型在长视频生成中存在误差累积问题，表现为颜色漂移、运动停滞和视觉崩溃。现有方法（如 attention sink）虽能部分缓解但无法彻底解决，因此需要一种更有效的训练无关方法来稳定长视频生成。

Method: 从频域角度分析误差累积本质是低频段能量漂移，提出频谱自锚定（SSA）方法：利用锚点注意力的低频分量作为"锚"维持视觉稳定性，同时通过局部注意力的高频分量保留动态运动信息，形成 FreqForcing 框架，无需额外训练即可扩展生成时长。

Result: 在 Self-Forcing 预训练模型（5秒片段）上实现24倍外推至2分钟长视频生成，定量和定性结果均优于现有训练无关方法，与需要训练的方法相比也具有竞争力。

Conclusion: FreqForcing 通过频域分析和频谱自锚定有效解决了自回归视频扩散模型的长视频误差累积问题，为训练无关的长视频生成提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文研究自回归视频扩散模型的长视频生成问题，属于视频生成方法，与 [CANONICAL] 文生视频与视频扩散 子主题高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly addresses autoregressive video generation error accumulation with a training-free spectral method, highly aligned with your priority on video generation and model efficiency. Signals: autoregressive video generation, training-free, long video extrapolation, frequency-domain analysis, attention sink

Abstract: Autoregressive video diffusion models enable real-time streaming video generation. However, errors introduced during self-rollout accumulate over long horizons, manifesting as color drift, motion stagnation, and eventual visual collapse. In this paper, we characterize this phenomenon from a frequency-domain perspective: error accumulation appears as a pronounced energy drift in the low-frequency bands. We further investigate the effectiveness of attention sink in the frequency domain, and find that it improves the video quality by alleviating the spectral energy drift to some extent, but cannot fully resolve it. Motivated by the above analysis, we propose FreqForcing, a training-free framework that addresses error accumulation in long-video generation via Spectral Self-Anchoring (SSA). The proposed SSA leverages the low-frequency components of anchor attention to maintain long-horizon visual stability, while preserving dynamic motion through the high-frequency components of local attention. Our FreqForcing extends Self-Forcing pretrained on 5s clips to two-minute generation, achieving 24x extrapolation. Extensive experiments show that FreqForcing outperforms existing training-free methods quantitatively and qualitatively while remaining competitive with representative training-based approaches.

</details>

### [34] [DistillAlign: Coordinating Mode Covering and Mode Seeking in Autoregressive Video Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26811)
*Jiaxing Li, Kai Zou, Cindy Zhou, Kaichen Huang, Junyao Gao, Zile Wang, Yang Liu, Bin Liu, Bo An, Yangguang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态生成

Subtopic: 生成模型加速与高效化

Importance: 值得关注 (66.64) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出DistillAlign，一种面向自回归视频蒸馏的联合蒸馏方法。作者从分布视角重新审视现有DMD多阶段蒸馏流程，发现初始化阶段与DMD阶段追求不同目标分布会导致覆盖度不足。通过引入分布评估协议和联合蒸馏（结合DMD的mode-seeking与一致性蒸馏的mode-covering约束），提升了视频生成的质量、覆盖度和多样性。实验表明，即使使用1.3B的DMD教师，该方法也能超越用14B教师精调的基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自回归视频蒸馏方法采用DMD多阶段流程，但初始化阶段和DMD阶段追求不同的目标分布，导致中间学生模型虽然视觉评分高，但分布覆盖度不足，限制了后续精调的效果。作者希望从分布对齐的角度改进蒸馏流程，使初始化更好地匹配DMD教师的模式覆盖，并解决DMD反向KL目标在后期训练中降低覆盖度和多样性的问题。

Method: 1) 提出分布评估协议，在共享潜空间中测量学生与教师分布之间的精确率（precision）和覆盖度（coverage），揭示视觉评分隐藏的分布差异。2) 提出联合蒸馏（Joint Distillation），将DMD的mode-seeking目标与一致性蒸馏（Consistency Distillation）的mode-covering约束相结合，在保持高质量生成的同时维持分布的覆盖度和多样性。

Result: 实验表明，DistillAlign在生成质量、覆盖度和多样性上均优于现有方法。特别地，使用Wan-1.3B DMD教师蒸馏的学生模型，性能超过了使用Wan-14B教师精调的基线，证明了分布对齐在自回归视频蒸馏中的重要性。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了自回归视频蒸馏中分布对齐的关键作用，指出仅追求视觉评分而忽视分布覆盖度会导致次优结果。提出的联合蒸馏方法有效协调了mode-seeking与mode-covering两种目标，为视频生成模型的蒸馏提供了新的思路。

Classification reason: 该论文研究自回归视频生成模型的蒸馏加速方法（DistillAlign），属于生成模型加速与高效化（efficient_generation）范畴。论文核心是改进DMD蒸馏流程，提升视频生成的质量、覆盖度和多样性，与蒸馏、少步采样等效率优化主题高度吻合。

Importance reason: Directly addresses autoregressive video generation and model efficiency, with strong alignment to priority subtopics and boost keywords. Signals: autoregressive video generation, model efficiency, distillation, mode covering, distribution matching

Abstract: Existing autoregressive video distillation methods commonly adopt a Distribution Matching Distillation (DMD)-based multi-stage pipeline. However, they typically decouple the initialization and DMD stages -- which then pursue different target distributions -- and judge the intermediate student mainly by visual scores such as VBench. In this paper, we revisit this design from a distributional perspective. Given the mode-seeking nature of the distribution matching loss, a good initialization should match the mode coverage of the target DMD teacher, rather than merely pursuing high quality. To analyze this, we introduce a distributional evaluation protocol that measures precision and coverage between student and teacher distributions in a shared latent space. It exposes differences hidden by visual scores: some initializations reach high precision but low coverage, leading to suboptimal refinement, while mode-covering ones preserve broader support. Furthermore, even when the target distributions are aligned, DMD's reverse-KL objective can still drive the student toward high-probability teacher regions in late training, reducing coverage and diversity. To address this, we propose joint distillation, which combines DMD's mode-seeking objective with a Consistency Distillation-based mode-covering constraint. Experiments show that our method improves generation quality, coverage, and diversity; notably, even with a Wan-1.3B DMD teacher, it outperforms baselines refined with Wan-14B, underscoring the importance of distributional alignment in autoregressive video distillation.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文生图扩散模型在处理复杂组合提示时经常出现语义不对齐问题。已有的免训练方法要么只改进初始噪声（搜索成本高或语义注入不可靠），要么只改进去噪轨迹（缺乏及时诊断和纠正语义错误的机制），无法同时兼顾初始化和去噪过程的细粒度控制。

Method: AnchorSteer 包含两个组件：1) Semantic Anchoring：通过 CLIP 提取文本先验，提出 Latent-Prior Score Distillation Sampling (LP-SDS) 目标将 CLIP 视觉先验蒸馏到扩散模型的知识分布中，生成与文本对齐的初始化噪声；2) Reflective Steering：在去噪过程中引入 Think-Erase-Retouch 循环，利用 VLM 诊断语义偏差，然后通过目标潜空间精炼抑制错误内容并恢复缺失属性。

Result: 在 GenEval 和 T2I-CompBench++ 基准上，AnchorSteer 在文本-图像对齐指标上一致优于现有基线方法，同时保持高视觉质量。

Conclusion: 通过同时控制初始化和去噪轨迹，AnchorSteer 有效提升了扩散模型对复杂组合提示的忠实度，为推理时对齐提供了一种无需训练的通用框架。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 AnchorSteer 框架，通过语义锚定（Semantic Anchoring）和反思性引导（Reflective Steering）在推理阶段提升文生图扩散模型的文本-图像对齐忠实度，属于生成模型的对齐与可控性优化，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型对齐与奖励" (generation_alignment)。

Importance reason: Novel training-free inference-time alignment framework with semantic anchoring and reflective steering, but not directly aligned with top-priority continuous multimodal or autoregressive generation subtopics. Signals: training-free inference-time alignment, semantic anchoring via CLIP prior, reflective steering with VLM diagnosis, Think-Erase-Retouch loop, GenEval and T2I-CompBench++ improvements

Abstract: While text-to-image diffusion models achieve impressive visual quality, they frequently struggle to maintain precise alignment with complex compositional prompts. An effective strategy is to improve the inference process of diffusion models, thereby better leveraging their pretrained priors to address misalignment. Existing training-free methods can be divided into two categories. The first category focuses on improving the randomly sampled initial noise, either performing costly search over noise pools or manipulating sampled noise without ensuring reliable semantic injection. The second category focuses on improving the denoising trajectory, lacking explicit mechanisms to timely diagnose and correct semantic errors. we propose \textbf{AnchorSteer}, a training-free framework that exerts fine-grained control over \textbf{both initialization} and \textbf{the denoising trajectory}. AnchorSteer consists of two synergistic components: \textbf{Semantic Anchoring} replaces uninformative Gaussian noise with text-aligned initializations via CLIP-based prior extraction and a novel Latent-Prior Score Distillation Sampling (LP-SDS) objective. Specifically, LP-SDS distills CLIP visual priors into the knowledge distribution of diffusion models, mitigating the domain gap between CLIP-based priors and diffusion-based priors. \textbf{Reflective Steering} transforms passive denoising with an active Think--Erase--Retouch loop that enables mid-generation self-correction. It leverages VLM-based diagnosis to detect semantic deviations and performs targeted latent refinement to suppress erroneous content and recover missing attributes. Extensive experiments on GenEval and T2I-CompBench++ demonstrate that AnchorSteer consistently outperforms existing baselines in text--image alignment while preserving high visual quality.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于反演的水印方法在扩散生成图像认证中面临速度和鲁棒性的双重瓶颈：高NFE反演虽然精度高但速度慢，且现有方法过度优化内部截断误差而忽视了外部失真（如图像编辑、压缩等）对鲁棒性的主导影响。

Method: FARI 框架包含两个核心观察：(1) 反演轨迹曲率远低于前向生成轨迹，因此高度可压缩，适合低NFE近似；(2) 水印验证场景中外部失真主导误差，速度与截断误差的权衡不再关键。基于此，设计了一步反演（one-step inversion）结合轻量级对抗 LoRA 微调去噪器，实现端到端的鲁棒性训练。

Result: 在单张 NVIDIA RTX A6000 GPU 上约20分钟微调后，FARI 在水印验证鲁棒性上超越50步 DDIM 反演，同时推理时间大幅降低。

Conclusion: FARI 证明了在扩散模型水印场景中，通过利用反演轨迹的低曲率特性，一步反演配合对抗微调可以在速度和鲁棒性上同时取得显著提升，为实际部署提供了高效解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出扩散模型的水印反演方法，属于生成模型的可控性与安全性对齐范畴，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型对齐与奖励"（generation_alignment）中的 guidance 与可控性方向高度相关。

Importance reason: Focuses on diffusion model watermarking inversion, which is peripheral to the user's core interests in continuous language models, autoregressive generation, and world models. Signals: diffusion model watermarking, one-step inversion, adversarial LoRA fine-tuning, robustness against distortions, low curvature inversion trajectory

Abstract: Inversion-based watermarking is a promising approach to authenticate diffusion-generated images, yet practical use is bottlenecked by inversion that is both slow and error-prone. While the primary challenge in the watermarking setting is robustness against external distortions, existing approaches over-optimize internal truncation error, and because that error scales with the sampler step size, they are inherently confined to high-NFE (number of function evaluations) regimes that cannot meet the dual demands of speed and robustness. In this work, we have two key observations: (i) the inversion trajectory has markedly lower curvature than the forward generation path does, making it highly compressible and amenable to low-NFE approximation; and (ii) in inversion for watermark verification, the trade-off between speed and truncation error is less critical, since external distortions dominate the error. A faster inverter provides a dual benefit: it is not only more efficient, but it also enables end-to-end adversarial training to directly target robustness, a task that is computationally prohibitive for the original, lengthy inversion trajectories. Building on this, we propose \textbf{FARI} (\textbf{F}ast \textbf{A}symmetric \textbf{R}obust \textbf{I}nversion), a one-step inversion framework paired with lightweight adversarial LoRA fine-tuning of the denoiser for watermark extraction. While consolidation slightly increases internal error, FARI delivers large gains in both speed and robustness: with approximately 20 minutes of fine-tuning on a single NVIDIA RTX A6000 GPU, it surpasses 50-step DDIM inversion on watermark-verification robustness while dramatically reducing inference time. Code and pretrained models are available at .

Motivation: 现有图像取证基准多聚焦于前景物体为中心的篡改（如拼接、复制移动），而背景篡改（篡改证据位于显著前景物体之外）缺乏专门的公开基准。作者旨在填补这一空白，提供一个专注于背景篡改检测与定位的标准化评测平台。

Conclusion: BG-REAL 作为背景篡改检测领域的专用补充基准，揭示了现有方法在背景篡改场景下的局限性，特别是重编码伪影导致的假阳性问题，为未来研究提供了标准化评测平台。

Abstract: Background manipulation is a practical but under-specified image-forensics setting: the manipulated evidence can sit outside the salient foreground object, while many evaluations emphasize object-centric copy-move, splicing, or generic synthetic edits. We introduce BG-REAL, a public real-data anchored benchmark package for background manipulation detection and localization. The current release is built from Open Images V7 instance-segmentation sources and contains 7,000 processed samples over 1,200 source groups, including 6,000 public-data anchored samples and 1,000 synthetic control samples. BG-REAL covers six edit families, matched authentic controls, source-group splits, mask and leakage QA, 599 human-assisted quality-control rows, three completed external baselines (TruFor, MVSS-Net, and HiFi-Net), and five-seed model evaluation. Beyond aggregate accuracy, we use matched-authentic-control diagnostics to measure how often baselines misclassify re-encoded authentic images as manipulated at a threshold fixed on held-out validation data; false-positive rates range from 0.57 (TruFor, the lowest) to 1.00 (several weak or mask-informed baselines), indicating that re-encoding artifacts are a shared shortcut risk across baselines rather than a problem specific to any one model. The release provides the construction pipeline, evaluation protocol, paper-ready figures, and reproduction documentation. We frame BG-REAL as a background-manipulation-focused complement to general image-manipulation-localization benchmarks, not as a fully real-only or general-purpose benchmark.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出了 FakeIDet3-DB，首个基于真实政府签发身份证件的数字篡改数据库，涵盖经典篡改（如复制-移动）和生成式AI篡改（如换脸、修补），并采用隐私感知的补丁提取算法 PACE 在保护个人身份信息的同时最大化取证效用。实验表明现有最先进模型在检测和定位这些篡改时表现不佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 身份证件认证依赖于复杂的高频安全图案的结构完整性，但先进的生成式AI模型可以注入局部高保真篡改，绕过标准验证。训练鲁棒的图像取证模型受隐私法规限制，只能依赖缺乏真实ID复杂视觉图案的合成模板。为弥合这一领域差距，需要基于真实ID的篡改数据库。

Method: 构建 FakeIDet3-DB 数据库，包含真实政府签发ID上的经典篡改（复制-移动）和生成式AI篡改（换脸、修补），并采用图像精炼流程抑制视觉伪影。提出 PACE（Pseudo-Anonymized Contextual patch Extraction）算法，利用积分图映射和距离驱动的非极大值抑制（NMS），在保护PII的同时最大化语义密度，从真实/伪造ID图像中提取约520万补丁。

Result: 从超过6400张真实/伪造ID图像中提取了近520万补丁。使用最先进模型进行评估，结果显示它们在检测和定位生成式及经典篡改攻击时均表现困难：检测EER为32.45%，定位AUC-ROC为83.48%。

Conclusion: FakeIDet3-DB 填补了真实ID篡改检测领域的空白，揭示了现有模型在面对生成式AI驱动的ID篡改时的脆弱性，为未来更鲁棒的ID取证模型研究提供了基准。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 FakeIDet3-DB，一个针对真实身份证件数字篡改（包括经典篡改和生成式AI篡改）的检测数据库，属于生成模型评测与数据集方向，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "生成模型评测与数据集"。

Importance reason: Focuses on ID forgery detection dataset, not aligned with user's priority on continuous multimodal models or video/image generation. Signals: ID forgery detection, dataset contribution, privacy-aware patch extraction, generative AI attacks, not matching priority subtopics

Abstract: Identity document (ID) authentication relies on the structural integrity of complex, high-frequency security patterns. However, advanced Generative AI models can now inject localized, high-fidelity manipulations, creating deceptive attacks that bypass standard verification. Training robust image forensic models to detect these anomalies is hindered by privacy regulations, forcing reliance on synthetic templates lacking the intricate visual patterns of real IDs. To bridge this domain gap, we introduce FakeIDet3-DB, the first comprehensive database of digital manipulations on real, government-issued IDs. FakeIDet3-DB encompasses classical (e.g., copy-move) and Generative AI-driven manipulations (e.g., face-swapping, inpainting) enhanced with advanced image refinement procedures to suppress visual artifacts. In addition, to comply with strict data protection regulations (e.g., GDPR), we adopt a recently-proposed framework based on patches. In order to maximize forensic utility, we formulate privacy-aware patch extraction from a real ID as a geometrically constrained image processing problem. We propose PACE, a Pseudo-Anonymized Contextual patch Extraction algorithm, which leverages Integral Image mapping and distance-driven Non-Maximum Suppression (NMS). PACE efficiently contours anonymization masks that prevent Personally Identifiable Information (PII) leakage while maximizing semantic density in peri-censorship regions, yielding almost 5.2M patches extracted from more than 6.4K images from real/fake IDs. Furthermore, an extensive evaluation of the proposed FakeIDet3-DB is performed using state-of-the-art models, showcasing they all struggle to detect and locate attacks coming from generative and classic techniques (32.45\% EER in detection and 83.48\% AUC-ROC in localization).

</details>

Abstract: With the development of audio large language models (AudioLLMs), audio captioning needs to move from brief descriptions toward open-ended and fine-grained free-form descriptions. Existing evaluations often focus on generation quality or task performance, making it difficult to diagnose information coverage and description reliability. We propose MMAC, a \textbf{M}assive \textbf{M}ulti-dimensional benchmark for \textbf{A}udio \textbf{C}aptioning. MMAC contains 5,638 audio clips from more than 20 data sources, covering 6 capability categories and 15 evaluation dimensions. Given a model-generated caption, MMAC checks whether it mentions relevant information in the target dimension and whether the mentioned content is consistent with the reference label. We evaluate representative open-source and proprietary AudioLLMs. Results show clear differences across evaluation dimensions, information coverage, and description reliability. We will release the MMAC benchmark and evaluation code.

</details>

*Yanbo Ding, Zhizhi Guo, Quanyue Song, Yishan He, Zhixiang He, Yongxiang Li, Yali Wang*

TL;DR: Ripple 是一个实时流式音视频联合生成系统，通过跨模态循环记忆机制和固定长度滑动窗口注意力实现高效流式推理，在 480P 分辨率下达到约 28 FPS，支持长视频连贯生成，性能优于现有离线和在线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音视频生成模型质量高但延迟大，无法用于实时应用；已有的流式方法成本高且不支持长序列生成。因此需要一种既能实时推理又能保持长期上下文连贯性的音视频联合生成系统。

Method: Ripple 结合固定长度滑动窗口注意力与模态特定记忆状态（持续总结音视频上下文），并引入跨模态记忆交互增强音视频同步。采用三阶段训练：1) 将双向音视频教师模型适配为带模拟记忆的块状因果注意力；2) 通过端到端蒸馏优化记忆构建与交互流程；3) 针对流式音视频生成进行在线强化后训练。

Result: 在 480P 分辨率下达到约 28 FPS，推理速度远超教师模型；在短视频和长视频基准上均优于现有离线和在线音视频联合生成方法，且能生成连贯的长视频内容。

Conclusion: Ripple 通过跨模态循环记忆机制有效解决了音视频联合生成中的实时性与长序列连贯性问题，为实时流式多模态生成提供了可行方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出实时流式音视频联合生成系统 Ripple，属于音频与视频跨模态联合生成，与 [CANONICAL] 子主题 "音频与多模态联合生成" (audio_av_generation) 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Strongly aligns with user's priority on multimodal generation and efficient inference, with a novel cross-modal recurrent memory for real-time streaming. Signals: Real-time streaming audio-video generation, Cross-modal recurrent memory, Efficient sliding-window attention, 28 FPS at 480P, Three-stage training recipe

Abstract: Audio-video generative models achieve impressive quality but suffer from high latency, making them unsuitable for real-time applications. Although several streaming audio-video generation methods have been proposed, they remain costly and fail to support long-form generation. To address this, we propose \textbf{Ripple}, a real-time joint audio-video generation system with a cross-modal recurrent memory mechanism. To enable efficient streaming inference while preserving long-term context, Ripple combines a fixed-length sliding-window attention with modality-specific memory states that continuously summarize audio and video context. Cross-modal memory interaction is further introduced to enhance audio-visual synchronization. To learn this memory-augmented model effectively, we devise a three-stage training recipe: (1) adapting a bidirectional audio-video teacher to block-wise causal attention with simulated memory, (2) optimizing the memory construction and interaction pipeline through end-to-end distillation, and (3) applying online reinforcement post-training tailored for streaming audio-video generation. As a result, Ripple achieves ~28 FPS at 480P resolution, over faster than the teacher, while capable of coherent long-form generation. Extensive experiments on both short-video and long-video benchmarks demonstrate our superior performance over existing offline and online joint audio-video generation methods.

</details>

TL;DR: MPEcho 是一个基于旋律和音素感知的可控翻唱歌曲生成框架，通过引入音素编码器和长度调节器，显著降低了歌词的音素错误率（PER），并开发了 Phonsa 自动音素转录模型以解决高质量音频-音素对稀缺的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有翻唱歌曲生成模型 SongEcho 仅使用 F0 序列和清/浊音标签进行条件控制，缺乏显式的语言信息，导致歌词准确率低、音素错误率高。受歌唱语音合成（SVS）启发，作者希望引入显式的音素级条件控制来提升歌词准确性。

Method: MPEcho 在 SongEcho 框架基础上集成了音素编码器（phoneme encoder）和长度调节器（length regulator），提供显式的音素级条件信息和精确的时间边界。同时开发了 Phonsa，一个基于 Whisper 的自动转录模型，为歌唱语音提供高精度的音素级标注。

Result: 实验验证了 Phonsa 在音素对齐上的有效性，以及 MPEcho 在端到端翻唱歌曲生成任务中显著降低了音素错误率（PER），同时保持了旋律和语言内容的保真度。

Conclusion: MPEcho 通过引入显式音素级条件控制，有效解决了翻唱歌曲生成中歌词准确性的关键问题，为可控音乐生成提供了新的思路。

Classification reason: 该论文研究翻唱歌曲生成（cover song generation），属于音频生成任务，与 [CANONICAL] 音频与多模态联合生成（audio_av_generation）方向高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on controllable cover song generation, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and direction weights. Signals: Cover song generation, phoneme-aware, melody conditioning, singing voice synthesis, not in priority subtopics

Abstract: Cover song generation (CSG) should preserve the melodic and linguistic content of a reference song while recreating the remaining musical components. The state-of-the-art model SongEcho utilizes $F_0$ sequences and voiced/unvoiced (V/UV) tags for conditioning; however, implicit linguistic information from V/UV tags cannot guarantee lyric accuracy, leading to a high phoneme error rate (PER). Inspired by singing voice synthesis (SVS), we propose MPEcho, which integrates a phoneme encoder and a length regulator (LR) into the SongEcho framework. By providing explicit phoneme-level conditioning and precise temporal boundaries, MPEcho significantly reduces PER. To enable this, we developed Phonsa, a Whisper-based automatic transcription model that provides high-precision phoneme-level annotations for singing voices, overcoming the scarcity of high-quality audio-phoneme pairs. Experimental results validate the effectiveness of Phonsa for alignment and MPEcho for end-to-end CSG. The audio samples, code and weights can be accessed from .

</details>

Conclusion: 本文证明从静态人脸图像合成合理语音是可行的，且人脸到语音风格的映射具有跨语言泛化能力。该框架为仅依赖视觉信息的声音合成提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Zero-shot text-to-speech (TTS) clones a voice from a short audio prompt, but this reliance on reference audio is a barrier when only visual information is available, e.g. for historical figures or video-game characters. In this work, we propose a Face-to-Speech (F2S) framework that predicts a plausible voice from a static facial image. A lightweight Face Adapter, together with soft-tuning of the face encoder's upper blocks, aligns face-recognition features with the style space of a frozen StyleTTS 2 model, kept frozen during training. We evaluate on held-out identities from LRS3, a large-scale audiovisual corpus of English TED-talk videos. The synthesized speech is highly natural (UTMOS 3.7-4.0, matching or exceeding the 3.61 of ground truth), face-to-voice retrieval is consistently above chance, and the generated voice is consistent with the target speaker. Without any retraining, an English-trained adapter also produces fluent Spanish speech, indicating that the face-to-style mapping is largely language-agnostic.

</details>

### [43] [Where Detectors Fail: Closing the Tail-Domain Gap with Expert-Guided Mutual Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26555)
*Xuan Feng, Guihong Liu, Tianlong Gu, Shuai Zhao, Xuemin Wang, Chenzhong Bin, Yang Liu, Bo An*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态安全与攻防

Importance: 普通相关 (50.61) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出专家引导的互蒸馏方法（EGMD），通过输入级校准、专家引导教师对齐和原型锚定的域专有学生互学习，解决多模态假新闻检测器在尾部域上的泛化失败问题，在四个数据集上取得SOTA并将域偏差降低高达57.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态假新闻检测器在不同域之间泛化能力差，原因是模型会学习不可靠的证据：不平衡数据放大了域特定的捷径，而语义不一致的图文对使跨模态证据不可靠。现有方法未能系统解决这一问题。

Method: EGMD包含三个层面的设计：(1) 输入级校准——将图文对的一致性编码为共享增益，在融合前进行校准；(2) 表征级——专家引导的教师网络对齐域统计量，促使域特定模式集中在专门专家中；(3) 决策级——原型锚定的域专有学生网络通过互学习和双通道蒸馏，继承教师的特征几何和校准预测，同时抑制局部域先验。此外还构建了域平衡的Weibo_Balanced基准。

Result: 在四个数据集（两种语言）上，EGMD取得了SOTA准确率，同时将域偏差最多降低57.3%。

Conclusion: EGMD通过多层级蒸馏与校准机制有效缓解了多模态假新闻检测中的域泛化问题，证明了学习"该信任什么证据"比单纯追求域不变特征更有效。

Classification reason: 该论文研究多模态假新闻检测中的域泛化问题，属于多模态安全与可信性范畴。虽然具体任务是假新闻检测，但其核心问题是跨域泛化中的证据可靠性，与多模态安全/攻防方向下的防御与可靠性提升高度相关，因此复用 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态安全与攻防"。

Importance reason: Focuses on domain generalization for fake news detection, not aligned with user's priority on continuous multimodal models or video/image generation. Signals: Multimodal fake news detection, Domain generalization, Expert-guided distillation, No continuous token or generation focus, No autoregressive or diffusion video/image generation

Abstract: Multimodal fake news detectors often generalize poorly across domains because they learn to trust unreliable evidence: domain-specific shortcuts amplified by imbalanced data and semantically inconsistent text-image pairs that make cross-modal evidence unreliable. We propose Expert-Guided Mutual Distillation (EGMD), which learns what evidence to trust across the prediction pipeline. At the input level, input-level calibration encodes pair-level coherence as a shared gain before fusion. At the representation level, an expert-guided teacher aligns domain statistics and encourages domain-specific patterns to concentrate in specialized experts. At the decision level, prototype-anchored domain-specific students use mutual learning and dual-channel distillation to inherit the teacher's feature geometry and calibrated predictions while discouraging local domain priors. We further construct Weibo_Balanced, a domain-balanced benchmark that isolates the effect of imbalance on generalization. Across four datasets in two languages, EGMD achieves state-of-the-art accuracy while reducing domain bias by up to 57.3%.

</details>


### [44] [TPCD: Tone-Pressure Contrastive Decoding and the Label-Free Gating Bottleneck in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26536)
*Jinkun Zhao, Kui Zhang, Wenjun Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态安全与攻防

Importance: 值得关注 (72.44) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出音调压力对比解码（TPCD）方法，利用高压提示下VLM的logits分布作为对比解码的负分支，以缓解高压提示导致的模型过度承诺问题。在多个VLM上的实验表明，该方法能显著降低攻击成功率，但存在正例准确率下降的权衡，并提出了基于任务先验/答案不一致的门控机制来平衡性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高压提示（high-pressure prompts）会迫使视觉语言模型（VLM）做出不支持的承诺，如读取模糊文本、报告不确定时间或确认不存在的物体。现有防御方法缺乏对压力诱导分布本身的利用，本文旨在探索是否可以将压力诱导的分布本身作为对比解码的负分支信号来缓解这一问题。

Method: 提出音调压力对比解码（TPCD）方法：从安全中性指令下产生的logits中减去高压指令下产生的logits，作为对比解码的负分支。进一步设计了任务先验门控（task-prior gate）和答案不一致门控（answer-disagreement gate）来平衡攻击成功率降低与正例准确率保持之间的权衡。

Result: 在tone-matters基准上，LLaVA-1.5-7B在压力下ASR为66.75%，安全中性化降至9.88%，完整TPCD降至0.50%但正例准确率仅15.56%。使用任务先验/不一致门控后，正例准确率保持54.44%，ASR降至1.63%。在GLM-4.6V和Llama-3.2-Vision上的独立测试中，答案不一致路由器将ASR降至6.93%，优于安全中性化（10.98%）和分支不一致（9.67%），同时保持79.94%的正例准确率。

Conclusion: 压力是探测VLM承诺偏差的有用探针和可行的缓解信号，但当前的门控机制仍是基于事后和表面形式的，尚未成为独立验证的、基于grounding的检测器，需要进一步研究更鲁棒的防御方法。

Classification reason: 该论文研究高压提示（high-pressure prompts）对VLM的越狱攻击（使模型做出不支持的承诺），并提出TPCD对比解码方法进行防御。这属于多模态安全与攻防范畴，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态安全与攻防"（multimodal_safety_attack）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on VLM safety and decoding, not on user's priority subtopics like continuous models, video generation, or world models. Signals: VLM safety, contrastive decoding, pressure prompts, attack success rate, positive accuracy trade-off

Abstract: High-pressure prompts can push vision-language models (VLMs) into unsupported commitments, such as reading illegible text, reporting indeterminate times, or affirming absent objects. This paper asks whether the pressure-induced distribution itself can serve as a contrastive-decoding negative branch. Tone-pressure contrastive decoding (TPCD) subtracts logits produced under a high-pressure instruction from logits produced under a safe neutral instruction. On the 800-example tone-matters benchmark, LLaVA-1.5-7B under pressure reaches 66.75% attack success rate (ASR); safe neutralization reduces ASR to 9.88%; full TPCD reaches 0.50% but collapses positives to 15.56%. A benchmark-specific task-prior/disagreement gate preserves measured positive accuracy (54.44%) while lowering ASR to 1.63% on LLaVA. Treating this LLaVA analysis as the design split, full $n=800$ negative and $n=780$ matched-positive held-out runs on GLM-4.6V and Llama-3.2-Vision show that simple gates can improve over safe neutralization, with sensitivity analyses bounding the weak time-positive subtask. A category-prior-free answer-disagreement router reduces held-out aggregate ASR to 6.93%, improving over both safe neutralization (10.98%) and branch disagreement (9.67%) while matching branch disagreement's 79.94% positive accuracy, although it remains post-hoc and surface-form based. We conclude that pressure is a useful probe of commitment bias and a viable mitigation signal, but the current gates are not yet independently validated grounding-aware detectors.

</details>


### [45] [Do Latent Channels Actually Communicate? A Causal Audit of Latent Multi-Agent LLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26773)
*Huixiang Zhang, Mahzabeen Emu*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态安全与攻防

Importance: 值得关注 (74.01) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出一种因果审计方法，用于评估基于LLM的多智能体系统中潜在通信（latent communication）的信息传递效果。通过在发送者产生的表示进入接收者的边界处进行受控消息替换，该方法能够分解总体性能效应，区分消息存在、示例特定内容和其他智能体贡献的不同影响。实验表明，聚合准确率无法揭示潜在消息如何影响接收者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多智能体LLM系统中，潜在通信（传递连续内部表示而非文本）具有更大的表示容量，但仅凭端任务性能无法判断接收者是否真正使用了任务相关信息。现有评估无法区分观察到的效果依赖于消息存在、为当前示例生成的内容还是来自其他智能体的信息。因此需要一种因果审计方法来分解这些因素。

Method: 提出一种因果审计方法，在发送者产生的表示进入接收者的边界处进行受控消息替换。设计了四种消息设置，支持五种测量：编码的发送者信息、接收者对消息存在和身份的敏感性、示例特定内容的任务价值、以及来自其他智能体的额外价值。在GSM8K、ARC-C和MATH-500上使用Qwen3-4B和Qwen3-8B进行潜在中继（latent relay）实验。

Result: 在GSM8K上，Qwen3-4B的总体性能效应-1.00个百分点可分解为其他示例消息保留的-6.17点效应和示例特定内容贡献的+5.17点效应；在8B规模下两个分量方向均反转。在MATH-500上，Qwen3-4B的15.00点增益中8.33点来自其他示例消息，6.67点来自示例特定内容，而8B的增益主要由前者主导。自替换比较进一步表明示例特定内容和其他智能体价值是不同的。

Conclusion: 聚合准确率无法识别潜在消息如何影响接收者，受控消息比较应作为潜在通信的标准评估方法。该审计方法能够揭示潜在通信中不同信息成分的因果贡献，为多智能体系统的可靠性评估提供了新工具。

Classification reason: 该论文研究多智能体LLM系统中潜在通信（latent communication）的因果审计方法，属于多模态/语言模型系统的安全性与可靠性分析范畴。虽然论文主要关注LLM多智能体系统，但其核心是评估潜在表示通信中信息传递的因果效应，与多模态安全与攻防方向中关于模型内部表征、信息泄露、可靠性评测等主题高度相关。因此归入多模态安全与攻防（multimodal_safety_attack）子主题。

Importance reason: The paper focuses on multi-agent LLM communication auditing, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and lacks alignment with the configured direction weights. Signals: causal audit method, latent communication analysis, multi-agent LLM evaluation, no autoregressive generation, no continuous language model

Abstract: Latent communication in large language model (LLM)-based multi-agent systems (MAS) transmits continuous internal representations instead of text, but greater representational capacity does not establish that the receiver uses task-relevant information. End-task performance alone also cannot reveal whether an observed effect depends on message presence, content generated for the evaluated example, or information supplied by a separate agent. We introduce a causal audit that applies controlled message replacements at the boundary where the sender-produced representation enters the receiver. Four message settings support five measurements of encoded sender information, receiver sensitivity to message presence and identity, the task value of example-specific content, and the additional value supplied by a separate agent. We apply the audit to latent relay with Qwen3-4B and Qwen3-8B on GSM8K, ARC-C, and MATH-500. On GSM8K, the Qwen3-4B overall performance effect of -1.00 percentage point decomposes into a -6.17-point effect retained by an other-example message and a +5.17-point effect attributable to example-specific content; both component directions reverse at 8B. On MATH-500, the Qwen3-4B gain of 15.00 points comprises 8.33 points retained by an other-example message and 6.67 points attributable to example-specific content, while the 8B gain is dominated by the former component. Self-substitution comparisons further show that example-specific content and other-agent value are distinct. These results show that aggregate accuracy does not identify how a latent message affects the receiver and motivate controlled message comparisons as a standard evaluation for latent communication.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 设计可扩展的自对弈（self-play）算法：一个红队模型（GPT-Red）被训练攻击一组同时训练的多样化防御者（defender）智能体。红队模型在真实红队环境中训练，使用与最大规模RL后训练相当的计算资源。通过自对弈，红队模型不断从防御者的反馈中学习，发现更复杂的攻击策略。

Result: GPT-Red可靠攻破GPT-5.5及之前的模型；比人类红队发现更多成功攻击；泛化到未见环境、防御模型和工具；使用GPT-Red进行对抗训练得到的GPT-5.6成为对prompt注入最鲁棒的模型。

Conclusion: 自动化红队自对弈方法可有效提升LLM对prompt注入攻击的鲁棒性。随着每个新GPT模型鲁棒性的提升，将为更强的红队智能体提供更好的学习信号，形成自我改进的飞轮效应。

Signals: Automated red teaming, Self-play training, Prompt injection attacks, Large-scale safety training, Adversarial training for robustness

Abstract: We introduce \textbf{GPT-Red}, an automated red-teaming agent that is trained to discover novel prompt injection attacks against frontier LLMs. The goal of this model is to evaluate and improve the robustness of our production systems. To this end, we use it to adversarially train GPT-5.6, our most robust model to prompt injections to date. To create GPT-Red, we design a scalable self-play algorithm where the model is tasked with attacking a diverse population of simultaneously-trained defender agents. We train the model on realistic red-teaming environments using compute on the same scale as some of our largest RL post-training runs, making it the single-largest LLM safety training run ever documented. GPT-Red excels at red-teaming: it reliably breaks our past models up to GPT-5.5, it finds more successful attacks than human red-teamers, and it generalizes to held-out environments, defender models, and harnesses. In the future, we expect that as we improve the robustness of each new GPT model, it will in turn will provide better learning signal for \textit{even stronger} red-teamer agents, thus unlocking a self-improvement flywheel.

Conclusion: 匹配文本的语音韵律变化本身即可产生显著的越狱能力，应将其作为音频LLM安全评估的一等要素。研究为音频多模态安全提供了新的视角和评估工具。

Abstract: Audio-capable foundation models enable end-to-end spoken interaction, but they also introduce safety risks beyond transcript content. It remains unclear how much jailbreak capability can arise from matched-text variation in speech delivery rather than from lexical rewriting or broader style transfer. We study this question by holding transcript content fixed and varying six speech-delivery presets whose acoustic attributes may co-vary. We present PJ-Break, a black-box evaluation protocol with presets targeting arousal, authority, and speaking rate, together with AdvAudio-Prosody, a 600-sample benchmark with acoustically verified attributes. On the exact post-QC Qwen2-Audio panel, the Q=1 Panic (38/95), Anger (35/95), and Fast (32/95) presets are all well above Neutral (4/95). The fixed six-query pool covers 44/95 Qwen2-Audio seeds and 15/95 GPT-4o seeds and exceeds a matched-budget StyleBreak reimplementation (27/95) on Qwen2-Audio. A same-voice pool excluding the confounded Commanding condition still reaches 40/95, and a retained-panel ablation shows emotional-delivery audio alone (44/95) is far more effective than emotional text alone (11/95). Exploratory surrogate diagnostics and pilot mitigation observations are secondary, non-core analyses. Overall, matched-text speech delivery should be treated as a first-class factor in Audio LLM safety evaluation

</details>

TL;DR: 本文提出一种与具体守卫无关的"恢复-解码-再守卫"（Recover-Decode-Regard）防御增强管道，用于防御针对视觉语言模型（VLM）的编码越狱攻击（如将有害请求编码为集合论、形式逻辑、稀有语言、代码或图像文本）。该管道在守卫之前将编码内容转录并还原为明文，使现成的安全分类器能够检测真实请求。实验表明，该方法只能部分缩小安全-效用差距，且存在显著的安全-过拒斥权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VLM安全分类器（守卫）仅判断输入的表面形式而非语义含义，导致有害请求通过编码（如集合论、形式逻辑、稀有语言、代码或图像文本）即可绕过守卫。作者希望设计一种与具体守卫无关的防御增强方法，弥合这种"解码差距"。

Method: 提出一个模块化的"恢复-解码-再守卫"（Recover-Decode-Regard）管道：1) Recover：将图像内容转录为文本；2) Decode：将编码文本还原为明文有害请求；3) Reguard：将还原后的明文送入现成安全分类器进行检测。采用best-of-suite评估（类似AutoAttack），对11种攻击的集成进行评估。

Result: 未防御的集成攻击可攻破89-91%的行为；最佳守卫+放大器仍遗留63-65%的攻破率；放大器仅在10个守卫-目标对中的4个上有显著提升。Reguard层可大幅降低残余风险，但导致良性过拒斥率高达81-92%。没有配置能同时实现低攻击成功率和低过拒斥率。

Conclusion: 基于恢复的VLM防御存在固有的安全-效用天花板，无法同时实现低攻击成功率和低过拒斥率。放大器在接口层面与守卫无关，但效果并非均匀一致。论文绘制了恢复式VLM防御有效和无效的边界图，为后续研究提供了重要参考。

Classification reason: 该论文研究针对VLM的编码越狱攻击（encoded jailbreaks）及其防御方法，属于多模态安全与攻防范畴，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态安全与攻防"（multimodal_safety_attack）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on VLM jailbreak defense, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and negative keywords. Signals: VLM safety, jailbreak defense, guard-agnostic, safety-utility trade-off, encoded attacks

Abstract: Safety classifiers ("guards") are the dominant black-box defense for vision-language models, yet they judge an input's surface form, not its meaning: a harmful request re-encoded as set theory, formal logic, a rare language, code, or an image of text slips past a guard that would block it in plain language -- the decode gap. The natural fix is a guard-agnostic recover-and-decode amplifier that transcribes image content and restates encoded text into its plain payload before the guard, so any off-the-shelf classifier can screen the true request. We build this amplifier and evaluate it against the attacker's best case: an ensemble of eleven attacks, scoring a behavior as broken if any succeeds (best-of-suite, following AutoAttack) -- rarely reported for jailbreak defenses, yet ~3.5x the per-attack mean. This exposes our central finding: an empirical safety-utility ceiling for the non-iterative recovery defenses we evaluate, across five guards and two target VLMs. The amplifier only partly closes the gap -- the undefended ensemble breaks 89-91% of behaviors, and the best guard-plus-amplifier still leaves 63-65% -- and its gain over the guard alone is significant in only four of ten guard-target pairs. It is guard-agnostic at the interface, but not uniformly so in effect. A modular reguard layer closes much of the residual, yet drives benign over-refusal to 81-92% for well-calibrated guards; the one laxer guard that stays usable never reaches deployable safety (48% ensemble ASR). No configuration we evaluate reaches both low attack-success and low over-refusal, for the pipeline we study and for representation-shifting attacks -- encodings and cross-modal renders that leave a legible payload, not pixel- or embedding-space attacks. We contribute the amplifier, an ensemble evaluation that makes the trade-off visible, and a map of where recovery-based VLM defense works and where it does not.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于MLLM的AIGI检测器在捕捉细粒度异常方面存在感知瓶颈，主要关注视觉证据的组织与综合，而对内在感知能力的优化不足。

Method: 提出Veritas++框架，包含两个核心组件：1) Perception-oriented Learning (PoRL) — 将AIGI检测建立在三种基本感知能力（细粒度视觉细节、语义异常、像素级差异）上，用可验证奖励替代开放式描述监督；2) Value-aware On-Policy Distillation (VaOPD) — 自适应蒸馏机制，优先选择高价值蒸馏信号，通过特权自教师内化感知感知推理。

Result: 在标准、野外和新兴基准上的大量实验表明，Veritas++实现了良好的泛化性能，感知学习有效弥合了感知差距，VaOPD在不牺牲现有性能的情况下实现了高效的能力演进。

Conclusion: Veritas++通过显式强化基础感知能力并配合自适应蒸馏，有效提升了MLLM在AIGI检测任务中的表现，为合成图像检测提供了新的范式。

Classification reason: 该论文研究AI生成图像检测（AIGI detection），属于多模态安全与攻防范畴。论文使用MLLM进行合成图像检测，旨在防御生成模型带来的安全风险，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态安全与攻防"（multimodal_safety_attack）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on AIGI detection with MLLMs, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and lacks alignment with key boost keywords. Signals: MLLM-based detection, perception enhancement, on-policy distillation, AIGI detection, not in priority subtopics

Abstract: The growing capability of image generation models has made synthetic images a routine presence in open media, making robust and generalizable AI-Generated Image (AIGI) detection increasingly essential. While multi-modal large language models (MLLMs) offer a transparent alternative to black-box binary scoring, we observe that current MLLM-based detectors still exhibit notable perception bottlenecks in capturing fine-grained anomalies. They primarily focus on how visual evidence is organized and synthesized, leaving the intrinsic perception less optimized. To mitigate this gap, we present Veritas++, a perception-enhanced reasoning framework that establishes reliable perception as the foundation of authenticity reasoning. Rather than directly optimizing the model's explanatory ability, we ground AIGI detection on three basic perception abilities, i.e., capturing fine-grained visual details, semantic anomalies and pixel-level differences. Building on this insight, we introduce Perception-oriented Learning (PoRL), which replaces open-ended description supervision with verifiable rewards to explicitly strengthen these capacities. To further integrate enhanced perception with reasoning, we introduce Value-aware On-Policy Distillation (VaOPD), an adaptive distillation mechanism that prioritizes high-value distillation signals over uniform supervision, internalizing perception-aware reasoning through a privileged self-teacher. Extensive experiments across standard, in-the-wild and emerging benchmarks demonstrate that Veritas++ achieves promising generalization. The perception learning effectively bridges the perception gap and yields seamless gains on detection, while VaOPD further enables efficient capability evolvement without sacrificing existing performance. Code and checkpoints are available at .

</details>

### [50] [Seeing or Knowing? Visual Context Sensitivity in Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26326)
*Jiaang Li, Chengzu Li, Zhaochong An, Yifei Yuan, Xi Liu, Serge Belongie, Vésteinn Snæbjarnarson*

TL;DR: 本文通过图像重建探测和WhatIfVis基准测试，系统分析了MLLM在视觉证据与语言先验冲突时的表现。研究发现：粗粒度视觉属性在模型编码层得以保留，失败源于后感知利用阶段；模型对视觉上下文的敏感性不稳定，但SFT和激活修补可改善可控性；通过学习到的操控向量可调节视觉-先验权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLM在视觉证据与预训练语言先验冲突时经常失败，但失败的根本原因尚不明确——是视觉编码阶段信息丢失，还是后感知阶段利用不足？本文旨在定位这一瓶颈。

Method: 设计了两种诊断范式：(1) 图像重建探测——从冻结MLLM的最后一层图像token重建图像，检验视觉信息是否被编码；(2) 引入WhatIfVis基准，覆盖时空、颜色、计数、大小、重量五个维度的问答，答案可来自图像或先验。通过SFT、激活修补和操控向量等方法分析视觉上下文敏感性的可控性。

Result: 粗粒度视觉属性可从冻结MLLM的最终层图像token重建，说明视觉信息被编码；模型对视觉上下文的敏感性不稳定，SFT可提升可控性并跨域泛化；激活修补定位了视觉-先验权衡发生在特定架构深度；学习到的操控向量无需指令即可改善可控性。

Conclusion: 对于所研究的粗粒度属性，MLLM编码了视觉证据，但无法可靠地控制对其的依赖程度。瓶颈不在感知阶段，而在后感知的利用阶段。

Classification reason: 该论文研究MLLM在视觉证据与语言先验冲突时的失败模式，核心关注模型是否可靠地遵循视觉上下文而非依赖语言先验，属于多模态幻觉与可靠性范畴。论文提出的WhatIfVis基准和视觉上下文敏感性分析直接对应多模态幻觉与可靠性中的事实性、忠实性提升问题，因此复用[CANONICAL] subtopic 'multimodal_hallucination'。

Importance reason: Focuses on MLLM visual context sensitivity and hallucination, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous models or video generation. Signals: MLLM reliability, visual context sensitivity, post-perceptual failure, controllable steering, not aligned with user's top-weighted continuous/video generation priorities

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) achieve strong performance by integrating visual inputs with the rich priors of pretrained language models. However, they often fail on vision-centric tasks, especially when visual evidence conflicts with pretrained knowledge. We explore these failures separately using two diagnostic paradigms: (1) probing whether visual information is available, via image reconstruction, and (2) measuring multimodal context sensitivity, the extent to which the model follows visual context versus the language prior. To support the second, we introduce the WhatIfVis, a benchmark spanning five coarse-grained dimensions (spatial-temporal, color, count, size, and weight) whose questions admit answers from either the image or the prior. Our analysis yields three findings: (i) Coarse-grained visual evidence is preserved, as these attributes can be reconstructed from the final-layer image tokens of frozen MLLMs. Failures on questions about these attributes therefore point to post-perceptual utilization, rather than to degraded visual encoding during perception. (ii) Even when explicitly instructed to use or ignore visual evidence, vanilla models (without supervised fine-tuning on the WhatIfVis) show unstable visual context sensitivity. Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) improves this controllability and generalizes across domains, and activation patching further localizes the vision-versus-prior trade-off at architecture-specific depths across all six models. (iii) The vision-versus-prior trade-off is controllable along a learned vector. Applying this steering vector, even without any intent instruction, improves controllability over the vanilla model. Together, these results relocate the bottleneck, indicating that for the coarse attributes we study, MLLMs encode the visual evidence but cannot reliably control their reliance on it.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文发现前沿视觉语言模型（Claude、GPT、Gemini）在未收到任何医学图像时，仅凭患者的人口统计学描述（年龄、性别、种族）就会编造诊断结果，且诊断分布随描述词系统性变化。研究揭示了结构化输出通道中的"对冲"现象（文本承认无图但诊断字段仍给出疾病名），以及"皮肤痣"vs"皮肤病变"等提示词敏感性差异，表明幻觉是一系列不同的失败模式而非单一现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型在临床部署中需要可靠地处理缺失图像的情况，但现有研究未系统评估模型在无图像输入时的行为。作者发现模型不会主动拒绝回答，而是基于人口统计学信息编造诊断，这可能导致严重的医疗偏见和误诊风险。

Method: 对Claude Opus-4.7、GPT-5.4、Gemini-3.1-Pro三个前沿VLM进行系统测试：仅提供人口统计学描述（年龄、性别、种族组合）和检查类型（胸部X光、脑MRI、皮肤科），不附加任何图像，分析模型返回的诊断分布。同时对比"皮肤痣"vs"皮肤病变"等提示词变体，并分析结构化输出（诊断字段）与自由文本之间的不一致性。

Result: Claude对特定组合高度集中（65岁白人男性皮肤痣→黑色素瘤），GPT影响更广泛（年轻黑人患者胸部X光→结节病）。发现了"对冲"模式：文本承认无图但结构化诊断字段仍给出疾病名。Claude的皮肤科效应在"皮肤痣"换为"皮肤病变"后完全消失，而GPT保持不变，表明幻觉是多种不同失败模式。

Conclusion: 可信的VLM临床部署需要直接审计结构化输出通道，提示词敏感性应作为一级评估维度。模型基于人口统计学信息编造诊断的倾向可能加剧医疗不平等，需要建立更严格的测试协议来检测此类幻觉。

Classification reason: 该论文研究视觉语言模型在无图像输入时基于人口统计学描述编造诊断结果的现象，属于多模态幻觉（confabulation）问题，与 [CANONICAL] 多模态幻觉与可靠性 子主题高度匹配。

Importance reason: This paper focuses on medical VLM hallucination, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and has no overlap with their configured direction weights or boost keywords. Signals: Medical VLM hallucination, demographic bias in confabulation, structured output auditing, no image input, low relevance to continuous models, video/image generation, or world models

Abstract: When asked to describe a medical image that was never attached, frontier vision-language models do not abstain: they confabulate a diagnosis. We show that this confabulation is not random. It is structured by who the patient is said to be. Across chest X-ray, brain MRI, and dermatology, Claude Opus-4.7, GPT-5.4, and Gemini-3.1-Pro are each queried with only a demographic descriptor and no image, and changing the descriptor systematically shifts the diagnosis returned. Claude concentrates sharply: a 65-year-old white man asking about a skin mole receives Melanoma in nearly every response, and a 32-year-old Black woman asking about her chest X-ray receives a Sarcoidosis diagnosis whose reasoning reads "suspected, based on demographics and classic pattern.'' GPT-5.4's effect is broader, fabricating across every demographic cell we test, most conspicuously naming Sarcoidosis for young Black patients on chest X-ray. Two structural findings sharpen the problem. A hedged regime appears in which the prose acknowledges the missing image while the structured diagnosis field nevertheless names a disease, a dissociation invisible to prose-only audits. And Claude's dermatology effect collapses entirely when 'skin mole' is swapped for 'skin lesion' while GPT-5.4's is preserved, indicating that mirage is a family of distinct failure modes rather than a single phenomenon. Trustworthy VLM deployment in clinical pipelines requires auditing the structured output channel directly, and probe-word sensitivity should be treated as a first-class evaluation dimension

</details>

### [52] [Weak-to-Strong On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26246)
*Fangxu Yu, Zinan Lin, Xiaodong Liu, Weijia Xu, Michael Xu, Tianyi Zhou, Jianfeng Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 普通相关 (58.35) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 Weak-to-Strong On-Policy Distillation (W2S-OPD)，一种从多个弱模型（正负对比对）中蒸馏以增强强学生模型的框架。通过构建代理教师（proxy teacher）在 logit 空间中利用正负模型的差异来隔离能力方向，学生模型在其自身 rollout 上最小化逐 token 的 reverse KL 散度。在数学和代码基准上，W2S-OPD 超越了传统 OPD，使学生模型能够超越领域教师，并在所有监督源都更弱的情况下持续提升学生模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的 on-policy distillation (OPD) 假设教师至少与学生一样强，要么从大模型蒸馏到小模型（在无更大模型时失效），要么从共享基座训练多个领域专家（成本高昂）。本文旨在解决当所有可用监督源都比学生弱时，如何仍能提升强学生模型能力的问题。

Method: W2S-OPD 构建一个代理教师：从正模型和负模型的 logit 差中提取能力方向，加到学生的基础模型上。正负对比对包括：(i) RL 后专家 vs RL 前初始化（隔离 RL 赋予的技能），(ii) 大基座 vs 小基座（隔离规模带来的能力），(iii) 带正确提示 vs 错误提示的小基座（隔离实例级解题方向）。学生模型在其自身 rollout 上最小化逐 token 的 reverse KL 散度来蒸馏代理教师。

Result: 在四个数学基准和三个代码基准上，W2S-OPD 优于传统 OPD，使学生模型超越领域教师，并在所有监督源都更弱的情况下持续提升学生。分析表明不同对比对产生不同信号：RL 后与提示对比强调推理框架，规模对比强调解题过程。

Conclusion: W2S-OPD 是一种简单有效的弱到强蒸馏框架，通过对比蒸馏从多个弱模型中提取能力方向，突破了传统 OPD 对强教师的依赖，为 LLM 能力持续提升提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Weak-to-Strong On-Policy Distillation (W2S-OPD)，通过从多个弱模型中蒸馏来增强强学生模型，属于 LLM 的对齐与偏好优化范畴。虽然论文主要聚焦于 LLM 而非多模态，但其方法（on-policy distillation、reverse KL、对比蒸馏）与多模态 RLHF/DPO/偏好学习范式高度相关，且该方向下的 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态强化学习与对齐" 是涵盖此类蒸馏与对齐方法的最合适分类。

Importance reason: Novel distillation method for LLM alignment, but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous models or video generation. Signals: Weak-to-strong distillation, On-policy distillation, Reverse KL divergence, Proxy teacher from contrast pairs, Math and code benchmarks

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD), which aligns a student with the teacher's token-level distribution on the student's own rollouts, is an effective paradigm for transferring capabilities across LLMs. Prevailing approaches assume a teacher at least as capable as the student: they either distill a larger model into a smaller one, which fails at the frontier where no larger teacher exists, or consolidate multiple domain experts trained from a shared base, which requires costly training at the student's scale. We introduce Weak-to-Strong On-Policy Distillation (W2S-OPD), a simple yet effective OPD framework that improves the strong student by distilling from multiple weak models. W2S-OPD constructs a proxy teacher in logit space from a contrast pair of a positive and a negative model, both smaller than the student and cheap to obtain. Their logit difference isolates the capability direction, which is added to the student's own base model, yielding a proxy teacher that couples this direction while staying distributionally adjacent to the student. The student then distills it by minimizing the per-token reverse KL on its own rollouts. We instantiate the contrast pair as i) a post-RL expert against its pre-RL initialization, isolating the skill RL instills, ii) a larger against a smaller base model, isolating the capability from scale, and iii) a small base model with correct versus wrong hints, isolating the instance-level direction toward the solution. Across four math and three code benchmarks, W2S-OPD outperforms OPD, enables the student to surpass the domain teacher, and keeps improving the student even when every supervision source is weaker. Analysis shows different contrasts yield distinct signals: the post-RL and hint contrasts emphasize reasoning frameworks, while the scale contrast emphasizes the solving procedure. Our code will be available at .

</details>


### [53] [Misalignment Has a Personality: A Big Five Account of Emergent Misalignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26389)
*Hasibur Rahman, Smit Desai*

Main category: cs.CL, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 普通相关 (52.44) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出用大五人格（Big Five）向量来解释语言模型微调后出现的广泛性对齐失败（misalignment）。作者通过三级干预提取人格向量，验证其零样本迁移能力，并发现多种有害微调数据共享相同的人格特征签名（低宜人性、低尽责性、高外向性、高神经质），该签名在激活空间和文本输出中均可被检测到。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型在含有特定缺陷（如不安全代码、错误数学答案）的数据上微调后，会产生广泛的对齐失败，但其内部机制尚不明确。现有方法仅用单一二元对比提取激活方向，缺乏校准尺度，无法精细刻画模型行为变化。作者希望提供一个可解释的、人类可读的诊断框架来理解这种对齐失败。

Method: 作者为大五人格（开放性、尽责性、外向性、宜人性、神经质）分别提取激活向量，使用三级（低/中/高）干预而非传统二元对比，确保向量具有线性有序的校准尺度。在 Llama-3-8B 和 Mistral-7B 两个开源模型上验证向量的有效性，包括零样本跨语料迁移、层级敏感性分析。然后将这些向量应用于分析多种有害微调数据（如不安全代码、错误数学、有害回复等），计算其人格签名，并与微调后模型的行为和激活变化进行相关性分析。

Result: 三级干预的 Cohen's d 高达 6.2，向量可零样本、特质特异性地迁移到独立语料，且在中层效果最强。八种有害微调数据共享共同的大五签名：低宜人性、低尽责性、高外向性、高神经质，两个模型恢复的签名相关性 r=0.94。微调后模型的行为变化与该签名高度相关（激活测量 r=0.83，文本评判 r=0.90），内部激活偏移也相关（r=0.69）。此外，谄媚（sycophancy）被表征为高外向性+低尽责性，而非高宜人性，这是单一方向无法区分的。

Conclusion: 校准后的大五人格向量可以将模型对齐失败这一不透明的安全问题转化为人类可读的诊断画像。该方法不仅揭示了多种有害微调数据共享统一的人格签名，还能区分谄媚等细微行为差异，为模型安全评估提供了新的可解释性工具。

Classification reason: 该论文研究微调导致的语言模型对齐失败（misalignment）机制，通过大五人格向量解释模型行为偏移。虽然论文主要针对语言模型而非多模态，但其核心关注对齐（alignment）问题，与多模态理解与生成方向下的 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态强化学习与对齐"（multimodal_rl_alignment）在主题上高度相关，且该 subtopic 涵盖对齐的一般性机制研究。

Importance reason: Novel interpretability method for alignment, but misalignment personality vectors are not a priority subtopic. Signals: Big Five personality vectors, emergent misalignment, interpretable alignment, zero-shot transfer, activation-based diagnosis

Abstract: Fine-tuning a language model on data containing a narrow flaw, such as insecure code or incorrect mathematical answers, can cause broad misalignment through a mechanism that remains debated. We provide an interpretable account: in the models and corpora we study, misalignment behaves like a shift in personality. Prior work extracts activation directions for character traits from a single binary contrast, which can separate or steer behavior without establishing a calibrated scale. We instead extract personality vectors for the Big Five using a graded, three-level intervention and validate them on two open-weight models. The three levels are linearly ordered, with Cohen's d values of up to 6.2; the vectors transfer zero-shot and trait-specifically to an independent corpus; and their effects are strongest within a middle-layer band. Applied to training data, the vectors reveal that misaligned corpora across eight domains share a common Big Five signature: lower agreeableness and conscientiousness, together with higher extraversion and neuroticism. This signature is recovered by both models with a correlation of r = 0.94. Fine-tuning imprints the same profile, shifting the model's generations along the corresponding signature, with r = 0.83 using activation-based measurements and r = 0.90 using a text-based judge, while also shifting internal activations with r = 0.69. The same vectors characterize sycophancy as high extraversion and low conscientiousness rather than excess agreeableness, a distinction that a single direction cannot capture. Calibrated personality vectors transform an opaque safety phenomenon into a human-legible diagnostic profile.

</details>


### [54] [Constitutional Midtraining: Content Presence Drives Alignment Gains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26654)
*Desiree Cho, Cameron Tice, Bernie Hogan, Hunar Batra, Puria Radmard, Jun Zhao, Nigel Shadbolt*

Main category: cs.CY, cs.CL, cs.LG, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 值得关注 (74.01) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出"宪法式中期训练"（constitutional midtraining），在预训练中期插入基于原则的价值观内容（394M token），通过2x2因子设计（课程顺序×审慎推理）在120B规模上测试对齐效果。结果表明，宪法式中期训练能显著提升对齐泛化性和持久性，尤其在黑mail场景下效果突出（-17.5pp），且不损害MMLU等能力指标。内容的存在比结构更重要，该方法可作为SFT对齐的低成本补充。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后训练对齐（如SFT、RLHF）往往较浅，在微调后容易退化。作者希望探索在中期训练阶段（midtraining）插入原则性内容是否能产生更持久、更泛化的对齐效果，且与后训练阶段干净分离。

Method: 基于Anthropic的宪法构建394M token的宪法语料库，采用2x2因子设计（课程顺序×审慎推理），在120B规模下进行宪法式中期训练，产生4个实验条件+1个对照。在三个阶段（中期训练后、SFT后、良性微调后）评估对齐泛化性、价值冲突解决、黑mail抵抗等指标。

Result: 宪法式中期训练模型在对齐泛化和持久性上优于对照，尤其在黑mail场景：SFT在所有模型中引入了黑mail倾向，但宪法式中期训练显著削弱了该倾向，且优势在良性微调后仍保持（-17.5pp）。但该优势不扩展到需要主动抵抗上下文压力或冲突的场景。内容的存在比结构更重要，且对MMLU、ARC-Easy、PIQA、GSM8K等能力无负面影响。

Conclusion: 适度的宪法式内容在中期训练中即可产生广泛、持久的对齐增益，为以SFT为中心的对齐管线提供了一种廉价、互补的增强方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究通过对齐训练（constitutional midtraining）提升模型的对齐泛化性和持久性，属于多模态/语言模型的对齐方法研究，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态强化学习与对齐"（multimodal_rl_alignment）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on alignment midtraining, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: alignment generalization, constitutional midtraining, blackmail scenario, content over structure, no capability cost

Abstract: Post-training alignment is often shallow, eroding under fine-tuning. Whether midtraining interventions, cleanly isolated from post-training, can produce durable alignment remains untested. We test this via constitutional midtraining: inserting principled, values-based content into midtraining against a replay-only control at 120B scale. Our 394M-token constitutional corpus, built from Anthropic's Constitution, uses a 2x2 factorial design (curriculum ordering x deliberative reasoning) to produce four constitutionally midtrained conditions plus a control, evaluated on self-generated and established benchmarks including alignment under pressure, value conflict resolution, blackmail, and emergent misalignment across three stages: post-midtraining, post-SFT, and post-benign fine-tuning. Constitutionally midtrained models outperform the control on alignment generalization and durability, notably on blackmail: SFT instills a blackmail propensity in all models, but constitutional midtraining blunts it, with the advantage surviving benign fine-tuning (-17.5pp). This durability does not extend to settings requiring active resistance to in-context pressure or conflict, where the advantage attenuates after SFT. The presence of constitutional content at midtraining also matters more than its structure, and constitutional midtraining incurs no cost, on average, on the capabilities we test (MMLU, ARC-Easy, piqa, GSM8K) at any stage. A modest amount of constitutional content at midtraining could therefore yield broad, persistent alignment gains, offering a cheap, complementary addition to SFT-centered pipelines. Code, data, and models are available.

</details>


### [55] [SERPO: Self-Evolving Rubric Policy Optimization for Open-Ended Test-Time Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26873)
*Jianze Wang, Kunwang Zheng, Ying Liu, Yu Cao, Qilong Zhang, Jinlong Chen, Hua Yang, Qianglong Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 高优先级 (85.0) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: SERPO 提出了一种自演化评分策略优化方法，通过响应证据、查询特定评分标准和策略参数的三重共演化闭环，在开放生成场景下实现无需外部奖励模型的测试时强化学习，在多个基准上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时强化学习方法依赖答案投票（如多数投票），无法自然扩展到开放生成任务（如医疗问答、研究问答），因为开放生成中不存在共享的标准答案。同时，在没有外部奖励模型或更强评判模型的情况下，需要从模型自身输出中构建可靠的奖励信号。

Method: SERPO 构建了一个三重共演化闭环：(1) 响应证据演化——将模型生成的多个输出组织为 Good-Normal-Bad (G-N-B) 有序档案；(2) 评分标准演化——保留能够区分这些档案的判别性评分标准；(3) 策略演化——使用概率性标准评分将判定 token 似然转换为奖励信号来优化 actor。新的 actor 输出会刷新档案和评分标准，形成闭环。

Result: 在两个模型配置、两个域内基准和四个域外基准上，SERPO 在 HealthBench 和 ResearchQA 上相比基础模型分别提升高达 20.63 和 20.31 分，六个基准的宏平均提升高达 8.06 分，并支持域外迁移和跨基准持续演化。

Conclusion: SERPO 通过自演化的评分策略优化，有效解决了开放生成场景下测试时强化学习的奖励信号构建问题，展示了无需外部监督即可实现模型自我改进的潜力，并具有良好的泛化能力。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 SERPO（Self-Evolving Rubric Policy Optimization），是一种在测试时通过自演化奖励信号进行强化学习的方法，属于多模态/语言模型的对齐与强化学习范畴。虽然论文主要实验在文本领域（HealthBench, ResearchQA），但其方法本质是 RL/偏好优化范式，与多模态 RL 对齐方向高度相关，且该方向涵盖语言模型的对齐方法。因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态强化学习与对齐"（multimodal_rl_alignment）。

Importance reason: Proposes a novel self-evolving rubric policy optimization for open-ended test-time RL, but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: test-time reinforcement learning, open-ended generation, self-evolving rubric, no external reward, policy optimization

Abstract: Test-time reinforcement learning (TTRL) enables language models to self-evolve at inference time without labeled feedback. Existing methods rely on answer voting and therefore do not extend naturally to open-ended generation, where valid responses cannot be mapped to a shared canonical answer. Without external reward models or stronger judges, adaptation must instead construct reliable rewards from the model's own outputs. We introduce SERPO (Self-Evolving Rubric Policy Optimization), which replaces answer voting with a closed loop that co-evolves response evidence, query-specific rubrics, and policy parameters. Good-Normal-Bad (G-N-B) response evolution organizes maximally separated rollouts into ordered archives; rubric evolution retains criteria that discriminate these archives; probabilistic criterion scoring converts verdict-token likelihoods into reward signals; and policy evolution optimizes the actor with the resulting signals. New actor rollouts then refresh both the archives and rubrics, closing the three-way evolution loop. Across two model configurations, two in-domain benchmarks, and four OOD benchmarks, SERPO improves HealthBench and ResearchQA by up to 20.63 and 20.31 points over the corresponding base models, raises the six-benchmark macro-average by up to 8.06 points, and supports OOD transfer and continued cross-benchmark evolution.

</details>


### [56] [Progressive Multimodal Alignment for Continual Instruction Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26947)
*Duzhen Zhang, Yahan Yu, Qiaoyi Su, Jiahua Dong, Tielin Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态强化学习与对齐

Importance: 高优先级 (81.73) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出渐进式多模态对齐（PMA）框架，解决多模态持续指令微调（MCIT）中被忽视的投影器层遗忘问题。PMA通过轻量级表示描述符检测分布漂移，渐进扩展投影器专家，并保留预训练投影器作为稳定锚点，在保持稳定性的同时实现次线性参数增长，可作为即插即用模块提升现有MCIT方法性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MCIT方法主要关注LLM主干网络的灾难性遗忘，但忽略了投影器（projector）层在持续学习中因视觉分布漂移和指令语义演化导致的遗忘问题。投影器是多模态对齐的核心组件，其遗忘会严重影响跨模态理解能力。

Method: 提出PMA框架：1）轻量级表示描述符检测多模态分布漂移；2）仅在必要时渐进扩展投影器专家（projector experts）；3）可扩展路由器基于多模态特征集成专家输出；4）保留原始预训练投影器作为稳定对齐锚点。该方法实现稳定性与可塑性的平衡，参数增长为次线性，可作为方法无关的即插即用模块。

Result: 在两个最新MCIT基准上的大量实验表明，缓解投影器级遗忘能在现有SOTA方法基础上带来一致的性能提升。PMA在不同MLLM骨干网络上均表现鲁棒，展示了广泛适用的MCIT性能。

Conclusion: 投影器层遗忘是MCIT中一个被忽视但关键的问题，PMA通过渐进式专家扩展和稳定锚点机制有效缓解该问题，为持续多模态对齐提供了新的解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文研究多模态大模型在持续指令微调中的对齐问题，提出渐进式多模态对齐方法（PMA）以缓解投影器层的遗忘。核心关注多模态对齐（alignment），属于多模态对齐方向，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态强化学习与对齐"（multimodal_rl_alignment）。

Importance reason: Addresses projector-level forgetting in multimodal continual instruction tuning, a practical but niche problem not aligned with top priority subtopics. Signals: Multimodal continual instruction tuning, projector-level forgetting, progressive expert expansion, method-agnostic add-on, MLLM backbone scaling

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) rely on a projector to align visual representations with the language embedding space, making it central to cross-modal understanding. In Multimodal Continual Instruction Tuning (MCIT), however, shifting visual distributions and evolving instruction semantics cause this shared projector to drift, leading to projector-level forgetting, an issue largely overlooked by methods that focus primarily on the LLM backbone. We introduce Progressive Multimodal Alignment (PMA), a framework that enables the projector to adapt continually while preserving previously learned alignment. PMA detects multimodal distribution shifts via a lightweight representation descriptor and progressively expands projector experts only when needed. An expandable router integrates expert outputs based on multimodal features, while the original pretrained projector is retained as a stable alignment anchor. This progressive mechanism balances stability and plasticity with sub-linear parameter growth and serves as a method-agnostic add-on to existing MCIT approaches. Extensive experiments on two recent MCIT benchmarks demonstrate that mitigating projector-level forgetting yields consistent gains over prior state-of-the-art methods when combined with PMA. Moreover, PMA scales across diverse MLLM backbones, demonstrating robust and broadly applicable MCIT performance.

</details>

### [57] [Explainable and Resource-Efficient Spatial Reasoning in Multimodal LLMs for Decision-Critical Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27145)
*Piyush Jain, Kousik Dasgupta, Rajarshi Roy, Subarna Tripathi*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

TL;DR: 本文提出 ByDeWay-V2，一种无需训练的空间推理增强框架，通过开放词汇目标检测器计算物体间几何关系并注入结构化空间谓词到 MLLM 提示中，结合深度线索提升多模态大模型的空间关系理解能力，在 VSR 和 BLINK 基准上取得显著提升，且支持极低资源（40 token 预算、CPU 运行）的实时决策支持场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLM 在机器人、具身AI等决策关键场景中部署时，其空间判断的不可解释性限制了操作者信任和可审计性。现有方法（如 ByDeWay 的 LDP）使用单目深度估计分层提示来缓解幻觉，但粗粒度的深度分层无法解决同一几何平面内物体间的空间关系（如"左边"、"上方"、"内部"、"接触"等）。因此需要一种可解释、资源高效的空间推理增强方法。

Method: 提出 ByDeWay-V2 框架，使用开放词汇目标检测器 YOLO-World-L 检测图像中的物体，计算物体间的成对几何关系（包括投影关系如 left/right/above/below，以及拓扑关系如 inside/touching），将这些关系表达为人类可读的结构化空间谓词，与深度线索一起注入 MLLM 的提示中。整个过程无需训练，且支持极紧凑的 token 预算（40 token）在 CPU 上运行。

Result: 在 BLINK 空间子集上，ByDeWay-V2 相比 LDP 在 Qwen2.5-VL 上实现了 46% 的相对 F1 提升；在 VSR 基准上，将 BLIP-Base 从近乎随机性能恢复至 F1 0.53 的竞争性水平。最轻量配置在 40 token 上下文预算和 CPU 环境下运行，展示了资源受限实时决策支持的适用性。

Conclusion: ByDeWay-V2 通过显式注入结构化空间关系谓词，有效提升了 MLLM 的细粒度空间推理能力，同时提供了可审计的推理证据，适用于资源受限的实时决策支持场景。该方法无需训练，具有良好的通用性和可解释性。

Classification reason: 该论文聚焦于多模态大模型的空间推理能力提升，通过结构化空间谓词增强MLLM对物体间空间关系的理解，属于多模态推理范畴。现有[CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态推理与思维链"（multimodal_reasoning_cot）涵盖视觉推理与空间推理，与该论文主题高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on spatial reasoning in MLLMs but lacks direct connection to user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or autoregressive generation. Signals: spatial reasoning, training-free, resource-efficient, open-vocabulary detection, decision-critical applications

Abstract: As Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) are increasingly deployed in decision-critical pipelines such as robotics, embodied AI, and safety monitoring, the opacity of their spatial judgments limits operator trust and auditability. MLLMs demonstrate strong reasoning but often struggle with fine-grained spatial understanding and object hallucination. Prior work, ByDeWay, introduced Layered-Depth-Based Prompting (LDP), a training-free framework that mitigates hallucinations by structuring prompts using monocular depth estimation. However, coarse depth layering falls short in resolving object-to-object spatial relationships within the same geometric plane, such as projective ("left of", "above") and topological ("inside", "touching") relations. We propose ByDeWay-V2, which integrates explicit spatial relational context alongside depth cues, expressed as human-readable predicates that serve as auditable evidence for downstream decision support. Using an open-vocabulary object detector (YOLO-World-L), our framework computes pairwise geometric relations between detected objects and injects them as structured spatial predicates into the MLLM prompt, bridging 3D scene depth and 2D spatial semantics without any training. We evaluate ByDeWay-V2 on the Visual Spatial Reasoning (VSR) and BLINK benchmarks across multiple MLLMs, with hallucination grounding assessed via POPE. On the BLINK spatial subset, ByDeWay-V2 achieves a 46 percent relative F1 improvement over LDP for Qwen2.5-VL, and recovers BLIP-Base's spatial reasoning on VSR from near-random performance to a competitive F1 of 0.53. Our lightest configuration operates under a strict 40-token context budget on CPU, showing the framework's suitability for resource-constrained, real-time decision-support settings.

</details>

Result: 在大规模公开基准数据集上的实验表明，Zero-Fi 能够有效识别未见过的活动类别，实现了零样本Wi-Fi人体活动识别。

Conclusion: Zero-Fi 展示了信号-语言对齐在扩展Wi-Fi感知能力方面的潜力，使Wi-Fi感知能够超越预定义的活动类别，实现开放集识别。
