Importance reason: The paper focuses on Wi-Fi sensing and zero-shot activity recognition, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and lacks alignment with key boosted directions like video generation or continuous language models. Signals: Wi-Fi sensing, zero-shot recognition, contrastive alignment, human activity recognition, signal-language alignment

Abstract: Wi-Fi-based human activity recognition has advanced substantially, but most existing methods assume a closed set of activities and require labeled Wi-Fi samples for every target class, limiting their ability to recognize unseen activities. We present Zero-Fi, a contrastive signal-language alignment framework for zero-shot Wi-Fi-based human activity recognition. Zero-Fi learns unified representations from complementary Wi-Fi signal features and aligns them with the semantic representations of natural-language activity descriptions in a shared embedding space. This cross-modal alignment enables Zero-Fi to recognize new activity classes without requiring labeled Wi-Fi samples or model adaptation for those classes. Experiments on large-scale public benchmark datasets demonstrate effective zero-shot recognition of held-out activity classes, highlighting the potential of signal-language alignment to extend Wi-Fi sensing beyond predefined activity classes.

</details>


### [59] [SCALPEL: Semantic Cross-modal Alignment via LLM-Powered Encoder Learning for Medical Vision-Language Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26885)
*Yunzhan Fu, Enyu Bao, Xiangyu Shen, Yihao Wu, Chunbo Jiang, Fangli Guan, Liqi Yan*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态检索

Importance: 普通相关 (44.11) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: SCALPEL提出一种基于LLM的医学视觉语言预训练框架，通过临床报告对比微调将生成式LLM转化为各向同性编码器，采用非对称对齐策略和显式解剖-否定感知目标函数，在MIMIC-CXR、CheXpert和IU X-Ray上实现跨模态检索、零样本分类和医学VQA的SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学VLP框架受限于轻量文本编码器的有限上下文窗口和浅层表征能力，难以处理冗长、术语密集的临床报告。虽然集成医学LLM可提供临床推理能力，但面临三个瓶颈：生成式LLM在标准对比学习下的各向异性表征坍缩、端到端联合训练的巨大内存开销、以及忽略解剖侧性和否定修饰词的对比损失导致的医学幻觉。

Method: SCALPEL包含三个核心组件：(1) 临床报告对比微调（Clinical Report Contrastive fine-tuning），通过领域特定临床文本适配将生成式LLM转化为各向同性编码器；(2) 非对称对齐策略（Asymmetric Alignment），利用离线特征缓存实现高效训练；(3) 解剖-否定感知目标函数（Anatomy-Negation Aware Objective），显式惩罚涉及侧性混淆或错误否定的不匹配图像-文本对。

Result: 在MIMIC-CXR、CheXpert和IU X-Ray基准上，SCALPEL在跨模态检索、零样本疾病分类和医学视觉问答任务上均达到SOTA性能，显著优于现有医学VLP方法。

Conclusion: SCALPEL通过LLM编码器适配、非对称训练和医学感知目标函数，有效解决了医学VLP中LLM集成的三大瓶颈，为医学多模态表征学习提供了高效且准确的解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出医学视觉语言表征学习框架SCALPEL，核心任务是跨模态检索（图像-文本匹配），属于多模态检索范畴。论文使用LLM增强文本编码器，通过对比学习对齐视觉与语言表征，并在跨模态检索、零样本分类和医学VQA上评测，因此归类到[多模态检索]（multimodal_retrieval）子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on medical domain with negative keywords, not aligned with user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: Medical VLP, LLM encoder, cross-modal retrieval, negative keywords match, no autoregressive or video generation

Abstract: Vision-language pre-training (VLP) serves as a cornerstone for medical multimodal representation learning. However, existing medical VLP frameworks are often constrained by the limited context windows and shallow representational capacities of lightweight text encoders when processing lengthy, terminology-dense clinical reports. While integrating medical large language models (LLMs) offers unprecedented clinical reasoning capabilities, it introduces three major bottlenecks: (i) the anisotropic representational collapse of generative LLMs under standard contrastive objectives, (ii) the prohibitive memory overhead of joint end-to-end training with large batch sizes, and (iii) the medical hallucinations induced by vanilla contrastive losses that ignore fine-grained anatomical laterality and negation modifiers. To address these challenges, we propose \textbf{SCALPEL}, a \textbf{S}emantic \textbf{C}ross-modal \textbf{A}lignment framework via \textbf{L}LM-\textbf{P}owered \textbf{E}ncoder \textbf{L}earning. First, Clinical Report Contrastive fine-tuning converts a generative LLM into an isotropic encoder via domain-specific clinical text adaptation. Second, an asymmetric alignment strategy leverages offline feature caching to enable efficient training. Critically, we formulate an Anatomy-Negation Aware Objective that explicitly penalizes mismatched image-text pairs involving laterality confusion or false negations. Extensive experiments across MIMIC-CXR, CheXpert, and IU X-Ray benchmarks demonstrate that SCALPEL achieves state-of-the-art performance in cross-modal retrieval, zero-shot disease classification and medical visual question answering.

</details>

### [60] [Position: Evaluation Scores Are Perishable Knowledge Claims](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26191)
*Sankalp Gilda, Shlok Gilda*

Main category: cs.LG, cs.CL, cs.SE, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (55.69) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文指出语言模型评估中均值聚合会导致"信任膨胀"现象，即评估置信度超过最弱信号的可信度。作者提出评估分数应被视为具有形式性、范围和有效期三种属性的认知声明，并基于链式思维分析、可能性逻辑和代数理论，建议使用最弱环节聚合作为保守评估方法。在HELM排行榜上的实验表明，按均值排名和按最弱环节排名前五的模型完全不重叠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前语言模型评估方法将自动指标、LLM评判、人工评估和基准测试等多种信号通过均值聚合，导致评估置信度被夸大（信任膨胀）。作者希望建立更严谨的评估方法论，使评估结果的认知状态透明化。

Method: 作者从认识论角度将评估分数定义为具有三种属性的认知声明：形式性（人工评估证据强于自动指标）、范围（基准结果仅适用于测试分布）、有效期（基准结果随污染和分布漂移而失效）。借鉴链式思维分析、可能性逻辑和代数理论，提出最弱环节聚合作为参数化算子族的保守端点，由一个悲观参数控制。建议评估结果附带显式元数据（形式层级、范围声明、过期日期）。

Result: 在HELM公共排行榜上对54个前沿模型在10个场景下的分析表明，按均值排名和按最弱环节排名前五的模型完全不重叠，说明聚合方式对排名结果有根本性影响。

Conclusion: 评估分数应被视为具有时效性和局限性的认知声明，而非绝对真理。最弱环节聚合比均值聚合更保守可靠，评估结果应附带形式层级、范围和有效期等元数据以增强透明度。

Classification reason: 该论文讨论语言模型评估方法中的信任膨胀问题，提出评估分数应被视为具有形式性、范围和有效期三种属性的认知声明，并建议使用最弱环节聚合替代均值聚合。这属于评测方法论与基准设计范畴，与多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）这一CANONICAL subtopic高度相关。

Importance reason: Novel critique of evaluation aggregation but far from user's priority subtopics on continuous models, video/image generation, or world models. Signals: Trust inflation in evaluation, weakest-link aggregation, HELM leaderboard analysis, epistemic claims for scores

Abstract: Evaluation methodologies for language models increasingly combine multiple signals, from automated metrics and LLM-as-judge ratings to human assessments and benchmark suite results. When these signals are aggregated via averaging, evaluation confidence can then substantially exceed the reliability of the weakest signal: a phenomenon we call trust inflation in evaluation. We argue that evaluation scores should be treated as epistemic claims with three properties: formality (human evaluation provides stronger evidence than an automated metric), scope (a benchmark result applies to the tested distribution, not universally), and validity windows (benchmark results expire as contamination accumulates and distributions shift). Several converging research traditions (chain-of-thought analysis, possibilistic logic, and algebraic theory) establish weakest-link aggregation as the conservative endpoint of a parameterized operator family controlled by a single pessimism parameter. Drawing on those traditions, and on concrete lessons from building an evaluation harness for agentic AI, we propose that evaluation results carry explicit metadata (formality tier, scope declaration, and expiration date) to make their epistemic status transparent. We illustrate the cost of mean aggregation on the public HELM leaderboard: across 54 frontier models on ten scenarios, the top-five models ranked by mean score and by weakest-link are completely disjoint.

</details>


### [61] [MultivationBench: A Benchmark for Multimodal Sequential Motivation Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26465)
*Kawai Chung, Chunkit Chan, Yauwai Yim, Yuxuan Liu, Haochen Shi, Weiqi Wang, Qing Zong, Tianshi Zheng, Yixuan Fu, Kai Chung Wong, Hao Liang, Yifan Gao, Xi Yang, Janet Hui-wen Hsiao, Yangqiu Song*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 值得关注 (69.71) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 MultivationBench，一个基于心理学框架（Maslow需求层次和Reiss基本欲望）构建的多模态序列动机推理基准，用于评估多模态大模型在故事驱动的视觉叙事中整合累积上下文进行动态动机推理的能力。实验表明现有模型在该任务上表现不佳，揭示了静态识别与动态推理之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态评估主要关注静态文本或孤立的视觉快照，无法反映真实世界中行为驱动因素的累积性。多模态大模型在序列动机推理方面的能力尚未得到充分研究，因此需要构建一个能够评估模型在连续叙事中推断动态动机的基准。

Method: 基于Maslow需求层次理论和Reiss基本欲望理论构建评估框架，设计故事驱动的视觉叙事序列，要求模型整合累积的多模态上下文来推断不断演变的动机。通过多个多模态大模型在该基准上的表现来评估其序列动机推理能力。

Result: 所有测试的多模态大模型在MultivationBench上均表现不佳，难以在序列上下文中保持一致的动机推理，暴露出静态识别能力与动态推理之间的关键差距。

Conclusion: MultivationBench对现有模型构成了显著挑战，表明当前多模态大模型在类人社会理解所需的动态推理方面存在严重不足，为未来研究指明了方向。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 MultivationBench，一个用于评估多模态大模型在故事驱动的视觉叙事中进行序列动机推理的新基准，属于多模态评测与基准方向，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态评测与基准"。

Importance reason: Novel benchmark for multimodal motivation reasoning, but low alignment with user's priority on continuous models and generation. Signals: multimodal motivation reasoning benchmark, psychology-grounded evaluation, MLLM social intelligence gap, static vs dynamic reasoning, low priority subtopic match

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models have sparked significant interest due to their potential for social intelligence; however, their ability to perform sequential motivation reasoning remains insufficiently studied. Existing evaluations predominantly examine static text or isolated visual snapshots, which do not reflect the cumulative nature of real-world behavioral drivers. To address this gap, we introduce MultivationBench, a benchmark designed to rigorously evaluate multimodal motivation reasoning within story-driven visual narratives. The benchmark builds upon established psychological frameworks - Maslow's hierarchy and Reiss's basic desires - and requires models to integrate accumulated multimodal context to infer evolving motivations. Results indicate that MultivationBench presents a significant challenge: all tested models struggle to maintain consistent motivation reasoning across sequential contexts, revealing a critical disconnect between static recognition capabilities and the dynamic reasoning essential for human-like social understanding.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉安全理解评测多基于第三人称监控视频和二元分类指标，无法暴露模型在动态、部分可观测的第一人称场景中区分表面相关性与真正法医逻辑的认知差距。因此需要专门针对第一人称安全场景的因果推理评测基准。

Result: 对 Qwen3-VL、Gemini、VideoLLaMA 3 等 SOTA LVLM 的评估显示，模型在描述性得分上表现良好，但在因果推理和逻辑闭合方面存在显著脆弱性，揭示了感知-推理解耦现象。

Conclusion: EgoSafe-Bench 和 HRE 协议为开发逻辑鲁棒的视频理解系统提供了具有挑战性的数据集和系统化评估框架，暴露了当前 LVLM 在因果推理方面的不足。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 EgoSafe-Bench，一个面向第一人称视角（egocentric）视觉安全理解的评测基准，包含 12,000 个视频-QA 样本和分层推理评估协议（HRE）。这属于多模态评测与基准方向，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "multimodal_benchmark"（多模态评测与基准）。

Importance reason: Focuses on safety benchmark for egocentric video, which is outside the user's core priorities of continuous models, generation, and world models. Signals: First-person safety benchmark, Hierarchical Reasoning Evaluation, LVLM causal reasoning gap, No generation or world model focus, No continuous token or flow matching

Abstract: Reliable visual safety understanding in real-world scenarios demands more than just object recognition; it requires causal reasoning under epistemic uncertainty. While Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) demonstrate impressive semantic alignment on standard benchmarks, they often struggle to distinguish between superficial correlation and genuine forensic logic when grounded in the dynamic, partially observable nature of first-person experiences. Existing evaluations, dominated by third-person surveillance footage and binary classification metrics, fail to expose this cognitive gap. To address this, we introduce EgoSafe-Bench, a benchmark specifically designed to probe forensic reasoning in egocentric safety scenarios. It comprises 12,000 unique evaluation samples, generated by pairing each of the 3,000 video clips with a QA chain governed by our proposed Hierarchical Reasoning Evaluation (HRE) protocol. Unlike standard benchmarks, HRE mandates a rigorous reasoning trajectory from initial feature anchoring to blind-spot deduction and intent inference, thereby enforcing logical consistency and penalizing shortcut-based evaluations of state-of-the-art LVLMs (e.g., Qwen3-VL, Gemini, VideoLLaMA 3) reveal a significant perception-reasoning decoupling: models often achieve high descriptive scores but exhibit notable fragility in causal reasoning and logical closure. Our work provides both a challenging dataset and a systematic evaluation framework to foster the development of logically robust video understanding systems.

</details>


### [63] [SpatialQ: Understanding 3D Gaussian Splatting Scene Quality via Visual-based MLLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26595)
*Jingxuan Su, Shenglin Wang, Tiesong Zhao, Ge Li, Wei Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 SpatialQ，一个基于多模态大语言模型的 3DGS 场景质量评估框架。通过 3D 感知质量表征学习（VGGT编码器+质量头，融合多视图特征与几何线索）和基于 Qwen 的接地多模态推理机制，实现对 3DGS 场景质量的可靠评估，弥补了传统 IQA 方法无法感知空间结构和跨视图一致性的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS 场景质量评估需求日益增长，但传统图像质量评估（IQA）方法仅依赖 2D 感知线索，无法捕捉空间结构和跨视图一致性等场景级因素；而通用多模态大语言模型（MLLM）不擅长稳定的质量回归，可能产生不可靠的判断。因此需要专门针对 3DGS 场景的多模态质量评估框架。

Method: 提出 SpatialQ 框架，包含两部分：1) 3D感知质量表征学习——使用 VGGT 编码器配合专用质量头，将多视图图像编码为视图特定特征并聚合以捕捉跨视图一致性，同时通过深度图和点云结构信息的联合建模引入几何线索；2) 接地多模态推理机制——将原始图像、深度图、点云渲染和相机参数共同输入基于 Qwen 的 MLLM 进行推理。

Result: 在多个 3DGS 场景数据集上验证了 SpatialQ 的有效性，相比传统 IQA 方法和通用 MLLM 方法在质量评估准确性上有显著提升。

Conclusion: SpatialQ 通过结合 3D 感知质量表征学习和多模态推理，有效解决了 3DGS 场景质量评估中空间结构和跨视图一致性的建模问题，为 3DGS 场景质量评估提供了新的解决方案。

Classification reason: 该论文提出针对3DGS场景质量的多模态评估框架，属于多模态质量评测任务，与[CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态评测与基准"（multimodal_benchmark）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on 3DGS quality assessment, which is not a priority subtopic and lacks direct connection to continuous language models, autoregressive generation, or world models. Signals: 3DGS quality assessment, MLLM-based evaluation, not autoregressive generation, not continuous multimodal, not world model

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has emerged as an effective representation for novel view synthesis and 3D scene reconstruction, creating an increasing demand for reliable quality assessment. Unlike conventional image quality assessment (IQA), the quality of a 3DGS scene depends not only on the perceptual fidelity of rendered views, but also on scene-level factors such as spatial structure and cross-view consistency. Existing IQA methods are limited by their reliance on 2D perceptual cues, whereas general multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are not designed for stable quality regression and may produce unreliable judgments. To address these limitations, a multimodal quality assessment framework is developed for 3DGS scene understanding. First, a 3D-aware quality representation learning framework is introduced by augmenting a VGGT-based encoder with a dedicated quality head. Multi-view images are encoded into view-specific features and aggregated to capture cross-view consistency, while geometric cues are incorporated through joint modeling of depth and point-cloud-related structural information, enabling the learning of structure-aware quality representations beyond appearance-driven features. Second, a grounded multimodal reasoning mechanism is constructed by jointly feeding original images, depth maps, point cloud renderings, and camera parameters into a Qwen-based MLLM.

</details>


### [64] [OmegaUse-OfficeVal: Benchmarking LLM Agents on Long-Horizon Office-Suite Tasks with Economic Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27155)
*Jingbo Zhou, Yusai Zhao, Qi Bao, Jingjia Cao, Zhenghai Chen, Chang Gao, Kaiqi Guo, Muxin Guo, Mingxuan Li, Xinjiang Lu, Yanru Ma, Yixiong Xiao, Zenghui Zhang, Le Zhang, Hua Wu*

TL;DR: 本文提出了OmegaUse-OfficeVal，一个用于评估LLM智能体在长程办公套件任务上的基准，包含100个任务，平均需要2.32小时人工完成。每个任务配有人工劳动时间和任务价格代理两种经济信号，支持人类成本与LLM推理成本的直接比较。实验表明，尽管所有评估的LLM比人类工人更便宜、更快，但尚未达到人类级别的交付质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体评测基准缺乏对办公套件工作流（如文档、表格、演示等）的评估支持，且无法评估智能体在合理成本下完成任务的能力。作者希望构建一个具有经济信号（人工成本与任务价格）的基准，以支持人类与LLM之间的成本-质量对比。

Method: 从从业者提出的办公套件需求出发，经过隐私保护处理，构建了100个长程办公任务。每个任务配有两个经济信号：人工劳动时间和任务价格代理。开发了基于代码的验证器（code-based verifiers），从细粒度评分标准（fine-grained rubrics）出发进行稳定评估。评估了多个前沿LLM并设置了人类基线。

Result: 所有评估的LLM在成本和速度上均优于人类工人，但在交付质量上尚未达到人类水平。论文提供了详细的成本-质量对比分析。

Conclusion: OmegaUse-OfficeVal为LLM智能体在办公套件任务上的评估提供了经济信号驱动的基准，揭示了当前LLM在长程办公任务中虽然成本效率高但质量仍有差距的现状。代码和数据集已开源。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一个用于评估LLM智能体在办公套件长程任务上的基准（OmegaUse-OfficeVal），属于多模态理解与生成方向下的评测基准类别。虽然任务涉及办公软件操作（非典型多模态视觉输入），但论文属于LLM Agent评测基准，与multimodal_benchmark（多模态评测与基准）这一CANONICAL主题在"评测基准"这一核心概念上匹配。该基准包含经济信号（人工时间与任务价格代理）用于成本对比，属于评测协议创新。

Importance reason: Focuses on office-suite task benchmarking with economic grounding, not aligned with user's priority on continuous multimodal models or video/image generation. Signals: office-suite tasks, LLM agent evaluation, economic grounding, human cost comparison, not in priority subtopics

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly expected to assist users in completing tasks. However, existing benchmarks provide limited support for evaluating whether agents can carry out office-suite workflows at a reasonable cost. We introduce OmegaUse-OfficeVal, a benchmark for evaluating LLM agents on long-horizon office-suite tasks with task-level economic grounding. The benchmark comprises 100 tasks derived from office-suite requests proposed by practitioners and adapted through a privacy-preserving process. On average, these tasks require 2.32 hours of human labor to complete. An important feature of the benchmark is that each task is paired with two economic signals: human labor time and task price proxy. These signals enable direct comparisons between human costs and LLM inference costs, as well as value-weighted evaluation. To support stable evaluation, we develop code-based verifiers from fine-grained rubrics. We evaluate several frontier LLMs together with a human baseline. Although all evaluated LLMs are substantially cheaper and faster than human workers, they have not yet approached human-level deliverable quality. The code and dataset are fully open-sourced, and more information is available on our project website: .

</details>


### [65] [TREK: A Travel Reasoning and Evaluation Kit for LLM Agents in Complex Trip Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26977)
*Jinhu Qi, Wentao Zhang, Siu Man Ng, Feiyang Xu, Yanyu Chen, Yaoman Li, Irwin King*

Main category: cs.CL

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (50.61) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: TREK是一个面向LLM智能体在复杂旅行规划中的推理与评测基准，包含800个多约束任务（533个可行、267个不可行），基于包含212,530条记录、375个城市和13种人物画像的合成知识库，通过确定性规则评估器进行评分。评估15个LLM智能体后发现，最强模型GPT-5.6在可行任务上仅46.2%能生成完全可行的计划，满足用户未明示需求是普遍瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体评测基准对旅行规划任务采用软性或LLM评判的评分标准，无法保证返回的计划是可执行的，且不可复现、不可审计。需要一个能够严格验证计划在约束正确性、无幻觉、时空可行性、预算合规性和人物画像匹配度等多维度上是否完全可行的确定性评测基准。

Method: 构建了一个包含212,530条记录、375个城市和13种人物画像的合成知识库，通过生产风格的RESTful API工具沙箱提供服务。设计了800个多约束任务（533个可行、267个不可行），每个任务附带人工验证的黄金参考解。采用完全确定性的规则评估器，从9个约束维度（包括实体存在性、时空可行性、预算、人物画像匹配等）对智能体输出进行评分。

Result: 评估15个LLM智能体，最强模型GPT-5.6在可行任务上仅46.2%能生成完全可行的计划，中位数仅6.6%，最差为0.0%。满足旅行者未明示的人物画像需求是普遍瓶颈，即使最前沿模型也未能解决。

Conclusion: TREK提供了一个可复现、可审计的旅行规划评测基准，揭示了当前LLM智能体在复杂多约束规划任务中的严重不足，特别是对用户未明示需求的推理能力是核心瓶颈。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一个面向LLM智能体在复杂旅行规划中的推理与评测基准TREK，属于多模态理解与生成方向下的多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）子主题。虽然任务本身是文本形式的旅行规划，但涉及工具使用、多约束推理、预算与时空可行性等复杂推理能力，与多模态评测基准的范畴一致。

Importance reason: Focuses on travel planning benchmark for LLM agents, not aligned with user's priority on continuous multimodal models, video/image generation, or world models. Signals: LLM agent benchmark, travel planning, constraint satisfaction, not multimodal generation, no continuous token or flow matching

Abstract: Travel planning is a demanding stress test for tool-using LLM agents: a usable itinerary is a single artifact that must be right along many axes at once - every flight, hotel, and attraction must exist and be bookable, the days must be physically traversable, the total must clear a budget, and the plan must serve a traveler whose needs are only partly stated. Existing agent benchmarks reward these properties one at a time and grade the final output with soft or LLM-judged rubrics, which cannot certify that a returned plan is executable and are neither reproducible nor auditable. We introduce TREK (Travel Reasoning and Evaluation Kit), a benchmark for feasible itinerary synthesis: producing a single plan that is jointly constraint-correct, hallucination-free, spatio-temporally executable, budget-valid, and responsive to the traveler's unstated persona needs. TREK comprises 800 multi-constraint tasks - 533 feasible and 267 provably infeasible with typed route/entity/budget causes - over a synthetic, internally consistent knowledge base of 212,530 records across 375 cities and 13 personas, served through a production-style tool sandbox of validated RESTful APIs. Every task is scored by a fully deterministic, rule-based evaluator with no LLM judge and ships a human-verified gold reference that scores a perfect 1.0 under that same evaluator, so the ceiling is demonstrably achievable and every remaining gap is an agent limitation rather than scorer strictness. Evaluating 15 LLM agents across nine constraint dimensions, we find that even the strongest (GPT-5.6) produces a fully-feasible plan on only 46.2% of solvable tasks, with a median of 6.6% and a floor of 0.0%; satisfying travelers' unstated needs emerges as the universal bottleneck, unsolved even at the frontier. We release the dataset, tool sandbox, deterministic evaluator, and agent code as a fully reproducible benchmark.

</details>


### [66] [See2Think: Do Multimodal Models Really Use Intermediate Visual States?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26769)
*Siyu Yan, Zhuoran Yan, Haiying Xu, Panhao Zhou, Jingyu Chen, Chenhao Ji, Shuo Cao, Yongheng Zhang, Haoze Liu, Siyu Zhang, Xiwen Gu, Yihao Liu, Alex Jinpeng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 值得关注 (73.21) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: See2Think 是一个统一评测框架，包含 See2ThinkBench 基准和 Visual Action-of-Thought (VAoT) 推理协议，用于系统评估多模态大模型在推理过程中是否真正使用中间视觉状态（如草图、标注、工具输出等）。实验发现视觉推理高度依赖模型和环境，忠实渲染是主要瓶颈，且模型在受控干预下表现出对视觉状态的行为依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评测基准存在两个局限：一是任务集合覆盖范围窄或部分样本可纯文本解决；二是评估只关注最终答案，不诊断中间视觉状态如何生成、渲染和使用。因此需要统一的评测框架来系统分析多模态模型是否真正依赖中间视觉状态进行推理。

Method: 构建 See2ThinkBench（1200个开放视觉依赖问题，覆盖12个任务类别，包括2D结构化、3D场景和真实世界推理）和 VAoT（Visual Action-of-Thought）推理协议，记录文本思维、视觉动作、渲染状态及后续推理，在四种受控推理设置下评估代表性闭源和开源多模态模型。

Result: 视觉推理高度依赖模型和环境，没有单一设置在所有任务上持续占优。过程分析显示模型通常能选择相关视觉操作，但忠实渲染是最明显的瓶颈；高反馈采纳率并不一定转化为准确率提升。在任务相关的损坏反馈下，模型表现出对视觉状态的行为依赖，准确率在受控干预中下降超过10个百分点。

Conclusion: 多模态模型在推理中确实表现出对中间视觉状态的依赖，但忠实渲染仍是关键瓶颈。See2Think 框架为系统诊断视觉推理过程提供了有效工具，揭示了当前模型在视觉状态生成与利用方面的不足。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 See2Think 统一评测框架，包含 See2ThinkBench（1200个开放视觉推理问题）和 VAoT（视觉思维链推理协议），属于多模态模型在视觉推理过程中的评测与基准工作，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] 多模态评测与基准 (multimodal_benchmark)。

Importance reason: Evaluates whether MLLMs truly use intermediate visual states, but focuses on benchmarks and diagnostics rather than advancing core continuous or generative models. Signals: Multimodal evaluation benchmark, Visual reasoning diagnostics, Not aligned with priority subtopics, No autoregressive or continuous model focus, Negative keywords absent

Abstract: Multimodal large language models increasingly use sketches, annotations, tools, and intermediate images during reasoning, but it remains unclear whether they truly rely on these visual states. Existing benchmarks are limited both by task collections with narrow coverage or partially text-solvable samples and by evaluations that emphasize final answers without diagnosing how intermediate visual states are generated, rendered, and used. We introduce See2Think, a unified evaluation framework comprising See2ThinkBench and Visual Action-of-Thought (VAoT). See2ThinkBench contains 1,200 open-ended, visually dependent problems across 12 task categories spanning 2D structured, 3D scene, and real-world reasoning. VAoT records textual thoughts, visual actions, rendered states, and subsequent reasoning under four controlled inference settings. Evaluating representative proprietary and open-source multimodal models, we find that visual reasoning is strongly model- and environment-dependent, with no single setting consistently dominating across tasks. Process analysis further shows that models usually select relevant visual operations, while faithful rendering remains the clearest bottleneck and high feedback uptake does not necessarily translate into accuracy gains. Under task-relevant corrupted feedback, models exhibit behavioral dependence on visual states, with accuracy dropping by over 10 percentage points in controlled interventions.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文介绍了 ICDAR 2026 关于原子层沉积/刻蚀科学图表信息提取的竞赛，提出了 Sci-ImageMiner 基准数据集，涵盖分类、摘要、数据提取和视觉问答四个互补任务。68名参赛者提交了1263份结果，发现当前多模态模型在分类和摘要任务上表现良好，但在数据提取和科学推理方面存在明显不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学图表中蕴含大量未在论文文本中呈现的领域知识，现有多模态 AI 在科学图表理解与推理方面缺乏系统性的评测基准和竞赛平台，难以评估和推动该方向的发展。

Method: 构建了 Sci-ImageMiner 基准数据集，由领域专家标注，涵盖四个端到端互补任务：分类、摘要、数据提取和视觉问答。组织了 ICDAR 2026 竞赛，吸引68名活跃参赛者，共提交1263份公开/私有测试集结果。

Result: 最先进的多模态模型在分类和摘要任务上表现良好，但在数据提取和科学推理（特别是视觉问答）任务上表现不佳，揭示了当前模型在领域感知多模态推理方面的关键局限性。

Conclusion: Sci-ImageMiner 基准和竞赛为科学图表理解与推理研究建立了严格的评测平台，展示了当前方法的潜力，同时指出了领域感知多模态 AI 系统改进的方向和挑战。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 Sci-ImageMiner 基准数据集并组织了 ICDAR 2026 竞赛，专注于科学图表理解与推理的多模态 AI 评测，属于多模态评测与基准（multimodal_benchmark）这一 [CANONICAL] 子主题。

Importance reason: Focuses on a niche scientific figure extraction competition, not aligned with user's priority on continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: Scientific figure extraction, Benchmark competition, Multimodal evaluation, Low priority subtopic, No autoregressive or continuous model focus

Abstract: Scientific figure comprehension and reasoning using multimodal AI requires integrating visual perception with domain-specific reasoning to extract meaningful knowledge, often not presented in the text of a research publication. The Sci-ImageMiner benchmark dataset, accompanied by a community-driven competition, raises the bar over prior scientific competitions by curating a comprehensive, expert-annotated dataset across four end-to-end complementary tasks. The competition attracted 68 active participants and 1,263 public/private submissions from 9th January 2026 to 8th April 2026. Our results show that state-of-the-art multimodal models perform well on classification and summarization tasks but struggle with data extraction and scientific reasoning, particularly in visual question-answering. These findings reveal key limitations and highlight challenges and opportunities for improving domain-aware multimodal AI systems. Overall, the Sci-ImageMiner benchmark and competition establish a rigorous platform for advancing research in scientific figure comprehension and reasoning and demonstrate the potential of state-of-the-art approaches for a challenging and complex research area.

</details>


### [68] [Visual Credit Audit for Multimodal Spatial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27069)
*Feixiang Liu, Qiang Qiu, Lanbo Sun, Nan Wei, Huawei Shen, Xueqi Cheng*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 值得关注 (70.74) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 Visual Credit Audit (VCA)，一种用于审计多模态空间推理基准中模型决策是否真正依赖视觉证据的方法。VCA 通过对比图像、纯文本和空白控制条件，分离出"正确性"与"视觉信用"，发现 12.73-26.25% 的正确决策实际上并未获得图像的有效支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的封闭式 yes/no 空间推理基准存在一个根本问题：模型即使没有利用图像信息，仅凭文本上下文也可能猜对答案。这使得基准分数无法反映模型是否真正进行了视觉空间推理。作者希望设计一种审计方法，能够区分"模型答对了"和"模型确实依赖视觉证据答对了"。

Method: VCA 方法包含两个核心审计：(1) 无标签审计——比较模型在"图像+问题"、"纯文本"和"空白控制"三种条件下的决策，判断图像是否提供了额外支持；(2) 有标签审计——引入"依赖信用正确性 (D-CC)"指标，在正确回答中衡量图像相对于控制条件的正向增益。此外，通过图像置换实验、固定像素关系对比和 3×3 证据来源因子分析，进一步验证模型是否对关系特定的视觉证据做出响应。

Result: 在四个开源 MLLM 和两个空间推理基准上，12.73-26.25% 的正确决策未被视觉证据支持（即正确但无信用）。匹配的图像置换使 D-CC 下降 21.25-47.80 个百分点，所有配对 95% 置信区间均大于零。在控制条件下，正确但无信用的决策中，关系反转的响应率为 81.57-100.00%，32.11% 改变了答案。

Conclusion: VCA 能够有效分解多模态空间推理基准中的成功为三个维度：正确性、图像额外支持和关系一致响应。该方法揭示了现有基准中相当比例的正确回答实际上缺乏视觉证据支持，为多模态评测提供了更细粒度的分析工具。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Visual Credit Audit (VCA) 方法，用于审计多模态空间推理基准中模型决策是否真正依赖视觉证据，属于多模态评测方法论。这与 [CANONICAL] 多模态评测与基准 (multimodal_benchmark) 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on auditing spatial reasoning benchmarks, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous models or video generation. Signals: Benchmark audit, spatial reasoning, visual credit, MLLM evaluation, no direct alignment with priority subtopics

Abstract: Closed yes/no spatial benchmarks can reward a correct answer even when the image adds little support beyond no-image contexts. Under a fixed forced-choice interface, Visual Credit Audit (VCA) separates two estimands: whether the benchmark image gives the model's declared decision more support than text-only and blank controls, and whether the model responds to relation-specific visual evidence. The first audit is training- and label-free and does not require an answer flip. Applying labels yields dependence-credited correctness (D-CC); on correct items, it equals same-control gold-aligned positive gain, while prediction alignment extends the audit to errors. Across four open MLLMs and two spatial benchmarks, 12.73-26.25% of decisions are correct yet uncredited. Matched same-split image permutation reduces D-CC by 21.25-47.80 points, with every paired 95% interval above zero. Fixed-pixel relation contrasts and a 3x3 evidence-source factorial show why null controls cannot identify relation response. Among controlled correct-but-uncredited agreement decisions, response to relation reversal spans 81.57-100.00%, while 32.11% pooled change answer. Independently audited outcomes on 108 geometry-compatible edits provide a bounded natural-image correspondence check. VCA thereby decomposes benchmark success into correctness, additional image support, and relation-consistent response.

</details>


### [69] [SciFigQual-Bench: A Benchmark for Scientific Figure Quality Assessment with Full-Manuscript Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27084)
*Zihan Deng, Chuanzhi Xu, Huiqi Liang, Haoyang Li, Xiaozhen Zhong, Lequan Yu*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态评测与基准

Importance: 普通相关 (52.54) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 SciFigQual-Bench，一个面向科学论文图像质量评估的全文本上下文基准，涵盖清晰度、布局、标题匹配、上下文相关性和误导风险五个维度，包含 6308 张来自顶级计算机科学会议（2020-2025）的图像及专家标注。同时设计了 SFQ-Agent 分阶段跨模态评估框架，通过多模态证据收集与融合实现可审计的细粒度评分，在测试集上优于直接评估和辅助 VLM 方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像质量评估方法主要针对自然照片或 AI 生成内容设计，无法直接应用于科学论文中的图像。已有的学术图表研究仅停留在视觉表面比较，未能验证标题对齐、引用相关性和视觉误导性。因此需要专门针对科学图像的全文本上下文质量评估基准。

Method: 构建 SciFigQual-Bench 数据集：从 2020-2025 年顶级计算机科学会议论文中收集 6308 张图像，由多位领域专家在五个维度（清晰度、布局、标题匹配、上下文相关性、误导风险）独立评分并聚合为金标准标注。每张图像绑定其标题、引用句和论文全文上下文。设计 SFQ-Agent 分阶段跨模态评估框架，通过收集和融合多模态证据实现可审计的细粒度评分。

Result: 在 eval1200 测试子集上，SFQ-Agent (F3) 搭配 GPT-5.6-Sol 取得了最低的整体平均绝对误差（0.418）和最高的一致性率（93.4%），持续优于直接评估和辅助（Sidecar）视觉语言模型评估方案。

Conclusion: SciFigQual-Bench 填补了科学图像质量评估领域的空白，提供了首个结合全文上下文的细粒度多维度评估基准。SFQ-Agent 框架展示了通过分阶段跨模态证据融合进行可审计评估的有效性，为科学图像质量自动评估提供了新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了 SciFigQual-Bench，一个面向科学图像质量评估的多模态基准，包含图像、标题、引用句和全文上下文，属于多模态评测范畴。该基准涉及视觉质量评估与文本上下文对齐，因此归类到 [CANONICAL] 多模态评测与基准 (multimodal_benchmark)。

Importance reason: Focuses on scientific figure quality assessment, which is outside your priority subtopics and lacks direct connection to continuous multimodal models or video/image generation. Signals: scientific figure quality, full-manuscript context, benchmark, multimodal evaluation, not aligned with priority subtopics

Abstract: Scientific images are the core elements of presenting experimental conclusions, elaborating system architecture, and supporting comparative arguments in scientific papers. However, existing image quality assessment (IQA) methods are predominantly designed for natural photographs or AI-generated content, which cannot be directly applied to scientific papers. The few existing studies on scholarly charts remain confined to visual-surface comparisons, failing to verify caption alignment, citation relevance, or visual misleadingness. To address this, we propose SciFigQual-Bench, a full-text contextual benchmark that evaluates scientific images across five dimensions (clarity, layout, caption fit, context relevance, and misleading risk). The data covers top computer-science conferences from 2020 to 2025; 6,308 images were independently scored by multiple domain experts in five dimensions and aggregated into gold-standard annotations. Unlike previous scientific figure benchmarks, our dataset binds each image to its caption, citing sentence, and manuscript context. To enable automated evaluation on this benchmark, we designed a staged cross-modal evaluation framework SFQ-Agent to achieve auditable and refined scoring through the collection and fusion of modal evidence. Multiple mainstream large models were evaluated on the test subset eval1200, and SFQ-Agent (F3) equipped with GPT-5.6-Sol achieved the lowest overall average absolute error (0.418) and the highest consistency rate (93.4%), consistently outperforming both direct evaluation and auxiliary (Sidecar) visual language model evaluation schemes.

</details>

### [70] [MedARC: Training-Free Adaptive Redundancy Compression of Visual Tokens for 3D Medical Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26554)
*Yitao Zhu, Mengjun Liu, Yingji Fu, Haowen Pang, Anqi Qiu*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态高效化与轻量化

Importance: 值得关注 (77.51) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: MedARC 提出了一种无需训练的 3D 医学 VLM 视觉 token 自适应冗余压缩框架，通过融合自注意力、文本-视觉相似度和局部特征偏差三种线索估计 token 重要性，并采用显著性感知的合并策略保留信息性 token、合并冗余 token，在 CT-RATE 和 MR-RATE 上减少了 token 开销和推理时间，同时保持或提升了诊断性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D 医学图像生成极长的视觉 token 序列，存在严重的空间和层间冗余。现有 token 压缩方法通常采用统一缩减或依赖单一重要性信号，容易移除临床相关或结构独特的区域，影响诊断性能。

Method: MedARC 通过三种互补线索估计 token 重要性：(1) VLM 视觉编码器的自注意力，反映模型内在视觉焦点；(2) 投影视觉 token 与文本嵌入的相似度，识别查询相关区域；(3) 局部视觉基础模型特征与体素级特征中心的偏差，突出结构独特的解剖区域。基于重要性分布，采用显著性感知的合并策略，保留信息性 token 并合并冗余 token。

Result: 在 CT-RATE 和 MR-RATE 数据集上，MedARC 显著减少了视觉 token 数量和推理时间，同时保持或提升了诊断性能。多线索评分的额外开销被处理更少 token 带来的节省所抵消，且在大语言模型上收益更大。

Conclusion: MedARC 是一种有效的 3D 医学 VLM 视觉 token 压缩方法，通过多线索融合和显著性感知合并，在降低计算开销的同时保障了诊断性能，为医学多模态模型的高效推理提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 MedARC，一种无需训练的视觉 token 自适应冗余压缩方法，用于 3D 医学 VLM，核心贡献在于减少视觉 token 数量以提升推理效率。这属于多模态高效化与轻量化（efficient_multimodal）中的 token 压缩方向，与 canonical 条目高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on medical VLM token compression, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and has negative keyword overlap. Signals: Training-free token compression, 3D medical VLM, Multi-cue importance estimation, Saliency-aware merging, Redundancy reduction for volumetric images

Abstract: Integrating 3D medical images with vision-language models (VLMs) holds substantial promise for computer-aided diagnosis. However, volumetric images generate prohibitively long visual-token sequences with considerable spatial and inter-slice redundancy. Existing token compression methods typically apply uniform reduction or rely on a single importance signal, increasing the risk of removing regions that are clinically relevant to the query or structurally distinctive. To address this limitation, we propose MedARC, a unified, training-free framework for Adaptive Redundancy Compression of visual tokens in 3D medical VLMs. MedARC estimates token importance by integrating three complementary cues: self-attention from the VLM vision encoder, which reflects the model's intrinsic visual focus; similarity between projected visual tokens and text embeddings, which identifies query-relevant regions; and deviations of local visual foundation model features from the volume-level feature center, which highlight structurally distinctive anatomy. The resulting importance distribution guides a saliency-aware merging strategy that preserves informative tokens while consolidating redundant ones rather than simply discarding them. Experiments on CT-RATE and MR-RATE show that MedARC reduces visual-token overhead and inference time while preserving or improving diagnostic performance. Its multi-cue scoring cost is outweighed by the savings from processing fewer tokens, with greater benefits expected for larger language models.

</details>


### [71] [Decoupled Visual Processing: Efficient Multimodal Adaptation via Modality-Specific Transformer Substitution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26596)
*Mingkuan Feng, Zhengqi Wen, Jianhua Tao*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 多模态高效化与轻量化

Importance: 高优先级 (87.5) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 Decoupled Visual Processing (DVP)，一种高效的 MLLM 微调框架。在 LLM 解码器前半层共享处理后，将视觉 token 路由至一个轻量可训练的 transformer 块，而文本 token 继续通过冻结的原始层，仅需训练极少量参数即可在 LLaVA-1.5 上取得有竞争力的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MLLM 全参数微调计算开销巨大，且视觉与文本 token 在深层网络中的表征需求存在显著差异，因此无需对所有参数进行统一微调。作者希望探索一种参数高效的视觉指令微调方法。

Method: DVP 将预训练 LLM 的解码器分为两部分：前半层共享处理视觉和文本 token；后半层将视觉 token 路由至一个新初始化的单 transformer 块进行独立处理，而文本 token 继续通过原始冻结的解码器层。两路输出在语言模型头前拼接。训练时仅更新该单 transformer 块。

Result: 在 LLaVA-1.5 框架上，DVP 在 MME、POPE 和 ChartQA 基准上取得了与全参数微调相当的性能，同时仅训练了总参数的一小部分，显著降低了训练成本。

Conclusion: DVP 表明 MLLM 中的视觉表征可以通过解耦的参数高效路径有效学习，为多模态模型的高效微调提供了新思路。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 DVP 框架，通过解耦视觉处理路径、仅训练单个轻量 transformer 块来高效微调 MLLM，属于多模态模型参数高效微调与轻量化方向，与 [CANONICAL] subtopic "多模态高效化与轻量化" (efficient_multimodal) 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly addresses efficient MLLM adaptation with a novel decoupled visual processing method, aligning with user's priority on model efficiency and unified multimodal models. Signals: Efficient multimodal adaptation, Parameter-efficient fine-tuning, Decoupled visual processing, MLLM efficiency, LLaVA-1.5 benchmark

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities by integrating visual and textual understanding within a unified transformer architecture. However, fine-tuning all parameters of these models for visual instruction tuning is computationally expensive and often unnecessary, as the representation requirements for visual and textual tokens diverge significantly in the deeper layers of the network. In this paper, we propose Decoupled Visual Processing (DVP), an efficient training framework that replaces the upper decoder layers of a pretrained LLM with a lightweight, independently trainable single transformer block dedicated exclusively to visual token processing. Specifically, after shared processing through the first half of the decoder layers, visual and textual tokens are split: visual tokens are routed through a newly initialized single transformer block while textual tokens continue through the original frozen decoder layers. The two streams are then concatenated before the language modeling head. During training, only the single transformer block is updated, dramatically reducing the number of trainable parameters. Experiments on the LLaVA-1.5 framework demonstrate that DVP achieves competitive performance on MME, POPE, and ChartQA benchmarks while training only a fraction of the total parameters, suggesting that visual representations in MLLMs can be effectively learned through a decoupled, parameter-efficient pathway.

</details>

### [72] [SciFigAlign: Scoring Scientific Figures by Fine-tuned Alignment of Visuals with Manuscript Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27066)
*Chuanzhi Xu, Zihan Deng, Huiqi Liang, Chengkun Yue, Zhanlin Cui, Pengfei Ye, Weidong Cai*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 生成模型评测与数据集

Importance: 普通相关 (52.54) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出SciFigAlign，一个面向科学论文图表质量评估的多模态评分模型，并构建了包含3,857张图表、沿清晰度/相关性/信息量/结构四维度标注的数据集。模型通过微调CLIP和SciBERT，结合跨模态注意力与CubeMLP融合，联合优化回归损失与排序损失，在论文级划分测试集上MAE为0.3524，比最佳LLM基线降低59%误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统图像质量评估方法无法判断科学图表是否支撑论文的科学论点，CLIP方法缺乏对稿件上下文的理解，零样本LLM/VLM评分结果过于集中且视觉与文本证据融合有限。因此需要专门针对科学图表质量评估的方法和数据集。

Method: 构建了3,857张来自同行评审会议论文的科学图表数据集，沿清晰度(Clarity)、相关性(Relevance)、信息量(Informativeness)、结构(Structure)四维度标注。提出SciFigAlign模型，输入包括图表裁剪、标题、引用段落和论文上下文，微调CLIP视觉编码器和SciBERT文本编码器，通过逐模态交叉注意力(per-modality cross-attention)和CubeMLP融合，联合优化SmoothL1回归损失与论文内排序合页损失(ranking hinge loss)。

Result: 在论文级划分测试集(n=396)上，SciFigAlign的宏观MAE为0.3524，论文内成对准确率81.64%，相比最佳LLM基线(MAE 0.864)相对误差降低59%。消融实验证实稿件上下文输入、引用段落去噪和排序监督均至关重要。

Conclusion: 科学图表评估需要视觉内容与稿件证据之间的学习对齐，而非仅靠提示工程，即使使用最先进的VLM也是如此。SciFigAlign证明了针对科学图表质量评估进行专门微调的有效性。

Classification reason: 该论文提出了一个面向科学论文图表质量评估的新数据集（3,857张图表，四维度标注）以及一个多模态评分模型SciFigAlign，属于多模态理解与生成方向下的生成模型评测与数据集（generation_benchmark）子主题。该子主题在multimodal_understanding_generation方向下已有非规范条目，且论文核心贡献是构建评测基准与数据集，匹配度很高。

Importance reason: Focuses on scientific figure quality assessment, which is outside the user's priority subtopics and lacks alignment with key boost keywords. Signals: Scientific figure scoring, Multimodal alignment, Fine-tuned CLIP/SciBERT, Dataset construction, Peer review application

Abstract: Scientific figure assessment in peer review differs fundamentally from general image quality evaluation: a figure must be visually legible, faithfully support the manuscript's claims, and communicate evidence with a clear visual hierarchy. However, if we apply traditional image assessment methods to scientific figure quality assessment, limitations emerge: classic IQA models capture perceptual quality or aesthetics but cannot judge whether a figure serves the paper's scientific argument; CLIP-based methods assess generic image-text correspondence, yet lack understanding of manuscript context; and zero-shot LLM/VLM judges, when repurposed for figure scoring, often yield overly concentrated scores with limited fusion of visual and textual evidence. We introduce an annotated dataset of 3,857 scientific figures from peer-reviewed conference papers, each rated along four peer-review-oriented dimensions: Clarity, Relevance, Informativeness, and Structure. We propose SciFigAlign, a fine-tuned multimodal scorer that grounds figure quality assessment in manuscript evidence. Given a figure crop, caption, citing paragraphs, and light paper context, SciFigAlign fine-tunes CLIP and SciBERT end-to-end with per-modality cross-attention and CubeMLP fusion, jointly optimizing SmoothL1 regression with a within-paper ranking hinge loss. Under paper-level splits, SciFigAlign achieves a macro MAE of 0.3524 and a within-paper pairwise accuracy of 81.64% on the test set with n = 396, a 59% relative error reduction over the best LLM-as-judge baseline with MAE 0.864. Ablations confirm that manuscript-grounded inputs, citing-context denoising, and ranking supervision are all critical, showing that scientific figure assessment requires learned alignment between visual content and manuscript evidence rather than prompting alone, even with state-of-the-art VLMs.

</details>

### [73] [Do Unified Multimodal Models Think in One Space? A Lens Through Cross-Branch Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26411)
*Yu Wang, Sharon Li*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 统一多模态模型

Importance: 高优先级 (90.04) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出跨分支语义引导（cross-branch semantic steering）框架，通过从理解分支提取语义方向并应用于生成分支，发现理解→生成的引导可以有效控制图像生成并提升语义忠实性，而生成→理解的引导效果有限。这表明统一多模态模型的架构统一并不保证语义空间的对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一多模态模型（UMM）将理解与生成集成在同一架构中，但两种能力是否共享统一的、可迁移的语义空间尚不清楚。由于理解分支（文本 token）和生成分支（视觉 latent）的表征空间和训练目标存在本质差异，直接比较十分困难，因此需要一种干预式的方法来探测两者语义空间的关系。

Method: 提出跨分支语义引导（cross-branch semantic steering）框架：从理解分支或生成分支中提取语义方向向量（steering vectors），然后将其应用于另一分支的推理过程，观察其对生成/理解结果的影响。通过对比理解→生成和生成→理解两个方向的引导效果，分析两个分支语义空间的对齐程度。

Result: 理解分支提取的引导向量可以成功迁移到生成分支，实现可控图像生成并提升语义忠实性；而生成分支提取的向量在反向引导理解分支时效果有限。分析表明，理解分支的向量捕获了可迁移的、以物体为中心的语义，而生成分支的向量主要编码低层外观特征。

Conclusion: 架构统一并不保证语义对齐，理解与生成分支之间存在语义不对称性。跨分支语义引导可作为探测多模态模型表征空间的有效工具，为未来设计真正语义对齐的统一多模态模型提供了重要启示。

Classification reason: 论文研究统一多模态模型（UMM）中理解分支与生成分支的语义空间是否对齐，属于理解与生成一体化方向，与现有 subtopic "统一多模态模型"（unified_multimodal_model）高度匹配。

Importance reason: Directly probes semantic alignment in unified multimodal models, a core priority subtopic, with actionable insights for representation transfer. Signals: cross-branch steering, semantic space alignment, understanding-to-generation transfer, controllable image synthesis, representational mismatch

Abstract: Unified multimodal models (UMMs) aim to integrate understanding and generation within a single architecture, yet it remains unclear whether these capabilities share a unified and transferable semantic space. This question is fundamentally challenging, as the two branches operate over heterogeneous representations (text tokens vs.\ visual latents) and distinct training objectives, making direct comparison difficult. To address this, we introduce \emph{cross-branch semantic steering}, an intervention-based framework that extracts semantic directions from one branch and applies them to the other. We show that steering vectors learned from the understanding branch can transfer to generation, enabling controllable image synthesis and improved semantic faithfulness. In contrast, the reverse direction consistently shows limited effectiveness. Our analysis suggests that this asymmetry may be related to a practical representational mismatch: understanding-derived vectors capture transferable, object-centric semantics, while generation-derived vectors primarily encode low-level appearance features. Our results reveal that architectural unification does not guarantee semantic alignment, and establish cross-branch steering as a practical tool for probing multimodal representations.

</details>

## 视觉语言模型与 MLLM [[Back]](#toc)

通用视觉语言模型、MLLM 架构、多模态指令微调与对话。

### [74] [Speech2Grasp: Data-Efficient Transfer of Text-Conditioned Grasp Detection to Speech in Humanoid Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26567)
*Hung Nguyen, Kim Nhat Minh Nguyen, Van Duc Vu, Van-Danh Le, Hoang Huy Le, Dinh Tuan Nguyen, Pham Tuyen Le, Van-Truong Nguyen, Quan Nguyen*

Main category: cs.RO, cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 值得关注 (67.49) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 Speech2Grasp 框架，通过轻量 MLP 投影器将文本条件抓取检测模型 ALBEF 高效迁移到语音输入，在仿人机器人上实现优于级联 ASR 管线的抓取性能并降低推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仿人机器人需要多模态理解以实现自然的人机交互，但现有视觉语言模型通常假设文本输入而非更自然的语音输入。直接使用级联 ASR 管线存在误差累积和延迟问题，因此需要探索将成熟的文本条件模型高效迁移到语音输入的方法。

Method: 以 ALBEF 为案例进行诊断分析，发现轻量 MLP 投影器能有效将语音特征适配到文本特征空间，同时保持语义区分性和鲁棒性。基于此发现，提出 Speech2Grasp 框架，使用 MLP 投影器将语音编码器输出映射到 ALBEF 的文本嵌入空间，实现语音条件抓取检测。

Result: 在真实仿人机器人实验中，Speech2Grasp 在抓取检测任务上优于级联 ASR 管线，同时降低了推理延迟。诊断分析验证了 MLP 投影器在语义区分性和鲁棒性方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 研究表明，通过轻量投影器将文本条件模型高效迁移到语音输入是一种实用范式，为扩展已有文本条件系统到语音模态提供了可行路径。

Classification reason: 论文研究将文本条件抓取检测模型（ALBEF）迁移到语音输入，属于视觉语言模型（VLM）的跨模态扩展与适配，映射到 [CANONICAL] 的 vlm_mllm_general（视觉语言模型与 MLLM）。

Importance reason: Focuses on robot grasping and speech transfer, not aligned with user's priority on continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: speech-to-grasp, data-efficient transfer, humanoid robot, MLP projector, low relevance to priority subtopics

Abstract: Humanoid robots increasingly require multi-modal understanding for natural interaction with humans. Despite the prominence of vision-language models, they generally assume textual rather than the more natural speech inputs. In this paper, we investigate whether a well-established text-conditioned model can be transferred to speech in a data-efficient manner. Using ALBEF as a case study, we conduct diagnostic analyses showing that a lightweight MLP-based projector effectively adapts it to speech, while preserving semantic discrimination and robustness. Motivated by these findings, we introduce Speech2Grasp, a framework for data-efficient transfer of text-conditioned grasp detection to speech. Real-world humanoid robot experiments show that Speech2Grasp outperforms cascaded ASR-based pipeline, while reducing inference latency. Our findings suggest a practical paradigm for extending established text-conditioned systems to speech.

</details>


### [75] [FAS-R1: A Unified Multi-Task MLLM for Reasoning Face Anti-Spoofing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26432)
*Hongyang Wang, Yichen Shi, Hongrui Li, Yiru Huo, Jun Feng, Zitong Yu*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 值得关注 (72.31) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: FAS-R1 是一个面向人脸反欺骗的统一多任务 MLLM 框架，通过两阶段训练（长思维链 SFT + 难度感知 GRPO）实现真实性分类、攻击类型识别和欺骗区域定位，在多个基准上取得优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 FAS 模型主要提供二分类（真/假）结果，缺乏攻击语义解释和可解释性；而基于 MLLM 的方法虽然能输出结构化结果，但依赖监督微调，生成的推理过程模板化，对困难攻击（如化妆、口罩等细微欺骗）优化不足。

Method: 提出 FAS-R1 两阶段框架：1) 构建 FAS-R1-23K 高质量长思维链数据集进行冷启动 SFT；2) 采用 FAS 特定的 GRPO 后训练，包含退化模拟增强（DSA）和难度感知 GRPO（DA-GRPO）以缓解简单样本主导问题，提升对细微/模糊攻击的推理能力。

Result: 3B 模型在域内达到 98.75% 真实性准确率、93.33% 攻击类型准确率、96.30/94.73% AP@40/AP@50；在跨域泛化和答案-推理质量上均优于对比系统，且展示了良好的规模扩展行为。

Conclusion: FAS-R1 通过推理导向的两阶段训练策略，有效提升了 MLLM 在人脸反欺骗任务上的性能、可解释性和泛化能力，验证了推理增强方法在安全敏感视觉任务中的潜力。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 FAS-R1，一个面向人脸反欺骗（FAS）的多任务 MLLM 框架，属于视觉语言模型在特定视觉任务上的应用。虽然任务特定，但核心方法是基于 MLLM 的架构设计、两阶段训练（SFT + GRPO）和推理能力增强，与 [CANONICAL] 的 vlm_mllm_general（视觉语言模型与 MLLM）主题高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on face anti-spoofing, a niche application not aligned with user's priority subtopics like continuous language models or video generation. Signals: MLLM for FAS, long-CoT reasoning, difficulty-aware GRPO, degradation augmentation, domain generalization

Abstract: Face anti-spoofing (FAS) is increasingly expected to provide not only bona fide/spoof decisions, but also attack semantics and image-grounded evidence for human inspection. Existing discriminative FAS models remain largely label-centric, while recent MLLM-based methods offer structured outputs but still rely mainly on supervised fine-tuning, often producing template-like rationales and weak optimization for difficult attacks. We propose FAS-R1, a two-stage reasoning-oriented MLLM framework for unified FAS prediction, covering authenticity classification, attack-type recognition and spoof-region localization. FAS-R1 first uses FAS-R1-23K, a high-quality long-CoT dataset, for cold-start supervised fine-tuning, and then performs FAS-specific GRPO post-training. Degradation-Simulated Augmentation (DSA) encourages stable spoof-cue reasoning across visual-quality shifts, while Difficulty-Aware GRPO (DA-GRPO) mitigates easy-sample dominance that may leave difficult task--attack groups under-optimized, especially for subtle or ambiguous attacks such as makeup and mask attacks. The main 3B FAS-R1 model achieves 98.75\% authenticity accuracy, 93.33\% attack-type accuracy, and 96.30/94.73\% AP@40/AP@50 in-domain. It also outperforms the compared systems in cross-domain authenticity generalization and answer-and-rationale quality. Experiments with different base models further show favorable scaling behavior. The code will be released soon.

</details>


### [76] [CaM-Wolf: Causal-Aware Multimodal Agents for Social Deduction Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26393)
*Zheng Zhang, Nanjie Yao, Jiarui He, Deheng Ye, Peilin Zhao, Hao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 值得关注 (75.94) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: CaM-Wolf 是首个集成多模态感知与生成的社会推理游戏（狼人杀）智能体，通过处理玩家视频输入、因果推理器（强化学习训练）建立行为与隐藏角色间的逻辑链，并以动画头像呈现，在游戏表现和人机交互质量上均取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有社会推理游戏 AI 智能体主要基于纯文本，忽略了人类社交互动中多模态信息（如面部表情、肢体语言）的基础性作用。作者旨在弥合这一差距，构建能感知多模态信号并参与复杂社交动态的类人 AI 智能体。

Method: CaM-Wolf 包含三个核心组件：(1) 多模态感知模块，处理其他玩家的视频输入；(2) 因果感知推理器（Causal-aware Reasoner），通过强化学习训练，建立可观察行为与隐藏角色之间的逻辑链条；(3) 动画头像呈现模块，用于智能体的自我表达。整体架构将多模态输入与 LLM 推理能力相结合。

Result: 实验和用户研究表明，CaM-Wolf 在智能体游戏表现上优于现有方法，并显著提升了人机交互的质量。

Conclusion: CaM-Wolf 证明了多模态感知与生成对于社会推理游戏中 AI 智能体的重要性，是向创建能参与复杂社交动态的类人 AI 智能体的重要进展。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 CaM-Wolf，一个集成多模态感知与生成的社会推理游戏智能体，处理视频输入、使用因果推理器（Reasoner）进行推理并通过动画头像呈现。这属于多模态理解与生成方向下的通用视觉语言模型/MLLM 应用，因此映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "vlm_mllm_general"。

Importance reason: Focuses on social deduction game agents, not aligned with user's priority on continuous multimodal models or video generation. Signals: multimodal perception, causal reasoning, social deduction game, reinforcement learning, human-AI interaction

Abstract: Social deduction games (SDGs) such as Werewolf have become challenging testbeds for AI agents. These games require complex social skills such as reasoning, deception, and collaboration. While recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have driven significant progress in SDG agents, current approaches are predominantly text-based, overlooking the multimodal nature that is fundamental to human social interaction. To bridge this gap, we introduce CaM-Wolf, the first SDG agent that integrates multimodal perception and generation. CaM-Wolf processes video inputs from other players, employs a causal-aware Reasoner trained via reinforcement learning to establish logical chains between observable behaviors and hidden roles, and presents itself through an animated avatar. Our experiments and user study show that CaM-Wolf achieves superior agent gameplay performance and enhances the quality of human-AI interaction. This work represents a significant advancement towards creating more human-like AI agents capable of participating in nuanced social dynamics. Our code is available at .

</details>


### [77] [Understanding Knowledge Transfer Mechanism in Heterogeneous MLLM Fusion: A Simple Linear Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26608)
*Yinghao Hou, Jiahe Fan, Yuanhao Pu, Zongyuan Chen, Hong Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉语言模型与 MLLM

Importance: 值得关注 (70.74) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出 Cross-Scale Directional Parameter Injection (CDPI) 方法，通过简单的线性探针分析异构 MLLM 融合中的跨尺度知识迁移机制。实验发现知识迁移具有选择性：推理能力（尤其是高层推理）显著提升，而感知能力几乎不变；迁移主要来自语言模型部分，且在小比例注入区间效果最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无训练异构 MLLM 融合方法虽然能提升聚合性能，但小模型究竟从大模型继承了哪些能力尚不清楚。已有研究局限于有限任务集或聚合指标，缺乏对跨尺度能力迁移机制的深入理解。

Method: 提出 CDPI（Cross-Scale Directional Parameter Injection），一种简单的线性探针方法，通过将大模型参数沿特定方向注入小模型来研究知识迁移。结合局部理论分析（一阶选择性由能力相关的共享注入方向响应决定，二阶曲率效应约束有效迁移区间），在四个 Qwen3-VL 模型对和十二个多模态基准上进行实验。

Result: 实验揭示了一致的迁移选择性模式：增益集中在推理能力（尤其是高层推理），感知性能接近原始目标模型。消融实验表明高层推理增益主要来自语言模型部分；比例分析发现正向选择性迁移主要发生在小比例注入区间。

Conclusion: 跨尺度异构 MLLM 融合本质上是窄区间、低干扰条件下的选择性语言侧推理迁移，而非广泛的能力继承。这一发现重新定义了异构 MLLM 融合的能力迁移本质。

Classification reason: 该论文研究异构多模态大模型（MLLM）的无训练融合与跨尺度知识迁移机制，属于通用 MLLM 架构与能力分析范畴，与 [CANONICAL] 子主题 "视觉语言模型与 MLLM" 高度匹配。

Importance reason: Focuses on MLLM fusion mechanism, not on the user's priority subtopics like continuous models or video generation. Signals: MLLM fusion, knowledge transfer, linear probe, reasoning vs perception, not autoregressive or continuous token

Abstract: Training-free fusion of heterogeneous multimodal large language models (MLLMs) provides a direct route for cross-scale capability transfer, yet improvements in aggregate performance do not reveal what a smaller model actually inherits. Existing studies are largely designed and evaluated on limited task sets or aggregate metrics; as evaluation expands to broader task collections, whether different capabilities can transfer across scales remains poorly understood. To investigate this question, we introduce Cross-Scale Directional Parameter Injection (CDPI), a simple linear probe to analyze cross-scale knowledge transfer during heterogeneous fusion. A local theoretical analysis indicates that knowledge transfer selectivity is determined at first order by capability-dependent responses to a shared injection direction, while second-order curvature effects constrain the effective transfer regime. Across four Qwen3-VL model pairs and twelve multimodal benchmarks, our experiments reveal a consistent pattern of selectivity: gains concentrate on reasoning, particularly high-level reasoning, whereas perception performance remains close to that of the original target model. Component-wise ablations further show that high-level reasoning gains arise primarily from the language model, while ratio analysis finds that positive selective transfer occurs mainly in the small-ratio regime. These findings recast cross-scale heterogeneous MLLM fusion as selective language-side reasoning transfer within a narrow, low-interference regime, rather than broad capability inheritance.

</details>

### [78] [TraceCLIP: Recovering Local Semantics from Patch-to-CLS Contributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26107)
*Xinran Liu, Shouqian Shi, Yutong Chen, Ge Wang, Xin-Wei Yao, Sheng Zhong*

Main category: cs.CV, cs.AI

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉问答与文档理解

Importance: 值得关注 (72.56) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: TraceCLIP是一种无需训练的框架，通过分离CLS注意力输出中的patch特定贡献项，恢复CLIP中潜在的局部语义证据，并利用语义-测地线拓扑门校准最终层patch亲和度以重建密集特征。在8个零样本语义分割基准上，相比最强的无训练方法，平均mIoU提升1.3-4.5个点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CLIP的图像级对比学习目标将文本与CLS全局表征对齐，但局部视觉-语言对应关系仅受到间接约束。现有方法要么引入额外监督、外部模型或任务特定适配，要么仅从现有patch特征中恢复密集响应而未探究局部语义在CLIP内部何处最易获取。作者希望在不增加训练、外部模型或区域级监督的情况下，从CLIP内部结构中恢复空间定位的语义信息。

Method: TraceCLIP包含两个核心组件：(1) 通过隔离CLS注意力输出中写入的patch特定贡献项，恢复潜在的patch级语义证据（贡献特征）；(2) 将贡献导出的语义响应转换为语义-测地线拓扑门（semantic-geodesic topology gate），用于校准最终层patch亲和度，从而重建密集特征。该方法无需训练，仅利用CLIP内部计算结构。

Result: 在8个零样本语义分割基准上，TraceCLIP在两个骨干网络和不同背景设置下，相比最强的无训练方法，平均mIoU提升1.3到4.5个点。诊断实验表明贡献特征具有强局部语义判别能力和文本条件空间对齐能力。

Conclusion: 空间定位的语义信息可能仍然可在全局对齐表征的内部构造中获取。TraceCLIP证明了无需额外训练或外部模型，仅通过分析CLIP内部注意力机制中的贡献项即可有效恢复局部语义信息，为理解CLIP内部表征结构提供了新视角。

Classification reason: 该论文关注CLIP的密集视觉语言理解（包括目标定位、区域识别、开放词汇语义分割），属于多模态理解与生成方向。虽然论文不直接做VQA，但其核心是恢复CLIP的局部语义对应关系以提升密集视觉语言理解能力，这与多模态理解中视觉-语言对应关系的研究高度相关。最接近的现有子主题是"多模态评测与基准"（不匹配，因为论文不是做评测基准）或"视觉语言模型与MLLM"（也不完全匹配，因为论文不是提出新MLLM架构）。考虑到论文核心是提升CLIP的局部语义理解能力，且现有子主题中"多模态VQA与文档理解"涵盖密集视觉语言理解任务（如开放词汇语义分割），选择该子主题作为最接近的映射。

Importance reason: Training-free CLIP dense understanding is useful but not aligned with your priority on continuous/multimodal models or generation. Signals: training-free, zero-shot semantic segmentation, CLIP patch analysis, local semantics recovery, no external models

Abstract: Dense vision-language understanding, including object localization, region recognition, and open-vocabulary semantic segmentation, requires associating language concepts with spatially grounded visual regions. CLIP provides a strong foundation for these tasks by learning a shared image-text embedding space from large-scale contrastive pre-training. However, its image-level objective aligns text with a CLS-derived global representation, leaving local vision-language correspondence only indirectly constrained. Existing methods either introduce additional supervision, external models, or task-specific adaptation, while training-free approaches mainly recover dense responses from existing patch features without examining where local semantics become most accessible within CLIP. We introduce TraceCLIP, a training-free framework that recovers latent patch-level semantic evidence by isolating the patch-specific terms written into the CLS attention output. TraceCLIP further converts contribution-derived semantic responses into a semantic-geodesic topology gate that calibrates final-layer patch affinity for dense feature reconstruction. Diagnostic experiments show that these contribution features exhibit strong local semantic discrimination and text-conditioned spatial alignment. On eight zero-shot semantic segmentation benchmarks, TraceCLIP achieves gains of 1.3 to 4.5 points in average mIoU over the strongest prior training-free methods across both backbones and background settings, without additional training, external vision foundation models, or region-level supervision. More broadly, these findings suggest that spatially localized semantics may remain accessible within the internal construction of globally aligned representations.

</details>


### [79] [Rad-JEPA 3D: Radiology Joint-Embedding Predictive Model for 3D Computed Tomography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26196)
*Quoc-Huy Trinh, Minh-Van Nguyen, Ulas Bagci*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉问答与文档理解

Importance: 值得关注 (74.01) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: Rad-JEPA 3D 是一种面向 3D CT 影像的自监督联合嵌入预测框架，通过混合 H-Mamba 编码器和隐状态正交正则化（HSOR）学习保留空间结构的体素表征，在约 12 万 CT 扫描上预训练后，以 4.0B 参数在医学 VQA 和空间推理任务上取得 SOTA 结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 3D 医学影像自监督预训练方法难以保留粗粒度的空间和几何结构，限制了在下游器官分离、异常检测以及与语言模型结合时的空间理解能力。作者希望设计一种能保持体素空间结构的自监督预训练框架。

Method: 提出 Rad-JEPA 3D 框架，核心包括：(1) 混合 H-Mamba 编码器，融合 Mamba 状态空间分支（建模层间连续性）和分组查询注意力分支（捕获跨平面空间上下文），通过轻量级 per-token router 组合；(2) 隐状态正交正则化（HSOR），在编码器各层对齐学生-教师隐状态并减少特征冗余，产生更一致且具判别力的体素表征。预训练采用 masked-view 预测完整扫描 latent 的 JEPA 范式。

Result: 在约 12 万 CT 扫描上预训练后，Rad-JEPA 3D 以 4.0B 总参数在闭式 VQA 上取得与 SOTA 竞争的结果，并在 Spatial-Med 基准上获得最佳平均空间推理分数。消融实验验证了混合块和 HSOR 的互补增益，且表明诱导的空间结构可替代原始语言模型规模在体素推理任务上的作用。

Conclusion: Rad-JEPA 3D 证明通过精心设计的自监督预训练（保留空间结构的 JEPA 范式 + 混合编码器 + 层间正则化），紧凑模型也能在医学影像 VQA 和空间推理上超越更大规模的模型，为医学多模态理解提供了高效的新范式。

Classification reason: 该论文提出 Rad-JEPA 3D，一种用于 3D CT 影像的自监督预训练框架，并在 Spatial-Med 基准上评测了 VQA 和空间推理能力。论文核心关注医学影像的视觉表征学习及其与语言模型结合后的 VQA/空间推理性能，因此归类于多模态理解与生成方向下的「视觉问答与文档理解」（multimodal_vqa_doc）子主题。该子主题涵盖 VQA 任务，且论文明确在 VQA 基准上评测，属于合理映射。

Importance reason: Focuses on medical CT imaging with Mamba and VQA, which is outside your priority subtopics and has negative keyword overlap. Signals: Medical imaging, 3D CT, Mamba encoder, Self-supervised learning, Not in priority subtopics

Abstract: Self-supervised pretraining is central to 3D medical image analysis, where unlabeled CT volumes are abundant but expert annotations are scarce. Yet existing volumetric encoders often fail to preserve the coarse spatial and geometric structure that downstream reasoning depends on, limiting their performance on organ disentanglement, abnormality detection, and spatial understanding when paired with language models. We introduce Rad-JEPA 3D, a joint-embedding predictive framework that learns volumetric CT representations by predicting the latent features of a complete scan from a masked view. At its core is a hybrid H-Mamba encoder that fuses a Mamba state-space branch, which models inter-slice continuity through sequential scanning, with a grouped-query attention branch, which captures cross-plane spatial context, combined through a lightweight per-token router. To improve the quality of intermediate representations, we further propose Hidden States Orthogonal Regularization (HSOR), which aligns student-teacher hidden states and reduces feature redundancy throughout the encoder. This layer-wise regularization produces more consistent and discriminative volumetric representations, leading to improved performance on organ recognition and spatial reasoning tasks. Pretrained on approximately 120,000 CT scans, Rad-JEPA 3D attains state-of-the-art results despite its compact size: with only 4.0B total parameters, it achieves competitive results with state-of-the-art on closed-ended VQA and the best average spatial-reasoning score on the Spatial-Med benchmark. Ablation studies confirm that the hybrid block and HSOR contribute complementary gains, and that the induced spatial structure can substitute for raw language-model scale on volumetric reasoning tasks.

</details>


### [80] [Towards Grounded GI Endoscopy VQA via Multi-Task Learning on Small VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27122)
*Itbaan Safwan, Ramail Khan, Muhammad Annas Shaikh, Muhammad Atif Tahir*

Main category: cs.CV

Direction: 多模态理解与生成

Subtopic: 视觉问答与文档理解

Importance: 普通相关 (40.96) - 未入选精读

TL;DR: 本文提出一种多任务微调方法，在小型VLM上通过辅助定位和描述任务提升胃肠道内窥镜VQA的准确率和视觉对齐能力，在Kvasir-VQA-x1数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胃肠道内窥镜图像分析已从单标签分类转向VQA，但现有VLM虽然回答准确率不错，其内部表征缺乏对视觉证据的显式对齐，临床部署需要模型不仅能回答正确，还能反映答案背后的视觉依据。

Method: 从现有VQA数据集中以最小额外标注构建辅助定位（grounding）和描述（description）任务：复用专家标注的息肉掩膜，对缺乏真实掩膜的类别使用GI域预训练分类器+Grad-CAM提供弱监督。在三个小型VLM骨干上使用LoRA进行微调，对比纯VQA微调与多任务微调的效果。

Result: 在Kvasir-VQA-x1数据集上，多任务微调相比纯VQA微调在分布内和分布外数据上均取得一致的准确率提升，同时答案token与相关图像区域之间的隐式对齐也有所改善。

Conclusion: 多任务微调通过引入辅助定位和描述任务，能够在不增加额外标注成本的情况下，同时提升小型VLM在医学VQA任务上的准确性和可解释性，有助于推动内窥镜VQA的临床落地。

Classification reason: 论文研究胃肠道内窥镜图像的视觉问答（VQA），属于多模态理解与生成方向下的视觉问答任务，映射到 [CANONICAL] subtopic "视觉问答与文档理解"（multimodal_vqa_doc）。

Importance reason: Niche medical VQA application with no overlap with user's priority subtopics or boost keywords. Signals: Medical domain, small VLM, multi-task fine-tuning, weak supervision, no continuous language model

Abstract: Gastrointestinal (GI) endoscopic image analysis has shifted from single-label classification toward visual question answering (VQA), where a model must answer free-form clinical questions about an image. While recent vision-language models (VLMs) achieve promising answer accuracy on this task, clinical adoption also requires the model's internal representations to reflect the visual evidence behind its answers. We propose a simple multi-task fine-tuning recipe that constructs auxiliary grounding and description tasks from an existing VQA dataset with minimal additional annotation: expert-annotated polyp masks are reused directly, while a GI-domain pretrained classifier with Grad-CAM localization provides weak supervision for finding categories that lack ground-truth masks. Three small VLM backbones are fine-tuned with low-rank adaptation under matched VQA-only and multi-task recipes on Kvasir-VQA-x1, and we show consistent accuracy gains together with improved implicit alignment between answer tokens and the relevant image region, evaluated on both in-distribution and out-of-distribution data.

</details>
